# Roblox launch checklist (merged from the KB)

_Generated 2026-11-01 08:01 by `claude-library/tools/roblox-kb/build_tools.py` from the topic files in this KB. Do not hand-edit; rebuild instead._

Pre-launch to launch-week checklist for a Roblox experience, merged and deduplicated across all topic files, grouped by phase. The `roblox-launch-checklist` skill tailors this to a specific game.

Items are merged across topic files; `x2`/`x3` means that many topic files independently list a near-identical check (higher = more broadly agreed). Each phase shows **Must** items first (most widely agreed), then **Also consider**. Source topic in brackets; open it with `kbq.py section <topic> checklist`.


Unique items: 3815 (from 3823 raw)


## How reliable is this sheet (trust audit 2026-11-01)

Items checked: 3815. Labels: OFFICIAL 1, CORROBORATED 8, SINGLE-SOURCE 3803, CONTRADICTED 0, OUTDATED-UNSURE 3.
Label rules (computed from the Flag column, cite count and date; code in `claude-library/tools/roblox-kb/trust_audit.py`): official = OFFICIAL; mixed or indie+devforum = CORROBORATED; devforum or one indie creator = SINGLE-SOURCE; indie with 2+ non-WEAK creators = CORROBORATED; checklist `xN` with N>=2 = CORROBORATED (topic files can share a source, so weaker than independent creators); a fast-moving fact (ads, DevEx, Premium, fees, eligibility, discovery) with newest date before 2025-11 = OUTDATED-UNSURE (tagged inline). CONTRADICTED = checked against current Creator Docs and found wrong; such rows carry an inline `[AUDIT ...]` note with the corrected value. Rows without an inline tag use the rule above.
Limit: labels describe source structure, not a re-verification of every number. Only the rows in the corrections file were checked against live Creator Docs.
Corrections applied here (4 rows, 4 rules): ads-audience-thresholds x1 -> OFFICIAL; ads-17plus x1 -> OUTDATED-UNSURE; ad-credit-rate x1 -> OUTDATED-UNSURE; hep-500-superseded x1 -> OUTDATED-UNSURE.
Five shakiest items still in the sheet (fast-moving, numeric, not officially re-checked, oldest first):
  1. [ ] Before enabling O18 targeting in paid UA, confirm the experience's age rating and Roblox audience rules allow it. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
  2. [ ] Check Audience Reach for Highly Engaged Players vs 250 (100 by Nov) [discovery-algorithm]
  3. [ ] Compare impressions before and after any monetisation change, especially for US under-13 audiences. [discovery-algorithm]
  4. [ ] Confirm your audience's age-check status when planning age-targeted features (64% of global DAUs have completed it; 80% Australia, 75% US and UK). [discovery-algorit...
  5. [ ] Track rewarded video opt-in (15%+) and frequency (3+). [analytics-metrics]

## 1. Idea and validation

### Must
- [ ] Write a hypothesis and choose a matching goal metric (e.g. DAY_1_RETENTION) [ab-testing-and-experiments]
- [ ] Before launching a test, confirm it changes exactly one variable; split multi-variable ideas into separate tests. [ab-testing-and-experiments]
- [ ] Set ratings and retention as guard metrics for any risky test and limit it to a small audience slice. [ab-testing-and-experiments]
- [ ] Log each experiment (hypothesis, metric, result, confidence, date) in a single test vault. [ab-testing-and-experiments]
- [ ] Leave audience on All Players, all regions and devices at first [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Re-advertise after big updates using Plays plus Reactivate (needs 20,000 lapsed players for Lapsed audience) [ads-and-sponsored-experiences] **[AUDIT 2026-11-01 OFFICIAL: confirmed: Recent Players needs 10,000+, Lapsed Players 20,000+ (Creator Docs ads-manager, fetched 2026-11-01)]**
- [ ] Use creator sponsorships matched to genre; check estimated views [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Confirm the experience's age rating allows ads (13+ audience floor; 17+ restriction reported in 2023, so verify). [ads-and-sponsored-experiences] **[AUDIT 2026-11-01 OUTDATED-UNSURE: superseded: since 2025-01-31 Roblox staff allow Sponsored Experiences and Search Ads for restricted (17+) and paid-access experiences, but 17+ experiences cannot serve ads to users aged 13-17. Ads Manager access needs age 13+ and a verified email (no ID check for advertisers); ad PUBLISHERS (monetising with ads) need 18+ and ID verification (DevForum 'Advertising paid access & restricted (17+) experiences' 2025-01-31; Creator Docs ads-manager fetched 2026-11-01)]**

### Also consider
- [ ] Use Recent Players audience for win-back campaigns; use All Players for new acquisition. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Start with US and Canada, then narrow age and gender to the audience that spends Robux. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Before enabling O18 targeting in paid UA, confirm the experience's age rating and Roblox audience rules allow it. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] For multi-game publishers, run separate audience and avatar tests per product before pooling budget. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Validate any pattern from old campaign data in a controlled test before budgeting on it. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Check the default 16+ Build age rating before publishing to younger audiences. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Identify the weakest metric versus same-genre benchmarks and fix it first. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Validate event data server-side against exploiters. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Write a hypothesis before each change and before each experiment. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Pin graphics level to 10 on all test devices for comparable performance runs. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Before launching a clone, test organic CCU on a small audience and compare against the original's CCU. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Check the original's CCU trajectory, not only its peak, before choosing a competitor to copy. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Validate type, nil, numeric range (math.isfinite), string length and Instance class/location on every incoming argument (T7462, T7463, T5935). [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Validate movement via server position deltas on the XZ plane with leeway, rounding and teleport exemptions (T5029, T5129, T9894, T5031). [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Enforce checkpoints in order for obbies and validate stage times. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Keep the existing exploit defenses in place; platform moderation does not replace server validation of remotes and economy. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Decide whether the brand or IP genuinely fits your game and audience [brand-deals-and-ip-licensing]
- [ ] Prepare a pitch: vision, creative elements, gameplay fit, timeline [brand-deals-and-ip-licensing]
- [ ] If planning a series, consider a short animated film as a proof-of-audience step before a crowdfunding campaign. [brand-deals-and-ip-licensing]
- [ ] Check that a brand character fits the game's tone and community before pitching it, as with any brand deal. [brand-deals-and-ip-licensing]
- [ ] Before opening Studio, write down genre, player count, art style and map shape; sketch the layout on paper with 1-3 basic shapes. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Add small ColorCorrection (Contrast 0.05, Saturation 0.1) and set EnvironmentDiffuse/SpecularScale to 1. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Do a week of research and a mood board [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Validate every client argument on the server for type, existence, ownership, range and cost before mutating state (T7460, T9721). [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Validate weapon hits on the server: origin near character, hit near part, no walls between, with latency tolerance (T7469). [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Explicitly require server validation when AI tools generate remote code, since they may not add it unprompted. [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Before shipping offline-capable features, audit every economy value stored on the client and move validation to the server (T17635). [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Confirm the generated RemoteEvent exists and validate its input on the server (T17891). [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Write helper logic as pure functions in shared ModuleScripts; keep validators in ReplicatedStorage. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Give each attack a cost (slowness, vulnerability, resource, cooldown) and a unique niche. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Validate every hit on the server: range with lag margin, os.clock cooldown, facing unless AoE. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Check that a boss does not require repeated stamina-draining actions and matches the game's genre (T17833). [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Validate demand and ship early, then iterate [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Check avatar cages against validation rules: distinct cages, clothing inside outer cage only. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Size a funded team's scope to market and demand evidence, not to a past studio's scope. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Before prototyping a genre, play top-grossing games critically and note the solved design problems. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] For kids' games, plan engagement against the actual age audience, not just viewer count. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Rebuild community ideas in your own style [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Check DevForum vote counts (for example, 33 votes for a thumbnail-fix request) as a demand signal before escalating a feature request. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Write a one-line pitch for the core mechanic and confirm it is fun to watch before adding systems. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Validate touch with Alt+drag (pinch/rotate) and Alt+Shift+drag (two-finger pan). [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] Estimate VR audience size before committing VR-specific build time. [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] Keep economy logic and validation on the server; never call DataStoreService from clients. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Validate data on save and on load, and keep a wipe guard that holds saves when data collapses. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Avoid abandoning live games that still have an audience [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Start with paper prototypes for early exploration, then prototype in Studio once ideas are settled. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Check team size and weekly hours against the genre before committing to it. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Before a build, remove the baseplate and build only the core action to test whether the idea is worth pursuing. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Log every new idea the same day in a shared list or board. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Write a one-page design doc: name, reference games, genre, core loop, progression, onboarding, monetisation. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] For a placement or build-by-player feature, get the client snapping working first, then add server validation before release. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Once the core idea works, make the title and icon before finishing the game so it is ready to show. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Set genre in game settings [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] Check Audience Reach for Highly Engaged Players vs 250 (100 by Nov) [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] Compare icon with front-page competitors [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] Compare impressions before and after any monetisation change, especially for US under-13 audiences. [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] Confirm your audience's age-check status when planning age-targeted features (64% of global DAUs have completed it; 80% Australia, 75% US and UK). [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] Keep titles, thumbnails and updates within one clear niche. [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] Place an in-game sign-up stand to build the update-day audience (T18716). [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] Check your audience's age profile and set early progression goals accordingly, since younger players convert less. [first-session-onboarding]
- [ ] Write a one-sentence pitch and test it on a stranger or kid [game-ideas-and-genres]
- [ ] Decide which ~20-40% to change, with research [game-ideas-and-genres]
- [ ] Check Roblox charts, search and genre competition [game-ideas-and-genres]
- [ ] Check demand outside Roblox (TikTok, other platforms) [game-ideas-and-genres]
- [ ] Identify the primary target audience and Bartle type [game-ideas-and-genres]
- [ ] Avoid saturated tutorial-clone niches without a twist [game-ideas-and-genres]
- [ ] Set the Creator Hub genre and subgenre by core loop [game-ideas-and-genres]
- [ ] Ask why past games in the genre failed [game-ideas-and-genres]
- [ ] Check whether your idea sits in RPG, strategy or shooter; if so, treat it as a demand-gap candidate but budget for higher polish. [game-ideas-and-genres]
- [ ] Write the one-room version of the idea before adding any second room, chapter or system. [game-ideas-and-genres]
- [ ] Check search results for your genre and candidate title before committing to a name; drop generic single words. [game-ideas-and-genres]
- [ ] Prototype an unproven or weird idea inside a 48-hour time box to test fun before scoping a full build. [game-ideas-and-genres]
- [ ] Confirm genre categorisation (RNG/idle = Simulator) [genre-mechanics]
- [ ] Match concept to young mobile majority audience [genre-mechanics]
- [ ] Apply genre-appropriate safety/access settings [genre-mechanics]
- [ ] If the genre needs endless variety, build a small library of hand-made pieces and assemble levels procedurally from them. [genre-mechanics]
- [ ] Prepare a pitch pack: screenshots, trailer, direct playable link [influencer-and-creator-marketing]
- [ ] Define audience by gender, age, country and platform, then interests, before selecting verticals and creators. [influencer-and-creator-marketing]
- [ ] Add a setTeleportationValidator check for team or eligibility rules before teleporting. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Load 60 fps mocap animations on demand; each can be 200-300 KB. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Define target audience and motivations before building. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Offer both rare, status-signalling items and frequent outfit-style items so achievers, self-expressers, speculators and competitors each have a reason to spend. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Pre-release test with your content audience before public launch [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Run a baseline on Ideal Fiber, then repeat on Home Wi-Fi or Standard Mobile (4G/LTE) and Bad Connection (3G). [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Ask testers for UX and feature ideas, not only bugs. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Pitch the core concept or a prototype to target players before major production. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Include at least one low-end device in each playtest round to match the target audience's hardware. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Test any 24-hour daily crate with a 30-second timer first to validate the claim loop. [retention-d1-d7]
- [ ] Before diagnosing a drop, check seasonality, competitors and algorithm changes [roblox-platform-changes]
- [ ] Map your target audience to age bands (Kids 5 to 8 mild; Select 9 to 15 up to moderate) and check the official definitions (N8015, unverified). [roblox-platform-changes]
- [ ] Validate all remote inputs on server [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Keep server memory under 50% of total and ideally under 3 GB. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Validate remote arguments with T or T.interface, then recheck outcomes server-side. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Name ServerStorage loadout folders exactly as their Team and validate names before granting items. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Validate all item gifting and trading on the server and run dupe tests before launch. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Pitch smaller creators with playable build and links [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Check Audience Expansion qualifiers: returning users inactive 60+ days, must play 10+ minutes. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Check the target audience age before showing Discord or social buttons. Roblox limits them to 13+. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Pitch a playable build to mid-size and large Roblox YouTubers before launch. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Before launch, join the top competitor servers and record the avatars and profiles of players to define your target audience. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Build audience and community before pitching [studio-business-funding-and-publishing]
- [ ] Prepare a Game Fund application: team, description, pitch deck, milestone schedule and budget [studio-business-funding-and-publishing]
- [ ] Run Calisthenics and Clothing validation tools, then verify avatars manually in Studio. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Validate avatar items via Save to Roblox > Avatar Item > Head, then cancel the publish to avoid fees. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Replace the default auto-generated icon with a custom icon that shows theme and genre. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Put the icon beside 5–10 front-page or genre competitors and confirm it is the most distinct. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Add one progression number (for example '+1 per click') only if it fits the genre. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] For horror games, use mood and mystery instead of oversaturated brights. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Write a conceptual art summary of ideas, context, style and art direction, and reuse it in design and marketing. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Attach a successful same-genre game's thumbnail as the style reference before generating AI variants. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Write down the target audience's age and favourite games before choosing art direction. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Reset saturation to 100 after recolouring with Hue/Saturation. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Title states genre/fantasy literally in few words [titles-descriptions]
- [ ] First sentence of description names genre and content [titles-descriptions]
- [ ] First ~160 chars: title, genre, unique feature, keywords, objective [titles-descriptions]
- [ ] Genre, tags, video, thumbnail match gameplay [titles-descriptions]
- [ ] Fill in the Game details window before the first publish: Name, Genre, optional Subgenre, and a summary Description. [titles-descriptions]
- [ ] Compare your title against current top-chart titles for verb-noun saturation and differentiate the noun or setting. [titles-descriptions]
- [ ] Write description lines that start with core verbs (build, grow, clean) plus genre keywords. [titles-descriptions]
- [ ] Test a short comparison promo (price and scope versus a competitor) on X/Twitter and track its effect before repeating it. [titles-descriptions]
- [ ] Compare your title and description against 3 to 5 top games in the same genre and rewrite any generic phrasing that copies the common template. [titles-descriptions]
- [ ] Before naming, write down the target audience (kids, teens, etc.) and the interests of that group. [titles-descriptions]
- [ ] Choose title wording that fits the audience's interests, not only the mechanics. [titles-descriptions]
- [ ] Open RoTrends and sort by momentum or visits [trend-research-and-validation]
- [ ] Search the genre keyword on Roblox and Creator Exchange sorted by visits [trend-research-and-validation]
- [ ] Count dominant games and note their genres [trend-research-and-validation]
- [ ] Check like ratio of competitors [trend-research-and-validation]
- [ ] Check off-platform demand: Steam wishlists, CrazyGames, YouTube [trend-research-and-validation]
- [ ] Compute an opportunity score (demand, competition, momentum, replicability) [trend-research-and-validation]
- [ ] Stress-test the idea for 1-2 weeks [trend-research-and-validation]
- [ ] Plan a launch audience (ads, TikTok, friends) [trend-research-and-validation]
- [ ] Check favorites as a demand signal on UGC [trend-research-and-validation]
- [ ] Rerun research on other platforms if no good trend appears [trend-research-and-validation]
- [ ] Check how many of the top-chart slots in your genre went to games launched in the last 90 days; new games held 28% of the top 100 by hours and 33% of the top 1000 in August 2026 (N7529). [trend-research-and-validation]
- [ ] Do not treat a single tracker breakout score as validation; check velocity or repeat appearances first (N7559). [trend-research-and-validation]
- [ ] Before choosing a genre, look up its approximate player share and visit totals on competitors, then verify with your own data. [trend-research-and-validation]
- [ ] Count dominant titles in the target genre. If two or three dominate, plan a clear twist or niche before committing. [trend-research-and-validation]
- [ ] Pull top-impression ads for about 10 direct competitors and note what each does that works. [trend-research-and-validation]
- [ ] Check Facebook Ads Library and TikTok Ads Library for the genre as a free first pass. [trend-research-and-validation]
- [ ] Review any genre trend across 90-180 days before acting on it. [trend-research-and-validation]
- [ ] Save competitor and adjacent-genre ads regularly on a clean device. [trend-research-and-validation]
- [ ] Check CTR trends for genre ads; falling CTR suggests saturation. [trend-research-and-validation]
- [ ] Shortlist candidate genres and mechanics from the Reblocks discover page alongside the front page and charts. [trend-research-and-validation]
- [ ] Build the avatar item through model, texture, import, Accessory Fitting Tool, Avatar Setup, validate, publish. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] Animation pack: original work only, one per day, 80 Robux, validate all seven state groups. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] Before upload, confirm inner and outer cages are complete on every accessory and layered clothing item; partial cages fail validation. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] Run Avatar Setup and the Blender validation tool, then verify the body against character specs even if the importer passed. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] Finalize and validate every animation state before publishing a pack; clips cannot be updated afterward. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] For accessories, upload via the Avatar tab as Hat type, wait for validation, then set the category (face accessory), name it, and add it to a bundle if required. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] Choose a UI style that fits the genre before building the palette and components. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Theme every release and save seasonal ideas for the right time [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Run a live event every 2-3 months at a time that suits your main audience's timezone [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Set a target cadence: weekly ideal, monthly minimum, and write the cadence into the schedule. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Keep experience details and genre accurate; genre changes are limited to once every three months. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Before a seasonal event, confirm the theme fits your audience and that the work is not rushed. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Use waiting-list or demand signals (e.g. players waiting for the first update) to prioritise the next release. [updates-live-ops]

## 2. Core loop and economy design

### Must
- [ ] Give each playtest a one-minute goal and a specific task to check. x2 [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Duplicate the first pose to the last frame for loops; rename final keyframe to 'End' only to replace defaults. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] For loops: copy the 0:00 start pose, paste at the end time, then turn on Loop to preview. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Set combat and ability clips to non-looping Action priority, preload them, and note the strike timestamp for the hitbox. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Make the last keyframe match the first for loops (already in existing rules; reinforced by Xaiyne). [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] For reward or difficulty tuning, run a multivariate test over player segments instead of one fixed formula. [ab-testing-and-experiments]
- [ ] Use launch data for ad-specific onboarding or rewards [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Add rewarded video with clear reward text and multiple placements; track opt-in rate and frequency [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]

### Also consider
- [ ] Set the ad goal to Maximize Plays for a new game, with all devices and regions unless you have a reason to narrow. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Don't buy ads for a game the algorithm already surfaces, or one with unfinished core loop. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Track the share of total earnings coming from rewarded-video ads, not just ad spend, to judge monetization health. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Remove feed-based rewards before seeking Kids or Select placement; check Creator Hub > Creations for media feed plus reward mechanics. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Test whether the average player believes watching a feed is required for rewards. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Decide target age and advertising scope before launch, because unlocking all ages may limit promotion. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Use official Investor Relations figures (57% global DAU age-check penetration, 90% long-term goal) rather than indie numbers in planning. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Instrument funnels, economy and custom events before launch (server-side only). [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Track economy sources, sinks and average wallet balance. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Log economy events after success, with itemSKU and positive amounts. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Track rewarded video opt-in (15%+) and frequency (3+). [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Pass economy transaction type and item SKU whenever currency changes. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Re-check server context per action: distance, ownership, alive state, cooldown, currency (T7461, T5354, T5590). [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Reject currency or item amounts that are <= 0 or fractional. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Remove large rewards for joining Communities and for kills on newly spawned players. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Do not publish reward winners' names, especially for valuable or one-of-one rewards. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Re-verify leaderboard and reward results on the server before any payout. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Audit every TeleportService call that passes TeleportData and confirm no secure or economy values are in it. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Return PurchaseGranted only after the reward has been applied to the player's data. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Do not grant gamepass or product rewards from PromptProductPurchaseFinished or PromptGamePassPurchaseFinished alone; check the ID and wasPurchased first. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] If you move drop or pickup collection to the client to stop theft, keep the reward grant on the server and check ownership, distance, and single claim there. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Before shipping client-side collection, test whether a modified client can claim other players' drops or grant itself rewards. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Draw the route graph from top view and confirm more than one route exists (no single circular loop). [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Put key progression items where players are guided to them. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Add a visible timer or closing door to obby reward rooms. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Place a goal trigger behind each goal: anchored, CanCollide off, transparency 0.5 for visibility. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Check that the goal trigger does not block the ball during playtest. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Check animated gradients loop: transparency sequence set, colour sequence ends on its start colour. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Make first and last poses identical for loops; keep clips at 10 seconds or less and body motion within 1.5 studs per frame. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Use Image asset IDs for icons and mission or reward icons, not Decal IDs. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Loop emotes: keyframe start pose and mid pose, copy the first keyframe to the end, use curved interpolation; a typical emote is about 6 s. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] When a camera zoom ends in a jolt, print start distance and height at the loop end and apply a small offset. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Keep the reward tool master in ReplicatedStorage and clone it into Backpack or Character on grant (T18410). [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Process purchases on the server: check product, balance and eligibility, then deduct or grant (T4983, T6377). [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Grant purchases in ProcessReceipt; reward handlers return true only when actually granted. [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Before moving a drop or pickup to the client, confirm the reward is granted only after a server-side validity check. [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Treat client-side pickup or drop visuals as cosmetic, and keep reward state in server containers. [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Use CollectionService tags to attach behaviour; wire GetInstanceAddedSignal and GetInstanceRemovedSignal, not just an initial GetTagged loop. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Connect GetInstanceAddedSignal before looping GetTagged, and pass each tagged instance into the handler. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Keep economy and purchase logic on the server Script. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Route every item grant, clear and currency change through one API function in a single ModuleScript. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Make parry window narrow; reward with stun/slow-mo, not an instant kill. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Keep rarity weights summing to 1; convert duplicates to currency. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Start resource caps low (for example bag Spaces=2) so players want upgrades early. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Check progression content length against playtime; a five-spawn-area template was exhausted in seconds (Q1598). [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Before adding an accessory, loop character children and destroy existing Accessory items at Handle > FaceFrontAttachment (T17784). [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Expose each player's upgrade values through one central module and have other scripts subscribe to change events (T17934). [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] When a gamepass unlocks a tool, grant it on the server by cloning the tool into player.Backpack, not from the client. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] When scaling enemy damage to remaining health, document the formula in your data tables so balance changes stay traceable. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Fix the core loop before spending on ads [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Make rewarded ad rewards deterministic and account-bound [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Plan sinks and event economy impact [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Verify every client-reported hit or result on the server before granting rewards or changes. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Walk the full progression to the final wave or level to find impossible points. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Ship when the core loop is fun and content is sufficient, not on a deadline alone (T13647, T13605). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Gate score accrual by a per-player alive flag; persist time-reward progress if it must carry over (T14400, T14582). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Audit reward wording in feeds for implied viewing rewards (T13734). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Attach a catch to every Promise chain and put task.wait() in every infinite loop (T10558, T14668). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Check the economy: selling price must be higher than purchase price, or there is no profit (LovJak). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Sandbox user code so errors and infinite loops cannot crash Studio (Last Level Studios). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Keep event progression in one hub, free, and avoid splitting it across many games (ThatGuyEvan). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Tie currency and rewards to player actions, not passive timers that auto clickers can exploit (Lnor). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Before each release, confirm the core loop is fun and stable, not just that the planned features exist. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Do not sell a bypass for a scarce reward you limited to one player at a time. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Test every developer product purchase in Studio to confirm each product ID grants the intended reward (T18854). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] After any change, test each sell and economy path end-to-end, including gold credit (T18879). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Test that progression areas must be completed in order and cannot be skipped (T18824). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Test for duplication exploits (e.g. stacking rewards from one item) and patch quickly if players find one (T18766, Q1944). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Announce any monetisation change before it goes live; do not hide paid progression (T18956). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Run community map submission contests via Discord; add accepted maps and reward every entrant (example: 500 Robux each, 8 maps). [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Add invite rewards and an in-game invite button. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Set up Open Cloud milestone-to-role sync if forum-posting unlocks should track in-game progress. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Reward teammates' effort in public and rotate helper or leadership roles. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Use monthly top-100 leaderboard rewards for a recurring reason to return. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Choose community structure based on whether rank unlocks gameplay (flat groups vs ranked hierarchy). [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Decide and publish your private-server policy before launch, including the fairness and economy reason. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Write the core loop as action, reward, repeat plus a progression engine; confirm it is explainable in one sentence/30 seconds. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Prototype with greybox parts and playtest the loop before building other systems. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Check each proposed system answers 'how does this feed the loop?'; cut it otherwise. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Define short, mid and long-term goals and show the long-term goal early. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Make the first session rewarding: low early XP thresholds, an unlock every few minutes. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Log unlock timestamps on a fresh alt account to find walls. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Add visible progression (XP bar, multipliers, rebirth/prestige). [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Plan sources and sinks; add caps, drains and event currency. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Add risk/reward (steal, cash-out, safe zone) where it fits the loop. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Keep health, combat, inventory and reward logic in your own deterministic game code. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Solo-test the loop end to end; if one player can finish it alone, fix that before adding features. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Confirm the loop always has a next goal or pending mission. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Gate each new zone with a speed or progression tier that the player can see. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Confirm rebirth keeps currency, resets speed, and grants a multiplier. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Playtest progression speed; add an unlock gate if players finish content too quickly. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Keep mini-games secondary; main income stays in the core loop. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Confirm the loop has more than one optimal strategy. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Match optional reward value to the time cost of the optional content. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Pair fuel or hunger drains with the harvest loop so gathering keeps progression moving. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Add a storage cap and a paid upgrade so players must sell, creating a gold sink. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Give upgrades a story reason so progression feels like helping a character. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Replace walk-to-collect pickups with a click or interaction step inside the loop. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Give each paid upgrade tier two benefits (for example strength and earnings multiplier) for a clear upgrade path. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Add upgrade or scaling paths so older units keep value in later waves. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Audit the loop for loading screens and waits between actions, and remove or shorten them. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Test each multiplier at the highest expected count, such as 400,000 rebirths, to check it does not compound out of control. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Spawn timed items with a loop and a fixed interval; start at 2 seconds and tune from playtests. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Add a game-over trigger at health 0 or below and break the spawn or wave loop when it fires. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Generate at least 100 procedural layouts and check them for loops, dead ends and corner bugs before release. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] For small maps, consider a build-to-earn or key-hunt loop when distance-based zoning is not possible. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Before a porting push, check your own concurrency and platform analytics rather than relying on the ~10-player heuristic. [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] Run an autosave loop with an interval of 30 to 120 s and a random first offset per player. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Save the PurchaseId before granting items; revoke granted currency manually on refunds. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Save on three triggers: PlayerRemoving, an autosave loop, and BindToClose (typical autosave ~60 s). [data-persistence]
- [ ] Build currency, unlocks and loadouts persistence before cosmetic or secondary systems. [data-persistence]
- [ ] If a feature moves players between servers by teleport, confirm that all secure data (currency, inventory, progress) is saved to a DataStore or MemoryStore before the teleport, and reloaded on arrival. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Do not put currency or inventory into TeleportData as the source of truth. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Run a per-player autosave loop (task.spawn) at about 60 seconds in production to limit DataStore load (self-reported). [data-persistence]
- [ ] Test save behaviour with the autosave loop, because a Studio stop may skip PlayerRemoving. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Define one core loop and cut everything else for the MVP [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Keep updates to 2-3 goals [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Build the minimal MVP around one core loop, then plan a bite-size 5-6 week release. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Set incremental goals when learning Lua, and dissect other people's game code line by line. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Judge ad experiments at day 5 by benchmarks and the core loop. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Enable AvatarJointUpgrade or other upcoming betas in a test place early to see breaking changes. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Map the full loop, including wrong-input handling, cleanup and reset, before coding. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Shorten growth, cooldown and timer values in a test config so the full loop can be verified in minutes; restore them before release. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] For a small prototype, exclude multiplayer and add networking after the core loop is proven. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] After playtests show unbalanced progression, add content (e.g. more items) and retest rather than only tuning numbers. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Confirm the core loop is fun in a playtest before spending time on title, icon, or description. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Review each step in the core loop and remove any that adds travel or chores without adding fun. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Add non-spammy monetization; consider rewarded video [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] Set session-length targets per age band: shorter loops for younger players, deeper loops for older players. [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] Reward events with a challenge, not on join [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] Add a live leaderboard with better first-place rewards [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] Cap supply of exclusive event pets or items so the trading economy keeps scarcity value. [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] Attach a usable key item (a consumable or stat-restoring item) to the final quest reward so the payoff matters in gameplay. [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] If you sell early single-player access, make it a pre-order unlock rather than a general early-access grant. [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] For leaderboard-based event rewards, set explicit score thresholds per reward tier (example: 1,500 for the best egg, 800 for the mutated egg). [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] Schedule an admin-abuse-style event on the update day to lift concurrency (N8024). [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] For a limited battle pass, use a separate event currency that drops in the world and set 20 tiers with exclusive eggs at the top. [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] Define at least two point thresholds for collection rewards, with the top tier as a prestige reward. [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] Make the core action and goal obvious within seconds of joining [first-session-onboarding]
- [ ] Reach first fun/reward within ~8-30 seconds [first-session-onboarding]
- [ ] Ensure full core loop is understood by 2-5 minutes [first-session-onboarding]
- [ ] Give starter currency/items and a low-friction first purchase [first-session-onboarding]
- [ ] Show visible goals/task list on HUD [first-session-onboarding]
- [ ] Gate advanced UI and features behind progression [first-session-onboarding]
- [ ] Congratulate on tutorial end with next goal or unlock [first-session-onboarding]
- [ ] Instrument funnel from join to first loop completion [first-session-onboarding]
- [ ] Set a starting currency balance (e.g. 10 coins) and verify the first purchase is reachable from it. [first-session-onboarding]
- [ ] When a progression system such as bags is unlocked at an NPC, place an in-world sign there announcing it (T18557). [first-session-onboarding]
- [ ] Scope the first tutorial to one complete core loop and stop there (example: buy, plant, wait, sell) (T18898). [first-session-onboarding]
- [ ] Verify every reward category shows particles and a notification on drop, not only the most common one (T18819). [first-session-onboarding]
- [ ] Pick a proven reference loop and list its building blocks [game-ideas-and-genres]
- [ ] Fix the 30-second core loop before opening Studio [game-ideas-and-genres]
- [ ] Plan a behavior-changing endgame unlock at the design stage, not as a late add-on. (T17985) [game-ideas-and-genres]
- [ ] For an infinite-climb style concept, clone platforms, add a periodic reward such as a jump boost every second, and remove the base plate (T18644). [game-ideas-and-genres]
- [ ] Decide mobile/PC balance [genre-mechanics]
- [ ] Balance or cut unused towers [genre-mechanics]
- [ ] Add build/upgrade depth to tycoons [genre-mechanics]
- [ ] Before building a meme- or brainrot-themed game, plan the depth systems (progression, collection, retention loops) because virality normalises quickly. Cite T17463 and T17471. [genre-mechanics]
- [ ] For horror, add a survival loop before adding scares, and keep jump scares rare. Cite T17484. [genre-mechanics]
- [ ] Gate giveaway rewards behind the tracked link [influencer-and-creator-marketing]
- [ ] Replace polling loops with event:Wait() or an event connection; check the flag first to skip yielding on already-fired events. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Cache services and frequently used paths in top-level locals; avoid globals in loops. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Remove matching array items by looping backward and using numeric indexes only. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] When one handler must run for many buttons, loop GetDescendants() and check IsA("TextButton") before connecting. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Use continue inside loops to skip invalid items instead of nesting the main body in another if. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Name unused loop indices _ in for-loops. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Set Access Control to Secure within universe only for progression-gated games, test places, or reserved-server-only places. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Replace wait() with task.wait; spread heavy loops across frames; use parallel Luau for DataModel-free compute. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] For tycoons and idle games, test the fully upgraded or maximum-progression state on a low-end device before release. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Track part, effect and light counts per upgrade tier to find which unlock causes the largest frame-time jump. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Write a scoring system that rewards combos and variety and penalises damage taken. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Use allCollected for a one-time reward per player. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] If using randomised rewards, show odds and ensure good results still happen. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Frame limited items and daily rewards as losses if the player does not return. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Give a free earn-once timed reward alongside any paid crates. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Add a visible pity counter that guarantees a top reward after N opens. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Pair milestones with a cosmetic unlock and an animated milestone screen. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Show an on-screen gain animation for every currency earned. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Stagger large reward reveals so one moment is not buried under many numbers. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Let a sink item (cosmetic trails or similar) use progression currency. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Test that upgrades serve casual and competitive players alike. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] If the game has collectible pets or mounts, decide what each one changes in the core loop (speed, income, reach) before adding more. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] After every tuning change, play the loop yourself and replay it before moving on [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Treat any AI-generated mechanic, balance or feel values as a draft until playtested [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Playtest every AI-generated enemy's stats (health, damage, speed) before release and rebalance them. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Gate premium currency behind time or progress rather than selling instant access. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Pair harder challenge with clear reward upgrades so difficulty does not drive players away. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Add a sound effect to egg openings and pad unlocks. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Add a door-slide, screen shake, smoke particles and a sound effect to area unlocks. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Frame login streak rewards around what the player loses by missing a day. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Run a cheat speedrun to test progression [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Ask testers to flag confusing mechanics, unclear goals, unpleasant UI or map, and hard-to-follow instructions [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Keep A/B testing placement, button design, reward type and amount for conversion [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Play the template before editing to learn the gameplay loop. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Check progression pacing both too fast and too slow, and test late waves. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Track rebirth and progress-reset behaviour to see what drives long-term play. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Change one balance value, then playtest it before changing the next. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Check whether items are too easy or too far to find, and whether upgrade costs feel too easy or too hard. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Audit each survey and test incentive; remove currency rewards for survey completion. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Add daily login reward with 7-day streak and durable item [retention-d1-d7]
- [ ] Add playtime rewards at ~10/30/60 min (or shorter thresholds early) [retention-d1-d7]
- [ ] Show short, mid and long-term goals in UI with progress bars [retention-d1-d7]
- [ ] Add rebirth/prestige or ranked system for D30 [retention-d1-d7]
- [ ] Reward losing players with XP; keep respawns short [retention-d1-d7]
- [ ] Add friend-play features and invite rewards for both sides [retention-d1-d7]
- [ ] Make group/favorite rewards highly visible [retention-d1-d7]
- [ ] Cap rewarded ads at 5-10 per day, let players bank [retention-d1-d7]
- [ ] Fix the core loop before bolting on dailies [retention-d1-d7]
- [ ] In evolution or rebirth games, check how early rebirth is available and test whether earlier access lifts D1 and D7. [retention-d1-d7]
- [ ] Playtest the core loop for 30 minutes straight and note where it starts to feel repetitive. [retention-d1-d7]
- [ ] Pick a calendar length (for example 24 days) and decide whether a missed day resets rewards or only locks them. [retention-d1-d7]
- [ ] Add a one-time group-join plus favorite reward and track group and favorite counts over time. [retention-d1-d7]
- [ ] Use tiered gift boxes awarded from many different actions, so several activities feed the same reward hook. [retention-d1-d7]
- [ ] Do not rely on Engagement-Based Payouts; model Creator Rewards [roblox-platform-changes]
- [ ] Spread heavy loops across frames [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Gate currency actions with an in-progress flag and a shared can't-act state. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Replace per-object background loops with one central manager table and events. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Handle players already in the server by looping GetPlayers with task.spawn. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Compute animation or audio frame index from playback time, not a loop. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Loop until Workspace.CurrentCamera is non-nil before using it. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Run each tagged part's animation in its own thread so one loop does not skip parts. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Filter GetChildren() loops with IsA('BasePart') before changing properties. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Handle ReferredByPlayerID and reward inviter and invitee [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Add a trading system and rewards for first social acts [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Add like goals if like ratio is low [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Make the referral reward banner description match the Studio implementation; only one banner can be published at once. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Give week-one milestone rewards for friend invites and bonus currency for group play, without penalizing solo players. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Broadcast rare unlocks to all servers and in chat so other players chase the same reward. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] For TikTok promo clips, use the in-built editor and open with a cliffhanger hook and loop. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Prototype a turn-based or async loop so it can use the upcoming turn-alert push notifications. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Add a group-join reward and badges such as Welcome and Play With Friends. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Gate player trading behind a progression milestone. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Plan event-based peaks (for example, a timed hunt) to lift concurrency records. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Loop background music, enable Looped, set Volume about 0.2, and call Play() from a client script on join. [sound-and-music]
- [ ] Cut loops at zero crossings in Reaper or Audacity. [sound-and-music]
- [ ] Layer loops of different lengths; check the LCM of the lengths. [sound-and-music]
- [ ] Drive TimePosition-timed effects from a Heartbeat loop. [sound-and-music]
- [ ] Start zone and looping ambience from a LocalScript and stop the previous zone first. [sound-and-music]
- [ ] Test reset logic on a test server and run at least two full game loops; set minimumPlayers to 2 for faster tests. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Include balance (pacing, rewards, difficulty) in the multiplayer playtest, not just bug checks. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Replace the default reward banner icon with an image of your game. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Make the season's final reward a new asset retired after the season. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Check that every feature or progression claim in the description can be found in-game, not only on an external wiki. [titles-descriptions]
- [ ] Record real, current core-loop gameplay with freecam and hidden UI [trailers-and-video-marketing]
- [ ] Re-check your Experience Details Page videos: keep at least one current gameplay clip that shows the core loop, since GamesBeat reports these feed Moments from late September 2026. [trailers-and-video-marketing]
- [ ] Produce polished cinematic and looping motion-graphic cuts for paid Meta/Google ads (T17951). [trailers-and-video-marketing]
- [ ] Enable Enhanced Protection in Settings > Security if you are a top creator or hold a high Robux balance. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] If building a trading-based item economy, keep rare items scarce and avoid frequent re-releases of them (T17481). [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] Before launch, review progression for grind-plus-paid-boost pairings. If the grind feels engineered to sell boosts, rebalance it (T17528). [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] Allow gear upgrades without forcing an unequip. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Clone currency flyouts from a hidden template, offset their start with scale-based spread, wait about 1 s, then tween to the HUD. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Disable a ProximityPrompt on the client once its location is unlocked. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Announce level-ups visibly and list newly unlocked abilities. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Remove options that have no effect from encounter or upgrade choices. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Add a reward or goal trail to quest UI so the objective shows what it pays. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Monitor metrics, spender balances and feedback; set KPIs before designing [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Set start and end dates and reward limits on every reward banner. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Rotate shop stock and refresh rewarded ad rewards on a set schedule. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Add group rewards as a recurring retention hook. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Use the LiveOps dashboard for events and rewards that do not need a code change. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Before re-releasing an old feature, rebalance it and write the feedback into a design doc. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Keep a public stats and drop-rate reference (Trello or changelog) and update it with every balance change. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Track whether event concurrency holds above the pre-event baseline, not just the peak during the event. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Give each update one visible new hook (arena, pet or mechanic) and at least one rare unlock to chase. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Schedule timed random events on a roughly 30-minute loop to keep sessions active, then compare session length to baseline. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Before a seasonal quest, decide which rewards are never-again and state the end date clearly so the urgency is real. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Test major dependency upgrades on a branch before merging. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] For collectibles, add a looping spin and bob tween and give the pickup a distinct color. [vfx-and-particles]

## 3. Build quality: scripting, security, performance

### Must
- [ ] Save on PlayerRemoving and in game:BindToClose. x2 [data-persistence]
- [ ] Enable Studio API access before testing DataStores in Studio. x2 [data-persistence]
- [ ] Set normal map colourspace to NonColor after swapping textures. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Publish animations before referencing; move KeyframeSequence/CurveAnimation to ReplicatedStorage for client access. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] For glTF imports: disable Guess Original Bind Pose, rotate 180 on Z, remove vertex colours, set animation start to 0, save textures before FBX. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Stop if critical harm is flagged, or if SRM is detected (then restart) [ab-testing-and-experiments]
- [ ] Playtest on low-end devices and with Test/Test Here [ab-testing-and-experiments]
- [ ] Check whether ramping a winning variant can be done in-Studio via Configs and Experiments before doing manual web checks. [ab-testing-and-experiments]

### Also consider
- [ ] Check daily that spend matches budget and that credits are not stopping early. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Do not schedule ads for launch day if you are still fixing bugs; ensure a stable build before traffic arrives (T18920). [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] For mobile-app UA only: favour first-party ad platforms and limit DSP middlemen (T18786, T18787). [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] For mobile-app UA only: align mediation with the buying network (T18788). [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Build a moderation system that combines published messages with a DataStore blocklist. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] For motion-based mobile play or physical reference poses, set safety guidelines and a safe space. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Keep descriptions, thumbnails and videos all-ages appropriate even for 17+ experiences. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Use the Social Links feature and experience description for off-platform links; never try to bypass moderation. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Keep group and account standing clean, since one flagged image led to an account and group lock. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Review player-built drawing, shape or free-form placement systems for misuse potential before any moderation flag arrives. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Check the current Creator Hub for the status of any 2026-09 announcement before citing it to a client or team. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Check error report, crash rate and Performance by Place Version after every publish. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Read FPS, memory and session time as P10/P50/P90 by platform and device category, not averages (T7336, T7343, T9698). [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Set alerts on ClientCrashRate15m and OomUnexpectedExits by platform and memory group (T7335, T9040). [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Set a DataStore quota alert per DataStoreRequestSource and watch the Memory Store yellow warning at 70% of quota (T7337, T7518, T8527). [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Review Creations > Monitoring > Data Stores for request counts and Request Count by Status to find failing API methods (T8597, T8598, T7521). [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Check the Performance Dashboard after each publish for FPS, memory and crash rate by device category (T8442, T9698). [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Use the Developer Console Network tab to inspect DataStore and HttpService status codes, timing and failures (T7803). [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Set an analytics_data category per notification type to compare notification performance (T10039). [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Export performance data within 28 days and thumbnail data within 30 days (T9192, T9193). [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Log funnel steps server-side at the moment progress happens, and confirm arrival in View Events before judging (charts take about 24 hours). [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Filter out exploiters and players inactive 15–30 days before analysing retention, playtime or revenue. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Check the Performance Dashboard after each update and link it to session time. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Export performance data within its 28-day history window before it expires. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Compute minimum server count as CCU divided by 30 and check headroom. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] After every update, set up a CCU and crash-rate watch and write the rollback threshold down before release (T17610). [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] When the Observability Platform is available, enable client session tracing for the release build and check logs for errors after launch (T17661). [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Inventory every RemoteEvent, RemoteFunction, ProximityPrompt, ClickDetector and DragDetector; treat each as an attack surface (T7466, T5131). [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Move all authoritative logic and data into ServerScriptService or ServerStorage; nothing secret in ReplicatedStorage (T7447, T5125, T6282). [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Add per-UserId server-side cooldowns or a token bucket to each remote; clear on PlayerRemoving (T7465, T5594). [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Add honeypot remotes that legitimate code never fires; log and kick on any firing (T9135, T5131). [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Remove or avoid memory checks and client-only detection bans (T5622, T9127). [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Audit free models: delete scripts, check creator and account age (T5235, T5245). [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Grant only required Capabilities; review Network, DataStore, LoadString and AccessOutsideWrite grants (T7025, T7026, T10372). [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Search scripts for loadstring, getfenv, setfenv, require, MarketplaceService and IsStudio (T5236, T5244). [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Store keys and tokens in the Secrets store; call GetSecret only from server scripts (T8937, T10254). [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Use minimal-scope, IP-restricted API keys; send Open Cloud requests server-side only (T8514, T10041). [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Use PKCE, state, server-side code exchange and userinfo sub for OAuth (T7652, T7649, T7647). [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Read DeviceTrustLevel server-side; treat Pending as untrusted; use a table for levels (T8610, T8611, T8612). [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Sanitize TextBox input on the server and pass public text through the text filter (T9668, Q1114). [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Type-check, existence-check and NaN-check every remote argument before use. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Look up prices and item data from a server-side table keyed by name; never accept them from the client. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Disable client-settable Dead state with Humanoid:SetStateEnabled. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Accept hit results only for targets the server sent; reject re-hits and expired attacks. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Forward ScriptContext errors to a remote and review manually before banning. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Return nil to suspected exploiters so they time out. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Use a server-invoked RemoteFunction for tamper signals instead of a fired ban remote. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Filter player text with TextService:FilterStringAsync on the server. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Threat-model each feature at 1,000+ calls per second before shipping. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Search free models for script, require, getfenv, and loadstring before inserting. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Check permission, rank, server type, ownership and current state on the server before granting seat or control requests. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Add server-side distance and line-of-sight checks to pickups and ProximityPrompt-based interactions. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Register the Safety Callback API in a server script at startup (early access, Oct 2026). [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Process every callback in the same session so no flag is left unresolved and no rescan shuts the server. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Build an in-game appeal path that restores falsely flagged creations using the UserId tag. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Use HumanoidDescription to build avatars so outfit scanning can flag violations before other players see them. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Track avatar-related kicks, callbacks handled, and server shutdowns as metrics to confirm the handling works. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Find every teleport trigger (Touched, ProximityPrompt, remote-triggered TeleportService calls) and confirm the destination is chosen server-side. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] On arrival, have the destination server re-derive any state it needs from its own datastore instead of trusting TeleportData. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Use __metatable = 'locked' (or similar) on server modules or config proxies whose metatables should not be read or replaced by other scripts. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Do not rely on __metatable for secrets or anti-cheat logic; keep those on the server and out of client-visible code. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] On PlayerRemoving, save the player's data first, then destroy the data folder. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Recompute the grid-snapped block position on the server and reject any client position outside tolerance. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Compare client and server positions with Vector3:FuzzyEq using a tolerance matched to the grid size. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Route all placement and break requests through one server handler that runs every check in order. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Use exactly one MarketplaceService.ProcessReceipt handler in one server Script for all developer products. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Check receipt product ID and buyer existence in ProcessReceipt; return NotProcessedYet when the player is not in the server. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Enable StreamingEnabled first and keep each model under about 64 cubic studs. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Tag repeated parts and drive them with one script using CollectionService. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Set Snap to Grid for Move to 4 studs for buildings; use 5-stud snapping with 90-degree rotation for greybox. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Set collision and shadow flags (CanCollide, CanTouch, CanQuery, CastShadow) at placement time. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Keep templates in ServerStorage or ReplicatedStorage and clone at runtime. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Enable streaming with defaults; place blocking geometry along paths. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Name each split mesh part so scripts and textures can target it. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Fill the map with real build content before scripting tycoon or mechanic systems [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Confirm every model fetched from ReplicatedStorage or ServerStorage exists, with a nil check or timed WaitForChild, before the flow depends on it. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Store maps and weapon templates in ServerStorage, not ReplicatedStorage. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Build UI mobile-first with Scale, aspect ratio and size constraints [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Keep textures at 1024 or below and watch texture memory [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Disable custom LODs when enabling mesh streaming [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] View UI in grayscale before shipping; fix any element that disappears. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Test font sizes on real mobile devices at the smallest expected screen size. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] For Quest, target 72 FPS, watch draw calls and keep SurfaceGui count low. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Save the animation after every edit and confirm in a playtest. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Check any contracted or rushed model before shipping; one crop model shipped with exclusive seeds not loading. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Put particle emitters, beams and lights inside an Attachment per part; set EmitCount, EmitDelay and EmitDuration attributes to script all effects uniformly. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Save a configured instance (e.g. anchored MeshPart) via Edit > Save as Default [Instance] so new inserts match. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Store large texture data as raw RGBA byte buffers in a ModuleScript; do not open raw bitmap modules directly in Studio, which can crash it. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Make the marketplace thumbnail with the ThumbMaker plugin from the Handle, then right-click Handle > Save and Export to Roblox. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Set a conveyor's AssemblyLinearVelocity on its axis instead of scripting it. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Use a floor texture strip for a checkered finish line instead of a flag. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Vet free Toolbox models by reviews and scripts before inserting them. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Add noise after clone stamping to restore texture. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Keep hand-made collision simple until the improved collision geometry feature ships; then check the debug overlay. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Before the 4K PBR High quality texture mode is enabled, budget memory per surface area as usual and plan for 4K maps only on hero surfaces. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Run chopping and interaction animations on the client for responsive feedback (T18591). [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Save Brush tool settings when building terrain so you can reproduce the look without rebuilding (T18768). [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Save and commit progress frequently; a power loss forced retexturing of finished pets (T18853). [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Identify every remote and mark it client-to-server or server-to-client; fire only in its direction (T9136). [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] For each RemoteFunction, return a success boolean and assign only one OnServerInvoke callback (T5353, T9956). [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Use RemoteFunctions only for client-to-server results; use RemoteEvents for server-to-client notifications and UI (T9955). [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Put remotes in ReplicatedStorage and route them through one typed network or SerDes module (T9957, T6387, T5730). [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Use UnreliableRemoteEvent for continuous data; keep payloads under 1,000 bytes and include sequence numbers (T9958, N3937, T9959). [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Connect handlers before firing remotes or unreliable messages will be lost or queued (T9961). [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Do not assume ordering between remotes and property/attribute changes; add sequence numbers when order matters (T7869, T9960). [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Run VFX, animations, tweens and sounds on clients; send only the trigger and location (T5593, T8278, T6262). [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Never tween or animate server-side objects; the replicated property changes every frame (T8278). [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Interpolate look or tilt on the server from throttled (~0.1 s) updates rather than per-frame remotes (T5001). [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Keep server-only data in ServerStorage or ServerScriptService; never in ReplicatedStorage (T5491, T8134). [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Call SetValue on replicated state alongside saved-data changes so UI matches the server (T5579). [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Use WaitForChild with a timeout for remotes, modules, tool children and runtime-created instances on the client (T5909, T8579). [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Use LocalScripts for camera, input, UI and tool activation; use server Scripts for authoritative logic (T7659, T9096). [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Use Script.RunContext Client when a script must run on each client rather than in a tool or starter folder (T9883). [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Keep cloud, secret, data and messaging API calls in server scripts only (T8939, T10257). [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Pick the model per game: competitive shooters, racing, sports and combat should evaluate engine Server Authority first. [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Under Server Authority, read input by polling InputAction.GetState in BindToSimulation, not Changed/Pressed events. [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Connect RemoteEvent listeners before any server fires; use WaitForChild with exact remote names on the client. [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Wrap every client InvokeServer in pcall and define a fallback return value. [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Wrap every server InvokeClient in a task.delay timeout of about 5 s. [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Batch related updates into one remote or one table per frame instead of many small remotes. [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Send shot origin, direction, synced timestamp and shot ID; have the server rewind and deduplicate. [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Replicate state via attributes server-to-client; send client intents through a single RemoteEvent. [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Create and modify Replicas only on the server; call RequestData once on the client and Subscribe players. [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Verify group membership on the server with GetGroupsAsync after the join prompt. [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Destroy server-owned objects to unload them rather than reparenting. [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Keep ownership deliberate: server-own NPC roots, avoid frequent SetNetworkOwner churn. [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Send round-end results (winning team, each client's own team) in the event payload. [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Test with Test > local server, one player and two instances, changing things on each side to see what replicates. [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Put each new server feature behind a server-side flag that defaults to off. [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Ramp a new flag 1% → 5% → 10% → 50% → 100%, watching crash rates at each stage. [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Keep a kill switch: turn the flag off immediately if crashes spike. [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Assign each RemoteFunction variable a name different from its OnServerInvoke handler function. [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] On CharacterAdded, fire a RemoteEvent so clients remove or rebuild custom PlayerGui. [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Pair enter/exit events with a periodic server sweep that forces exit for stale players. [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Do not make server logic depend on instances created in a LocalScript. [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Make player-data changes in server-side ProximityPrompt.Triggered handlers, not in client code. [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Keep turret, aim and AI targeting on the server even when it costs performance. [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Give server-side timing windows for parry and dodge inputs enough tolerance for latency. [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Do the heavy one-time indexing on the server, then have clients fetch chunk data via RemoteFunction. [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Keep Charm atoms server-side and replicate them when state must live outside components. [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] For competitive games, combine server authority with custom matchmaking to control match composition (T17600, indie, self-reported). [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Apply cosmetics on the server in PlayerAdded and CharacterAppearanceLoaded so they reapply on respawn (T17778). [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Before honoring a client sprint or similar action, check server-side health and alive status (T17891). [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Before a teleport, fire a server-to-client RemoteEvent so the client can show a transition GUI. [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Use separate start and stop RemoteEvents for toggle actions such as sprint; the server owns the state change. [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Before placing an item on a client request, verify PlayerData exists, the item type is valid, and the count is at least 1. [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Use Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter in shared server handlers (such as humanoid death) to identify the player. [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Confirm RemoteEvents referenced by AI-generated scripts exist in ReplicatedStorage before testing (low-advice source). [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Call PromptGamePassPurchase from a LocalScript and pass the local player. [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] In a server Script, connect MarketplaceService.PromptGamePassPurchaseFinished and check player, gamepass ID and wasPurchased before granting. [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Do not set Anchored on tools a character is holding; check for ground-clipping or stuck characters when testing tools. [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] If a script throws 'is not a valid member of DataModel', check for direct service access and switch to GetService. [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Start each class as a ModuleScript: Class.__index = Class, Class.new returning setmetatable, colon methods, return the object. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Put shared modules in ReplicatedStorage; server-only in ServerScriptService or ServerStorage; never ship server logic to clients. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Keep server scripts out of StarterPlayerScripts; use StarterCharacterScripts for per-character logic. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Choose one server entry script and one client entry script; load modules through a bootstrap or loader so startup order is deterministic. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Split any script doing multiple jobs; one responsibility per module, named after its purpose. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Keep constants, IDs and stage lists in one config ModuleScript; avoid hardcoded counts or limits. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Add --!strict to scripts and define shared export types in a ModuleScript. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Use local variables, descriptive names, indentation and a top-of-file block comment. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Use BindableEvents for coordination and ModuleScripts for shared data. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Check Workspace.SignalBehavior (Default, Immediate or Deferred) before debugging event order. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Consider Sera for shared serialization schemas if a project needs typed server-client serialization. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Put setup in initialize and cross-module connections in start for every module, and call them from one server and one client bootstrap. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Save each require() to a top-level local once; for profiles, use a Cache module keyed by UserId. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Define every remote and packet in one shared module required by both sides. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Use RemoteEvents to cross client-server and BindableEvents within one side. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Reset state-specific flags inside set_state on every transition; nil-guard optional hooks. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Name the tag after the module and use multiple tags (category plus specific) for grouping. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Split any script over ~1000 lines and any section over ~100 lines. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Order each script as services, modules, instances, variables; use PascalCase for classes, modules and types. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
