- [ ] Keep LocalScripts to input only; put logic in modules. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Write new logic as functions and modules from the first version; do not copy-paste scripts. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Keep the data layer as a Modules folder in ServerScriptService (DataManager, Template, LeaderStats). [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] For large static item catalogues, use one ModuleScript per item and a registry keyed by ID. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] When checking several downstream conditions, test the most restrictive one first and return early. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Choose one zone interaction approach per system and create one observer per system. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Return an exit function from zone observers and use metadata to identify the zone. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Put accessory and other clone-only templates in a dedicated ServerStorage folder, never in Workspace. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Name server scripts *.server.luau, client scripts *.client.luau, and ModuleScripts *.luau. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Pick one variable naming convention and check it across all scripts. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Use an Observers-style helper for player, class and tag observation and add its cleanup to your Trove. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Before scripting a new game, check whether an earlier game's framework already covers the needed system (data, state, UI, remotes, tags) and reuse it. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Wrap one world object's logic in a table module with setup and update, and require it from the main script. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Check team membership on server before damage. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Keep tools unanchored; fire tool effects from server via RemoteEvent. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Use UpdateAsync for shared multi-server data. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Use magnitude or OverlapParams for melee on the server; use raycast for guns; reserve Touched for anchored triggers. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Check the return value of the hitbox constructor on the client, where it can be false. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Gate attacks by state: block them while stunned, jumping or in other invalid states. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Set ability cooldowns server-side as timed states; add a client flag for button spam. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Use ProximityPrompt rather than ClickDetector for interactions, since ClickDetector fails on mobile. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Check cash before purchase; deduct and increment owned in one server call. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Test every gear item for Scripts that bypass map restrictions or damage others. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] On respawn, clone tools from ServerStorage into the Backpack, skipping any already present in the Character or Backpack. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Before designing team or round sizes, open Game Settings and check the Server capacity value (official figure: 7 per N7421). [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Make team caps and matchmaking minimums no larger than the server capacity, so rounds can always fill. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Before scripting an NPC, write down its role (tutorial, companion, opponent or boss), its abilities and its intelligence level. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] For enemy loot, read the death position and guard for a nil root part before dropping. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Reuse one pickup script for house spawn parts and enemy drops. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Build weapon variants by cloning a template tool and swapping only its model. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Cap pets, eggs and purchase counts server-side [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Check subscription status on the server [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Test every Luau if/and/or branch with 0 and empty string inputs; only nil and false are falsy. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Run a two-client test and a server restart test before promoting. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Repeat farming and stacking actions many times to check for infinite-resource and stacking exploits before launch. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Fix game-breaking bugs and check ad click-through before scaling ad spend. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Set memory store expiry expectations (45 days max) and use data stores for long-term persistence. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] After a restore, publish and restart servers; verify the live build (T15355, T13901). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Use GetTouchingParts and InputAction:GetState under rollback; don't publish Server Authority yet (T13088, T13087). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Parent actors and workers to valid containers; wrap LocalScripts in a ScreenGui (T12923, T11923). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Don't assign Scribe values with '='; deploy server and client together (T13589, T13590). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Enable Game Settings > Security > Studio Access to API Services, or Studio cannot access DataStores and saving fails (Amora). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Check that the respawn or reset popup does not reappear after each death, since a bug here can make the game unplayable (Mulch). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Plan hosting and server scale before growth; Rover needed 32+ GB RAM at ~100k servers (Last Level Studios). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Test any script or Toolbox asset for frame-rate impact before adding it (Last Level Studios). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Credit inspirations in the description, but don't rely on credit to prevent copycat accusations. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Before shipping open-ended creation, avatar, or roleplay features, list the unsafe-content paths and design them out first. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] When moderation flags an instance, remove it for all players, not just the one who reported or triggered it. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Search the codebase for ReserveServerAsync and replace it with HttpService:GenerateGUID plus ReservedServerId. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Test reserved-server teleport and join flows after migrating, including players arriving at the reserved server. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] If the update cadence is under pressure, cut features rather than shipping half-working systems. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] After each bug fix, run a regression check on the systems the fix touched before pushing. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Add comments or descriptions that explain why code works and key design decisions, not just placement. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Before launch, confirm the server max player setting is correct (one rollout broke at a wrong value). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Before any playtest, confirm the testing server is not public. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Give harvestable resources (e.g. trees) a despawn or respawn path so every server keeps supply. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Before soft launch, write expected LTV, retention and crash rate per market and device (T18794). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Check whether players will accept any plugin or generation tool before shipping with it (T18805). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Create a Discord server (and Roblox group) about a month before launch [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Link the Discord in the game description and in-game [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Ask permission before promoting in Discord servers [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Run a moderated community chat server linked to the game. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Set up a player bug-reporter community and cross-check Error Reports against player reports. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] File platform-level crash bugs with Roblox once crashes rise significantly. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Ask bug reporters for exact steps, extensions, frequency and situations. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Before beta, post screenshots, videos, or a livestream to start a community. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Set up a Discord ticket system for exploiter reports and unban requests. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Write a public statement of what staff can and cannot act on regarding exploiters. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] For Manual Approval groups, process JoinRequestPending via Open Cloud server-side. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Release free templates or resources to seed a Discord server. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Include the exact error, script, and line number when asking for help. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Turn off @everyone/@here and auto-delete staff-pinging messages. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Before shipping a live player-count sign, confirm MessagingService usage and the PrivateServerId key in current docs. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] After a stream, reply to chat and join viewers in their games to build supporter relationships. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Share finished builds, UI scripts, or tutorials on social platforms so people can find and follow you. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Add a social element and a solo/empty-server path. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Design the UI mobile-first with a single scaling UI. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Verify abilities, objectives and puzzles work and cannot be exploited. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] When taking camera control for a custom or cinematic view, set Camera.CameraType to Scriptable, and set it back to Custom when done (SimTek, T17883). [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Before shipping any pressure mechanic, write down its cost and what happens at zero. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Store room or content metadata in a ModuleScript table keyed by name, not hard-coded in logic. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Decide the target platforms up front: PC, mobile/tablet, console/gamepad, VR, Android TV. [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] Make touch controls usable: buttons easy to tap, UI readable, performance smooth on a phone. [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] Use Controller Emulator (with Device Simulator) to test gamepad, VR and TV remote input without hardware, and check Enum.GamepadType branches against the emulated type. [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] Test TV remote on hardware when possible; otherwise use Android TV emulator at 1920x1080 with Controller Emulator TV Remote. [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] Keep depth-of-field at graphics quality 8 or higher on non-mobile; check VR translation sensitivity (1-10) and camera sensitivity when tuning movement. [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] Choose camera configuration and supported input types (mouse, mobile, gamepad) early, since they affect accessibility and engagement. [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] Check the Access page or Studio Game Settings to confirm VR, Desktop, Mobile, Tablet and Console availability. [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] For VR, test default player scripts first before adding custom control code. [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] Put mobile controls in a 'Mobile Controls' ScreenGui and enable it only when TouchEnabled is true. [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] Keep max players at 100 or fewer if you need Voice Chat, and restart servers after lowering the limit. [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] Plan a release buffer for mobile and console client update lag. [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] Confirm your experience's current platform split in analytics instead of using the 2021 ~70% mobile / ~30% PC+Xbox figure. [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] Verify headset refresh rates in current device specs before tuning VR performance. [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] Test the experience on at least one low-end Android phone before launch. [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] Decide the launch platform list (PC, mobile, tablet, console) up front and map inputs for each. [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] If you run mobile ads to drive cross-platform installs, track installs and retention per platform to confirm the effect. [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] Put all persistent player data in one table keyed by UserId under one DataStore key. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Mark a profile as errored on load failure so defaults never overwrite real saves; tell the player. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Use a session lock (ProfileStore/ProfileService preferred) if the same player can load on more than one server. [data-persistence]
- [ ] In BindToClose, save all players in parallel and wait on a counter; budget is 30 s total. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Use OrderedDataStore for numeric leaderboards; page size up to 100. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Use MemoryStore only for temporary data, and set expiration on every write (max 45 days). [data-persistence]
- [ ] Treat throttle codes 301-306, KeyThrottled, DatastoreThrottled and HTTP 429 as back-off-and-retry. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Keep data store names fixed; use key prefixes instead of scopes. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Sanitize client input and reject inf, -inf and nan before writing. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Monitor DataStoreRequests by operation and status to catch throttling early. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Use ProfileStore/ProfileService for any player data that can load on more than one server; do not hand-roll unless you need to. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Convert UserId with tostring() for DataStore keys and tonumber() when reading ordered stores. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Load players already in the server at startup as well as on PlayerAdded. [data-persistence]
- [ ] In BindToClose save all players in parallel within 30 s, then wait about 3 s before shutdown. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Keep the autosave interval shorter than the session-lock expiry. [data-persistence]
- [ ] If you see 'data store request was added to queue', you are saving too often; reduce frequency and save only when data changed. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Monitor request budget (about 60 requests/min plus 10 per player, self-reported) and DataStore:Usage(). [data-persistence]
- [ ] Measure save size with JSONEncode length before optimizing; compress only as a last resort. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Use migrations that fail closed; never stamp or save a half-migrated profile. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Use ProfileStore:MessageAsync or queues for cross-server writes to a locked profile. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Use GetOrderedDataStore for leaderboards and MemoryStore when cross-server updates must be real-time. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Test DataStores in Studio with API access in a test version, then verify in a published build. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Put every saved field (type, default, min/max) in one template module. [data-persistence]
- [ ] For stateful objects (e.g. plants), save position, growth stage, mutation and state, and test a server change. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Before building a new save or session-lock layer, check whether Roblox's built-in transactions and session locking have shipped (targeted Early 2027). [data-persistence]
- [ ] For leaderboard reads of several entries, use OrderedDataStore BatchGet (shipped) and check each returned entry. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Do not write code against OrderedDataStore GetHistogramAsync until it appears in the official API reference (targeted Early 2027). [data-persistence]
- [ ] If you use reserved servers, write the reserved server ID to a DataStore keyed by session or party ID when it is created. [data-persistence]
- [ ] When a player rejoins a reserved server, read the stored reserved server ID by session or party ID before teleporting them. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Set the autosave interval to 3 seconds only for testing, and change it back before release. [data-persistence]
- [ ] After adding each saved system, test by leaving and rejoining to confirm the data persists. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Cap GetAsync calls at about 60 + 10 per player per minute, and cache loaded data in memory. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Keep writes to any single key at least 6 s apart across servers; coalesce changes first. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Use WaitForChild with about a 10 s timeout for folders created by other scripts. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Detect reserved servers via a non-empty PrivateServerId and PrivateServerOwnerId == 0 before using DataStores. [data-persistence]
- [ ] On reserved servers, key data by PrivateServerId, load once, cache, and save on BindToClose. [data-persistence]
- [ ] In Studio test configs, set save interval to 0 and saveOnClose to false. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Keep the save interval below the lock expiry window. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Pin DataStore module versions and review the changelog before major bumps. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Autosave every 1–2 minutes for larger games, in addition to save-on-leave and BindToClose. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Do not assume a purchase event means the perk is active on later joins; re-derive perks from saved or verified ownership at each join. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Persist shop inventory and refresh timers on the server, and confirm they survive leaving and rejoining. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Build map and UI before scripting systems [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Check performance continuously [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Gate risky updates behind config flags and roll out gradually [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Keep backup scripters and clear specs for hires [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Publish a place to the cloud early so the data model is saved and you can work from any computer. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Stage config changes in Studio, and use SetTestingValue to test on one server before going live. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Check the Creator Dashboard banner daily for reduced-exposure status; file a Bug Report with Universe ID if it looks wrong. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Use the Create alert shortcut from a Performance chart's overflow menu to pre-fill metric and filters. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Verify a memory-leak fix by checking the next update's crash chart. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Build performance best practices in from the start, not after publishing. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Sync scripts with Studio file sync in VS Code instead of adding Rojo. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Decide the access model before building paid content: public game, no private servers, copying disabled. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Verify RNG/luck outcomes and server-lock expiry before launch. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Enable Team Create, or recover lost work from File > Advanced > Open Autosaves. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] List every script system (map generation, ragdoll, UI) and every build, UI, VFX and model need so remaining work is visible. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Commit script drafts promptly and resolve conflicts with Merge From Server. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Group UI components into a storybook ModuleScript with storyRoots set to script. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] After a rough playtest, reserve about two weeks for bugs plus console and mobile support before wider launch. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Read and understand AI-generated code before shipping it. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Before depending on a Roblox API labelled alpha, test its behaviour in Studio and plan for bugs and changes. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Give interactive props their functional parts (axles, motors, scripts) in the scope estimate. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Track your roadmap on-time shipping rate; Roblox's reported benchmark is 79%. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Practise each service (TweenService, Instance.new, RemoteEvents) alone in a scratch baseplate until you can build it unaided. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] On mobile, add extra time for scripting and typing in Studio. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] When creating a new place from an existing game, duplicate the working scripts and change only the target place IDs. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Store shared templates in ReplicatedStorage and per-player GUI templates in ServerStorage; clone them at runtime. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Do scripting work on a computer with a real keyboard, not a phone. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] If a tutorial's model or file does not match your Studio version, use the version linked in its description (T17996 is from 2021; check it still matches the current Studio). [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] When planning roadmap dates, budget for a realistic miss rate; a platform-wide benchmark of about 79% on-time shipping (about 1 in 5 items late) is a reasonable starting assumption. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Write keyword-rich title/description matching real search phrasings [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] Add co-play features: invite prompts, private servers [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] Test your game on mobile, console and PC and fix device-specific issues. [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] Judge discovery performance at the 28-day mark, not after 7 days. [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] Audit the game profile page (description, thumbnail, media, icon) before launch, since profiles drive discovery (N7710). [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] Use a shared server progress bar with a visible percentage for cooperative objectives. [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] Document rotation and spawn timers and test them on a live server before quoting them to players. [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] Keep join/load short; stream assets, ReplicatedFirst loading screen [first-session-onboarding]
- [ ] Test on mobile, tablet, console and gamepad [first-session-onboarding]
- [ ] Fix bugs and onboarding before running ads [first-session-onboarding]
- [ ] Test that team switching by walking over spawns works on mobile and console controls. [first-session-onboarding]
- [ ] Confirm the game is simple enough for young mobile players [game-ideas-and-genres]
- [ ] Before picking a remake, search the Roblox catalog for existing copies of the mobile ad game (T18592). [game-ideas-and-genres]
- [ ] Limit scripting to one small system such as a revive system (T18647). [game-ideas-and-genres]
- [ ] Set up a creator server with assets, models and thumbnail renders [influencer-and-creator-marketing]
- [ ] Require at least 10 videos and about 500k combined views per vertical before judging a vertical's performance. [influencer-and-creator-marketing]
- [ ] Judge influencer spend across UA creative performance, store ranking, DAU and CPI, not just direct installs. [influencer-and-creator-marketing]
- [ ] Localize description and up to 10 thumbnails; keep the name [localization-and-international]
- [ ] Put --!strict on every script, or set the workspace type-check mode (Workspace.LuauTypeCheckMode) to strict. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Build shared types in a ModuleScript with export type and reference them as Types.Name. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Apply @native to specific hot server functions, annotate arguments, and avoid getfenv/setfenv and non-numeric math arguments. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Install Luau LSP, Selene linter, Roblox Lua Autocompletes and Roblox API Explorer in VSCode for IntelliSense and diagnostics. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Use the Script Editor's static lint and type checking and its Engine API signatures while coding. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Add --!native to a script only when the whole script is performance-critical; otherwise use @native on specific hot functions. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Iterate with for k, v in tbl when keys or array-ness do not matter; keep ipairs when stopping at the first nil is required. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Put all TeleportAsync calls in server Scripts; remove any client Teleport() calls and migrate them via RemoteEvents or prompts. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Generate reserved server IDs with HttpService:GenerateGUID(false) and store the ID to reroute players. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Test teleports by publishing and running in the Roblox client, not Studio playtesting. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Group players in five-player batches in a lobby, then teleport them once to a reserved server. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Create custom attributes (max 5 server, 5 player) before creating custom signals (max 2). [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Check Preview with 3 mock servers and hover scores before applying a config to live places. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Enable private servers for classes; rename them per group; use invite links or usernames (mobile must be invited directly). [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Use MessagingService (not DataStore polling) for friend notifications and real-time server browsers (refresh about 1 minute, max 20 servers). [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Revoke public access to fully shut down a game after restarting servers. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Elect one host server per game mode via a DataStore key before any queue confirms matches. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Notify players to rejoin if the host server re-hosts, since the queue is lost. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Wrap ReserveServer and GetPlayerPlaceInstanceAsync in pcall, as well as TeleportAsync. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Set exactly one of ShouldReserveServer, ReservedServerAccessCode or ServerInstanceId per TeleportOptions. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Place the PlayerArrivedFromTeleport handler in a ReplicatedFirst script. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Fire a RemoteEvent on touch to show a teleport GUI; use SetTeleportGui and GetArrivingTeleportGui on the client. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Confirm each queued player is still in the game before ReserveServer and TeleportToPrivateServer. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Reset the lobby between matches: return to the lobby spawn, clear the active players table, toggle the neutral spawn flag. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Use InitializeServerAttributesForStudio when testing custom matchmaking in Studio. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Before launch, confirm enough servers and players per region to avoid high-ping, empty lobbies (T16179, N6384). [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] For group PvE, create one queue or lobby per mission rather than drop-in servers (T16235). [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Use a bare queue server to gather players, then teleport the group when the threshold is reached (T16647). [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Consider a cross-server group search only after the simpler lobby and queue designs fail; plan for significant work (T16648). [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Before sizing a queue, party, or reserved-server group, confirm the game's configured max players per server (official reference figure: 10). [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Generate reserved-server IDs with HttpService:GenerateGUID(false) and reuse the same ID for every later teleport to that server. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] On arrival, check game.PrivateServerId ~= "" and game.PrivateServerOwnerId == 0 to detect a reserved server. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Set VipServerId on every teleport in a VIP session, and set it explicitly when players must not fall back to public servers. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Confirm each VIP-teleport target place belongs to the experience that owns the VIP server. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Store the origin VIP server ID in teleportData or a DataStore before teleporting to a reserved server. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Migrate every client-side Teleport() call to server TeleportAsync before setting Access Control to Secure. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] If the game supports console players, check the crossplatform setting before assuming a reserved server ID maps to a single server. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] When sizing party or lobby logic, confirm the per-server player cap (100 in the indie example) against the game's configured cap. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Record baseline FPS (Performance Summary), frame-time spikes (MicroProfiler), and memory (Developer Console) on a low-end client before changing anything. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Enable StreamingEnabled; set ModelStreamingBehavior Improved, StreamingIntegrityMode PauseOutsideLoadedArea, StreamOutBehavior Opportunistic if OOM persists. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Keep StreamingMinRadius 64 and StreamingTargetRadius 1024 by default; drop to 64 in testing. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Set ModelStreamingMode Atomic for scripted models, Persistent for always-needed content; flatten nested hierarchies. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Keep RenderFidelity on Automatic; use Precise for landmarks and Performance for decor. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Batch remotes; use Vector3int16, Int8-packed colours and axis-aligned CFrames where possible. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Spawn purely visual items on the client; keep server work to tiny authoritative blocks. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Set PhysicsSteppingMethod to Adaptive. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Keep MeshParts under 10,000 vertices, Scene Draw Count near 500, and budget mobile triangles. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Keep server memory under 50% using 6.25 GiB + 100 MiB × peak players. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Alert on ClientMemoryUsagePercentage and OOM exits per platform; investigate crash rates above 2–3%. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Run a 10–15 min mobile session to detect thermal FPS decline; re-test after each optimisation. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Ask Assistant whether a spike is physics-bound or script-bound and which category has the highest exclusive time. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] For server spikes, use the Developer Console server MicroProfiler (max 60 frames, 4 s delay); use counters mode for startup-to-dump data. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] On mobile, open the MicroProfiler web UI at the device IP:1338 (default 30 frames; add /90 for more). [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Fix one baseline low-end device (2014–2020 phone or laptop; 3.5 GB RAM threshold) and test every milestone on it. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Check frame time against 16.67 ms (60 FPS) and 33.33 ms (30 FPS); flag spikes, and aim above about 40 FPS on mobile. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Keep visible parts near about 5k in the render area; test at 9k to see where FPS falls. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Confirm Instance Streaming is on and streaming radii are at defaults (min 64, target 1024). [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Set ModelStreamingBehavior to Improved and PredictiveStreamingMode to Enabled, then re-test on low-end hardware. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Watch the Developer Console Memory tab and PlaceScriptMemory across sessions for growth with player interactions. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Keep replication under about 50 KB/s per client and budget per-player unit counts. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Check Open Cloud request volume against 2,500 per minute per server. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Set an explicit short DataStore/MemoryStore expiration instead of the 45-day default. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Run stress tests with many invited players before launch and check server headroom before adding about 50 more avatars. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Set a memory target: full game 400-500 MB; investigate near 1000 MB or when phones exceed 800-900 MB on 2 GB RAM devices. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Read Place memory (not Core memory) in Developer Console, and check texture and character memory at higher player counts. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Disconnect event connections and check that memory returns after a session or after leaving an area. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] When diagnosing mobile crashes, check memory usage before anything else. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Verify the FPS counter by setting Roblox Maximum frame rate to 120 or 144 and confirming it tracks the cap (uncapped was about 200-240 FPS in one test). [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Keep encoded audio short and module scripts small. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Enable streaming during development from the start, even if you disable it for release. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Only if native streaming cannot meet the need: add frame-time throttling, a priority queue, a minimum reparent count and a settings slider. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Count instances on the client during loading, not in Studio; aim for about 150,000 or fewer with streaming on. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Estimate client memory at about 500 bytes per instance (12,000 instances is roughly 6 MB). [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Reset Script Memory counters, play a session, and check for module counts that climb steadily. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Compare client and server instance counts to verify zone culling works. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Pair Instance Streaming with Texture Streaming and SLIM compositing for high-fidelity worlds (indie guidance). [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Test a distant streaming-enabled spawn island to reduce initial instance load. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Measure FPS during bulk spawns (for example 100 clones) to confirm the spawn cost is acceptable. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] If the game is a small linear space with discrete sections, consider an instant section swap at each transition point instead of gradual streaming. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Measure the frame-time spike during the section swap on a low-end device before shipping it. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Set Damping above 0 on springs to stop endless oscillation; set FreeLength to natural rest length (T8185, T9740, T9741). [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Build wheel suspension with CylindricalConstraint plus SpringConstraint, and set up constraints before writing vehicle scripts (T7891, T5263). [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Keep adaptive physics stepping; reserve fixed timestep for complex unstable mechanisms (T8266, T8049). [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Do distance and line-of-sight checks on the server (T8582, T8570). [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Playtest and hand-tune any AI-generated bot logic before shipping (T6630, Q1055). [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Load-test NPC counts at your target player count before shipping. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Copy Animate into a server Script for NPCs and set HumanoidRootPart Anchored to false. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Set cylindrical limits with upper -1 headroom; start at stiffness 3500, damping 200, free length 2.8. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] When testing ImprovedAnimationConstraint, pin AngularStrength at 1 and tune AngularDamping instead. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Turn on waypoint visualization while debugging NPC paths. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Insert the anti-lag script on NPC parts so the server keeps network ownership. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Run NPC pathfinding and AI decisions on the server. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Use IK Look At mode on the server to aim a part at a target. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] If writing custom suspension, include damping or the object never settles. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] For turrets or NPCs that track targets, apply rotateToward with a per-frame max angle instead of direct orientation snapping (T17795). [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Put NPC behaviour in one shared module and describe each NPC type with config values, not a separate script per NPC. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Gate FX client-side by player location and game state. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Before shipping a timed lethal trigger, confirm a visible warning of at least about 2 seconds exists before the kill. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Check that solo players can find a match; if not, label the mode as unsupported in the store description. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Run weekly team playtests with set focus areas and bug capture [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Use alt accounts or a ghost observer to watch unaware players [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Test on low-end phones, emulator sizes and console [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Have testers try to break the game and log exploits [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Fix bugs before prioritizing feedback [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Soft launch quietly and join servers on day one [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Triage bug reports by severity when public testing produces a flood [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] After each patch, observe higher-level players for exploit discovery. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Stress-test many players per server, long-lived servers and rejoins. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Watch the device picture during play; if a control does not highlight, the fault is upstream of input handling. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Evaluate the finished game against fun, challenging and bug-free before sharing. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Add in-game pop-ups that set expectations about bugs and unfinished features. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Use multi-client simulation, device emulation and collaborative playtesting in Studio before going public. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Make the bug-report template capture device info, tester identity and screenshots or console logs. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] During paid ads, join your own server and watch where players leave. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Run think-aloud sessions with your target age group using a fixed script. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Before a featured or front-page push, run a full save-and-reload test on a new account. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] In Team Tests, use only committed scripts; keep drafts in solo Studio sessions. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Run combat tests with several live players checking hitboxes, parries and lag. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Decide explicitly whether accidental behaviour is a bug to fix or a mechanic to keep. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Have friends playtest the rough prototype repeatedly across rounds, and fix each bug in turn. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Before investing in or funding a game, play it thoroughly yourself on desktop and mobile. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Reduce intermissions and walking; optimise performance for mobile [retention-d1-d7]
- [ ] Avoid modified clients or unofficial tools [roblox-platform-changes]
- [ ] Confirm the current status of 'trusted connections' on the Roblox developer announcements page before shipping any cross-age social feature. [roblox-platform-changes]
- [ ] After an unexplained post-release performance drop, check DevForum bug reports for a platform-side issue before changing code (T18604). [roblox-platform-changes]
- [ ] Key DataStores by UserId and never rename [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] pcall all DataStore calls [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Handle ProcessReceipt on server idempotently [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Add server cooldowns to remotes [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Keep unreleased code in ServerStorage [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Test on a real low-end Android [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Keep client memory under ~1.3 GB on 2 GB devices (F9) [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Enable StreamingEnabled [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Limit moving server assemblies to ~50 [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Consider server authority mode [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Generate remotes in code [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Put cosmetic popups and effects in a LocalScript, and cap spawn counts. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Use WaitForChild on every required module inside replicated scripts. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Call ConfigService and GetConfigAsync only from server scripts, inside pcall with fallbacks. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Enable instance streaming for headset builds. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Keep server CPU and client CPU each under 16 ms per frame. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Use MicroProfiler process groups and memory tags to locate expensive work. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Verify errors with F9, Script Profiler and Output before publishing. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Debounce every Touched handler with a flag or attribute cooldown. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Route all ProximityPrompt events through ProximityPromptService in one service script. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Track every connection, observer and zone in a Trove or Maid and destroy it on cleanup. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Wrap every DataStore, MemoryStore and MessagingService call in pcall and check the success flag. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Choose the smallest fixed-width type or buffer for high-frequency remote payloads. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Use workspace:GetServerTimeNow() for timers and simulation, not tick() or os.time(). [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Use CollectionService tags with GetInstanceAddedSignal handling, not duplicated scripts. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Set the server player cap and spawn buffer before launch; scale leaderboard values under the cap. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Add --!optimize 2 to new scripts; check Studio Output with a test run. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Remove loadstring, getfenv and setfenv from scripts that run in live or Store builds. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Merge remote fires into one remote per frame; use Packet or buffers for hot payloads. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Keep unreliable remotes for spam only and never batch them. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Create TweenInfo once; avoid server tweens for cosmetic motion. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Throttle client-to-server camera or look-vector sends with a dot threshold and minimum interval. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Benchmark Parallel Luau and thread recycling in MicroProfiler before adopting them. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Set Adaptive physics stepping and verify the frame-time change. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Use the Developer Console (F9) memory view and debug.setmemorycategory to find memory hogs. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Use Graph view to find what holds references keeping objects alive. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Time-budget bulk part loads and yield periodically to keep the server near 60 fps. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Grant hats with Humanoid:AddAccessory; clone from ServerStorage per player and check for duplicates. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Bind overlapping zones to EnterOnlyOneZoneAtATime and disconnect zone listeners on removal. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Run per-player atmosphere changes in a LocalScript. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Read the error's script path and line first, then check spelling and case. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Set Run Context to Client for scripts that must run inside a part. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Before writing a vehicle controller, check whether Roblox's pre-built physics behavior scripts (car, boat, drone, helicopter) cover the need. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Confirm navigation v2 has shipped before counting on its up-to-90% nav mesh memory reduction. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Generate any reserved server ID inside a server Script with HttpService:GenerateGUID(false). [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Confirm that no reserved server ID is generated, chosen, or derived from client input or client-side code. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Put camera code in a LocalScript under StarterPlayerScripts. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Clone accessory templates before AddAccessory so ServerStorage templates stay unchanged (already covered by existing per-player clone rule; confirm it is applied). [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Test teleports and reserved servers only on a published place, not in Studio. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Put grid snapping math in a ReplicatedStorage ModuleScript used by client and server. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Test each AI-generated system separately, then profile FPS after adding it. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Keep every ConnectParallel script inside an Actor before connecting to RunService events. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Learn Actors, the Microprofiler, and how threads map to CPU cores before adding parallel Luau. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Wrap MarketplaceService:UserOwnsGamePassAsync in pcall and warn on failure so an API error does not break the script. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Re-check memory growth and button logic after launch with real players; leaks and pointer bugs can appear only then. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Enable voice chat where server size allows [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Add leaderboards (native friend/global via ordered data store) [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Broadcast server events, level-ups and rare catches [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Add streamer mode, HUD hide and moderation tools [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Fix launch bugs before any viral push [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Use bots/new-player servers for first matches [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Read ReferredByPlayerId in PlayerAdded and fire a RemoteEvent to the client to credit the inviter. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Trigger CaptureService at challenge complete, rare item, or race finish; require consent before save or share. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Use Experiments in the FTUE to test matchmaking groupings if the game is social. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Check that the game has no per-server restrictions that split friends apart. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Test party flows with PartyId and SocialService:GetPartyAsync in Party Simulator using Server & Clients tests before publishing. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Decide the cross-server chat setting in Communication Settings based on whether the design needs isolated chat. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] For classroom games, disable voice chat and use private servers to create a closed garden. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Confirm your server size stays within the 100-user Voice Chat cap before relying on voice. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Announce troll-action buyers server-wide to convert griefing into retaliation play. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Plan a hidden ending or secret requirement that streamers can investigate publicly. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Check what a solo player sees when they join an empty server, and make sure friend features are visible. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Check whether the Friends-to-Connections rename has reached your target locales before shipping friend-list labels. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Add Experience Details Page share links to YouTube descriptions, X posts and creator briefs. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Replace flagged or privately restricted tracks with your own uploads, catalog tracks or Pro Sound Effects. [sound-and-music]
- [ ] Play weather sounds on the client only when the weather starts. [sound-and-music]
- [ ] Cache script:GetChildren() in a variable before random sound picks, not on every pick (T18107). [sound-and-music]
- [ ] Treat the underwater EQ tip as low-advice and test it on device and headphones before shipping (T18153). [sound-and-music]
- [ ] Consider selling only part of the game and keeping ownership [studio-business-funding-and-publishing]
- [ ] Run a 2-player Server and Clients session before any multiplayer change ships. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Check Script Analysis before running; treat command-bar errors on ModuleScripts as unverified until you run the game. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Wrap suspect code in debug.profilebegin/profileend and confirm the label in the MicroProfiler. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Run Network Simulator presets (Home Wi-Fi, Standard Mobile 4G, Bad Connection 3G) and a zero-value custom baseline for replication-heavy features. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Test on at least one low-end Android device and an Entry-level phone before release; run a 10-15 minute thermal session. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Publish the place, then enable Studio API access before testing DataStores; check Output for save errors. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Test purchases in a one-player server or Studio; do not expect Robux to be charged. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Keep debug cheats behind RunService:IsStudio() and PlaceId checks. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Have testers play and report bugs before publishing. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Keep client code in ReplicatedStorage so Output errors point to a stable path, not a per-player clone. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Playtest Assistant-generated code in Studio before keeping it. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Enable debugMode in Rewind.Server.Init to view rewound positions in the Iris panel when diagnosing hit registration. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Compile Luau with luau-compile -O2 -g2 to keep debug info for unused locals. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Enable Are Regions Enabled (Alt+S / Option+S) to see the prediction radius; enable Are Timesteps Shown to spot sleeping parts. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Subscribe to debug signals to monitor internal events, errors and network traffic while troubleshooting. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Guard Humanoid.Died with a boolean flag (canDie) if it fires more than once, so a kill counts once. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Test with task.spawn first to expose iteration bugs before switching to task.defer. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Preview animation blending with the real-time graph and parameter sliders before writing any script. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] For voice issues, inspect the Sound FMOD::Output::Mix and Voice CustomAudioMixer::Mix MicroProfiler tabs and the Sound/Voice memory counters. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Load-test with many concurrent players before release; watch for DataStore loss and server overload. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Profile mobile performance with the device MicroProfiler over the same Wi-Fi as your PC browser. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Enable Script Analysis and Output from the View tab before starting debugging. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Click errors in Script Analysis or Output to jump to the line; read the Stack Begin/End trace. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Remove Config.Tracking flags and hardcoded player lookups before release. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Keep in-game debug menus hidden by default and toggle with a key such as F2. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Fix collision and physics bugs before launch, not later. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Save generated unit tests as files so they run again on future changes. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Confirm real low-end hardware checks still happen before judging performance. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Check Asset Moderation feedback during development and fix flagged assets before publishing. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Check whether multiple client views and on-device testing are live in your Studio before relying on them. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Confirm save and rejoin behaviour works before adding new features that depend on saved data. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Before a live update, check announcements on a live server with a second client on a phone. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Show CFrame axes and the grid when debugging placement or rotation. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Add print or warn on every early return in server code. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] For purchase checks that behave oddly in Play mode, start a test server and retest. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] For badge features, confirm awards on the published game, or add a server print showing the award call fired, since Studio did not show awards. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Before publishing, run a short internal session and log every bug found, as janisjanis01 did (a 10-minute session surfaced many bugs). [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Complete title, description, schedule and thumbnails for off-platform featuring. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Add thumbnails and a description to the community group page. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] When testing icon, title or description, compare play-through rate from Home recommendations. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Preview asset thumbnails via right-click accessory > Save To Roblox… before uploading. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Save the final icon as PNG in a dedicated GFX folder so re-uploads are quick. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Keep a distinct title, not a near-copy of a popular game's name, so the game is not flagged as deceptive copy. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Use bold effects (e.g. electricity) around the large character, then check the result at mobile size. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Save reused images to disk, not the clipboard. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] When shipping an event or major update, update the icon in the same release. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Description under 1000 characters [titles-descriptions]
- [ ] No hashtags/tags in description [titles-descriptions]
- [ ] No control lists in description; use tutorial [titles-descriptions]
- [ ] Description all-ages appropriate [titles-descriptions]
- [ ] Localize title/description/thumbnail text for top countries [titles-descriptions]
- [ ] Update title/description/icon on major updates (sparingly) [titles-descriptions]
- [ ] Run the name and description through Studio's text filter before submitting. [titles-descriptions]
- [ ] Fill in the channel description before launch; it is a basic setup step (low-advice, single source). [titles-descriptions]
- [ ] Use emojis in the description only when they match gameplay, and keep the title within the existing emoji limit. [titles-descriptions]
- [ ] Confirm the brand or group theme is consistent across the title, description, icon and thumbnail before publishing. [titles-descriptions]
- [ ] When shipping an update, use promotional text in the game's metadata to announce it (T17623). Pair this with the existing bracketed freshness tag rule rather than replacing it. [titles-descriptions]
- [ ] Write a hook script and subtitles for each short [trailers-and-video-marketing]
- [ ] Add the game's Details Page URL to YouTube descriptions, X posts, and Discord announcements (T17662). [trailers-and-video-marketing]
- [ ] Check update cadence and bug complaints [trend-research-and-validation]
- [ ] Log CTR for each ad creative separately and compare it against a ~3% target; flag creatives near 2% for replacement. [trend-research-and-validation]
- [ ] Categorize honestly; report auto-categorization errors as bugs. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] Inspect free models for hidden scripts before insert; use RoDefender. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] Set MeshPart Material to Plastic, Transparency 0, VertexColor 1,1,1, and strip extra Scripts and Parts from the Model or Accessory. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] Replace legacy 'Face' and 'Head' AssetType filters with GetHeadShapesAsync, BodyPartDescription.HeadShape, and Roblox-creator Dynamic Head searches. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] Use Generated Accessory objects when you need Marketplace publishing, HumanoidDescription equipping, or Toolbox sharing. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] Audit any borrowed script for copied or decompiled source from other games before publishing. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] Set AutomaticSize and TextWrapped so localised text grows instead of clipping. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Keep thumbstick (bottom-left) and jump (bottom-right) zones clear on PC and mobile layouts. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Connect every button with Activated and put its handler in a LocalScript under the button. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Clamp bar ratios to 0-1 with UDim2.fromScale and tween changes instead of snapping. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Show exact ability numbers (hits, cooldown, scaling ratio) in descriptions. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Park FPS or debug labels in a screen corner, right-aligned and slightly down. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Keep Fusion UI in client scripts. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Compare the native blur against any existing BlurEffect used behind menus and keep whichever one meets your performance budget. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] When asking an AI assistant to change UI, name the exact script, frame or side to edit. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Hook HUD text and bars to the replicated value's .Changed event in a LocalScript instead of polling. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Use existing systems for cadence drops; save new systems for major updates [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Dedicate the first post-launch week to bug fixes and polish [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Set a crash-rate alert and a webhook to Discord, Slack or PagerDuty before each release. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] If metrics fall severely after an update, roll back, then profile with Developer Console, Script Profiler and MicroProfiler. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Rank bug and quality-of-life fixes by severity, effort and players affected. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Test every update before release and check errors and crash rate in the first minutes after publishing. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Broadcast events, updates and competition winners through announcement portals to all servers. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Select 'Restart only servers with outdated versions' unless the update is version-independent. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] If the player data format changes, restart all outdated servers. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Set 'Delay server restart' between 1 and 60 minutes so players can leave on their own. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Listen to DataModel.ServerRestartScheduled to show a countdown, prompt saves, or teleport players. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Create notification templates in the Creator Dashboard before scripting sends. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Run daily feedback checks and bug fixes for the first 7-14 days after release. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Ship critical bug fixes as same-day silent patches, not on the weekly cycle. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Plan restarts with a soft-shutdown script that moves players to a lobby and back via TeleportService. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] For large rebuilds, budget for specialist builders and a scripter. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Name expansions as numbered worlds and list new biome, pet or item counts in the description. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] For a lapsed-player push, bundle new characters, items, visuals and bug fixes into one update and send a notification. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Pick one source-of-truth model: Script Sync (code only), Rojo (whole project on disk), or Drafts Mode (in Studio). [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Install the Roblox Luau LSP VS Code extension and the Luau Language Server Companion plugin together. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Connect the Rojo Studio plugin on localhost:34872 after starting the server. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Enable Plugin Debugging before developing plugins; edit only the local-plugin copy. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Pass --verbose-style flags and capture stdout when debugging Studio from the command line. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Start the Rojo server from VS Code before pressing Connect in Studio. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Use GetPropertiesOfClass to check serialization before building save tooling. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Send .rbxlx as application/xml and .rbxl as application/octet-stream. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Add notes on every Save to Roblox (Ctrl+Alt+S / Cmd+Option+S). [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Regenerate the Luau LSP source map after package or instance changes instead of restarting all tools. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Verify a library's Wally name points to the maintained upstream, not a fork. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Connect the Studio MCP server when an external AI tool (Cursor, Claude Code) needs Studio access. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Add selene.toml to the project root and turn on StyLua format-on-save in your editor. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Enable Luau LSP auto-import in your editor so services and modules are suggested and require/GetService lines are written. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Split Wally dependencies into a shared section and a server-only section. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Add packages/, server packages, sourcemap.json to .gitignore; decide on wally.lock per team policy. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Save manually at regular intervals in Studio and do not treat autosave as a backup. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Commit to Git (or otherwise version the sources) at least daily so a crash cannot erase a full day of scripts or staging work. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Name each emitter exactly as the script expects, and set Enabled false if a script will trigger it. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] Keep Rate low: max 400/s desktop, 100/s mobile. Use Size, Speed and LightEmission for impact. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] Reuse particle textures across areas to save memory on low-end devices. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] Before emitting bursts, read SavedQualityLevel and scale Emit counts down on low settings. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] Test effects on low-end settings as well as high. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] If overlapping emitters on one part flicker, change ZOffset rather than moving the emitters. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] Send a RemoteEvent from the server to trigger confetti or celebration effects, and only fire it on the matching event (e.g. a pet hatch). [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] Add a texture-scrolling script to water parts to animate them. [vfx-and-particles]

## 4. First session and onboarding

### Must
- [ ] Limit each vertex to 4 influences; skip Root and HumanoidRootNode; enable Auto Normalize. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Build animation key poses first (neutral, anticipation, extreme, return), then add in-betweens and easing. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Keyframe upper limbs first, then child joints. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] For a targeted test, create the config first and set targeting (e.g. new players) [ab-testing-and-experiments]
- [ ] Order conditional rules so the first match is the intended one [ab-testing-and-experiments]
- [ ] Publish live value changes with GradualRollout (15 minutes) [ab-testing-and-experiments]
- [ ] Watch Playtime, ARPU and payer conversion during the first 24 hours (updates every 5 minutes) [ab-testing-and-experiments]
- [ ] Do not decide launch from the first 24 hours [ab-testing-and-experiments]

### Also consider
- [ ] If no progress after 3-5 days, improve onboarding/FTUE before spending more [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Check pacing and CCU within the first hour or two; lifetime budgets can burn out in hours. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Check delivery within about 30 minutes; zero impressions means investigate status. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Use Discord quest buys for existing games to target lapsed players, and track the reactivation split against new users. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Tell stakeholders the first influencer campaign is for learning. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Make the game public before running the first ad. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Launch the first campaign at 8 ad credits per day for 2 days with 2-3 thumbnails. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Step to 16 ad credits per day for 2 days only if the first campaign shows traction. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Log CTR and QPTR together for every campaign. If CTR is healthy (around 2%+) but QPTR is under ~1%, stop adding budget and diagnose the first session (T18915, N8124, N8125). [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Start with a small first campaign to get early stats, then tune the game before raising spend (N8079, N8126, T18925). [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] For third-party app submissions, prepare a public demo under 1 minute starting signed out, pick one category, and use HTTPS URLs of 256 characters or fewer. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Use Creator Hub for questionnaire appeals and moderation appeals; imported-model appeals resolve in up to 10 minutes. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Build an onboarding funnel with micro-steps and slice by device and source. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Check New User First Session Retention for onboarding issues. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Segment engagement by IsNewUser so new-player behaviour is not diluted by returning players (T9196). [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Ignore the last 3 minutes of chart data, which may be incomplete (T8600, N3496). [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Map linear games as ordered funnel steps: join, lobby, elevator, teleport, each checkpoint. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Before scaling ad spend, check day-1 retention, session time and percent still playing at 5 minutes. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Call AnalyticsService:LogOnboardingFunnelStepEvent with the same stepName strings in every call. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Log funnel step 1 on Players.PlayerAdded. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Verify funnel events fire by checking the Output window during testing. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Open Creator Hub Analytics > Funnels to see which step players drop at. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Track percent still playing at 5 minutes as an early first-session gate. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Ask a new player to join your game and watch their first minutes alongside the stats. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] When Journey analytics is available, rebuild multi-path funnels as branch-aware Sankey views to find drop-offs per branch (T17631). [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Use the 10–30 minute quit window only as a rough sanity check against your own session-length median, after discounting AFK time (N7840). [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Track visits, average session length (minutes) and like ratio side by side each day after launch. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Place badges at milestone and fail points to find where players drop off, then confirm with funnel events. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] When revenue events are sparse, set the ad optimisation event to a frequent action such as tutorial complete or CPA. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] On a movement violation, reset to last valid position first; log and increase the suspicion score (T9128, T9133). [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Rights holders: set revenue share, duration and visibility before first use [brand-deals-and-ip-licensing]
- [ ] When a clone or unlicensed adaptation appears, document it and file Rights Manager takedowns first; involve counsel before any lawsuit. [brand-deals-and-ip-licensing]
- [ ] Build terrain first, then zones and roads, then buildings, then props and decoration. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Time-box the starting area (about 6 minutes per terrain tool demo). [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Greybox the map with block and wedge parts first; check traversal, vantage points and scale before art. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Store zone atmosphere values as Attributes named exactly like the Atmosphere properties; tune in Lighting first. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Build TV UI at lower resolution first, then verify it inside TV-safe areas. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Parent background music to SoundService and check audio against 20 MB, 7 minutes and 48 kHz. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Polish the lobby and first-seen areas first, and give each stage or zone a distinct colour. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Pose from the torso outward: upper arms and thighs first, then hands and feet; set a mid-animation pose at 0.5. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Replace default Lighting and skybox first; add Skybox before Atmosphere. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Add particles and camera shake to explosions; budget about 15 minutes. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Apply new transparency or VFX to one object first and check it in-game before using it broadly (T18561, low-advice). [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Check mesh triangle counts before import and reduce polygons first if a mesh is very heavy (T18841, N8089). [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Keep the first-person view model local to the camera, not parented to the character (T4999). [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Restart shop restock timers on a fixed interval (300 s matches the 5-minute cycle in one example) with randomized stock per item. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Verify boss fights last more than a minute or two and that boss time-to-kill is not one-sided. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Keep tutorial NPCs out of the combat tag system so they cannot be damaged or targeted by accident. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Prototype a small, scoped first game [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Do not upload promotional content on the first day of a new social account (#7Phantom). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] List candidate features with a rough ROI estimate and do the cheap, high-return fixes first. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Tease with silhouettes and hints; post release-day messages [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Create a Discord and funnel players and followers into it before launch. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Ask new players if they need help and start chat conversations. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Focus program applications on domain expertise and check eligibility first. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Hold a 45-minute weekly sandbox session before each update. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Plan growth in order: community first, then ads for acquisition and reactivation, then social SDK and commerce for retention and spend. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Keep rarity weights and prices in a config module; roll from rarest first. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Playtest to measure average session length (aim above 10 minutes). [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Check drop-off per checkpoint/funnel step and fix the largest. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Watch real players for 5 minutes to find boring or frustrating moments. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] For every drained resource, measure time-to-empty against time-to-find and fix the gap if players run out first. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Choose the camera type (first-person, side-scrolling, isometric) to suit the gameplay. [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] Check that the entry flow, onboarding and key menus work in the Chrome web player. [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] Pass a default template so new players start with defined values, not nil. [data-persistence]
- [ ] For a new project with player data, install ProfileStore rather than ProfileService (verify the current package version first). [data-persistence]
- [ ] Judge viability in the first 1-2 weeks; shelve games with no traction [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Start in under an hour: sign up, play a few games, install Studio, take the onboarding tour, then build the 3D platformer tutorial. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Hold monetisation spend until you reach roughly 10 DAU; focus on D1 retention and playtime first. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] For a solo beginner, scope the first release to a 2-4 week MVP at 5-15 hours per week. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Build a playable within the first few days using Parts and Creator Store placeholders. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Set a time limit per build step and reserve at least 5 minutes for testing. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Publish every ten minutes or after any big change; use Alt+P (Option+P on Mac). [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Finish the official first-game or Baseplate tutorial before building custom systems. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Work at least 30-60 minutes a day on the project, not long sessions a few times a week. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Use 15-minute focused work blocks with 5-minute breaks. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Limit world-building for a first build to about a week (e.g. one house with a lawn), and expand later. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Reuse working systems from older projects for the first barebones prototype. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Before a significant feature, run an AI interrogation session (around 10 minutes, about 13 questions) and record each answer as a decision. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Turn on auto-publish from commits so each change can be tested on a phone within about a minute. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Follow tutorials with Studio open, then rebuild the same system without the video. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Plan about two months of daily practice before expecting a shippable first game. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Choose one platform for the first release; defer VR and console until you have experience. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Reserve the final minutes of a timed build for publishing. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Initialise version control (Git/GitHub) before the first real Studio session, and commit after each working feature. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Make the icon/title promise match the first 30-180 seconds [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] Polish tutorial and onboarding to lower first-play bounce [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] Aim for 10-15+ minute sessions; healthy, not addictive [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] Track PTR, first-play bounce, play days, playtime, spend, co-play days [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] Measure first-play bounce at 30, 60 and 90 seconds to see where players leave; target the official ~30-90 second window. [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] Check that your first-session experience works for brand-new players, since growth for new players is being measured. [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] Before spending on launch traffic, confirm the first session has a hook that holds early players (T17947). [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] Do not judge a new game in its first 72 hours. [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] Give battle pass tiers a free grind path alongside any paid skip option. [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] Before a big content drop, decide whether a free weekend fits; if yes, plan it with the update date and track new-player trials and cross-platform sales. [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] Spawn players facing the first objective [first-session-onboarding]
- [ ] Keep tutorial short, interactive, skippable or opt-in [first-session-onboarding]
- [ ] Teach one mechanic at a time; contextual tutorials later [first-session-onboarding]
- [ ] Fix the biggest drop-off step first [first-session-onboarding]
- [ ] A/B test onboarding variants with Experiments [first-session-onboarding]
- [ ] Group new players with new players [first-session-onboarding]
- [ ] Match first minute to thumbnail/title promise [first-session-onboarding]
- [ ] Add accessibility options (warnings, large text, repeatable tutorials) [first-session-onboarding]
- [ ] Track the age check as a separate step in the onboarding funnel so pre-game drop-off is not confused with in-game drop-off. [first-session-onboarding]
- [ ] Keep the first in-game objective and spawn fast after the age check, since the player has already spent up to about a minute on verification. [first-session-onboarding]
- [ ] Identify any first-session mechanic that can fail or punish the player, and show its rule in a safe context before the first penalty. [first-session-onboarding]
- [ ] For fail-on-miss or look-alike choices (such as fake vs. real doors), add a low-stakes practice instance or clear visual difference first. [first-session-onboarding]
- [ ] Place the opening room so its layout teaches the first action; avoid explaining it with text. [first-session-onboarding]
- [ ] Replace large tutorial pop-ups with a short intro and a persistent on-screen input prompt. [first-session-onboarding]
- [ ] Playtest the tutorial with the youngest target player, a six-year-old persona; if they cannot follow it, cut steps or simplify wording (Q1961, T18883). [first-session-onboarding]
- [ ] Store each tutorial step as its own data module so steps can be edited or reordered independently (T18883). [first-session-onboarding]
- [ ] Scope a first game finishable in days or weeks [game-ideas-and-genres]
- [ ] Scope the first build to about one week and a stud-style look (T18638). [game-ideas-and-genres]
- [ ] Ensure first boss teaches mechanics [genre-mechanics]
- [ ] Before modifying a read-only state value passed into an update function, table.clone it first. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Use priority queues so players who have waited longer are served first. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Start custom matchmaking from default signals; change only weights first. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Auto-clean debris after about 30 seconds and add cleanup for any player-driven fragmentation. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] If players can get stuck on the loading screen, provide a skip option as a fallback. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Offer a cheap first purchase to bring in new spenders (rasdev, 2026-10). [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Limit per-part feedback (such as bounce) to first interaction. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Watch at least a few brand-new players with no help [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Watch ad traffic players for about 5 minutes on day one [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Release risky updates to engaged players first [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Play your own game for long stretches as a new player [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Measure how long most players take per step, then set hint delay just above it and show each hint once [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Periodically play as a new player to counter developer blindness. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Have a naive player (child or relative) play 10 to 15 minutes; if confused or bored, simplify the concept. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Confirm new players can start, progress and keep their plots or progress after a restart. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Ask an experienced outside developer to play as a new player on a call and observe silently. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Have marketing or non-dev staff play the build and log funnel friction, screenshots and video. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Instrument D1, D7, D30 (and D8-28) by first-play cohort and acquisition source from day one [retention-d1-d7]
- [ ] Make first 30s and 5 min fun, low-grind, low-wait [retention-d1-d7]
- [ ] Find onboarding drop-off in First Session Retention curve and fix it [retention-d1-d7]
- [ ] End tutorial with a celebratory moment and a next-day hook [retention-d1-d7]
- [ ] Hit ~10+ minute average session before spending on ads [retention-d1-d7]
- [ ] Log average session length alongside D1 return; if sessions are ~30 minutes with no return, add return hooks earlier. [retention-d1-d7]
- [ ] Record where playtesters quit so you can compare against the 10 to 30 minute sampling window. [retention-d1-d7]
