- [ ] Configure RTBF deletion templates first; use webhooks only for complex erasure. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Use FindFirstChild with a conditional for optional objects. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Set pickup CanCollide to false and disconnect Touched after the first award. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Give dialogue typewriter text a click-to-finish skip. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Before animating procedurally generated parts, confirm they exist in Workspace (FindFirstChild or WaitForChild). [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Keep a table of parts already animating and skip duplicates; add a cooldown. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Check the error count when it spikes and fix the single line producing most errors first. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Build pathfinding from a known tutorial or pattern before adapting AI-generated code. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Set the favorite prompt using the place ID, with a 2-5 minute delay tuned to retention. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Post one short per day at launch. Add a second only after views grow, and space same-day posts 30+ minutes apart. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Keep Volume at about 2 or below for loud sources and lower it first when a sound is too loud. [sound-and-music]
- [ ] Launch 10+ ad creatives (up to platform limit) in first run [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Check CTR in Creator Hub; if below 3%, fix icon, title and thumbnail first. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Generate a colour palette first and use it as the baseline for thumbnails, icons and UI. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] If CTR is well under 1.5%, treat thumbnail and title as the first fix, then retest. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Do not ship an in-game screenshot as the icon without first checking it against the existing 'no raw screenshots as icons' rule. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Track click-to-rate against the 1.69% benchmark from one first campaign. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Keep FAQs short; cut Premium section first [titles-descriptions]
- [ ] Check that your group name is available early, since a first choice may already be taken. [titles-descriptions]
- [ ] Open with a 5-10s action hook or clear core experience in first seconds [trailers-and-video-marketing]
- [ ] Open Moments-targeted clips with your game's own visuals within the first seconds, because the feed now mixes game captures with influencer video. [trailers-and-video-marketing]
- [ ] Make the first seconds of Details Page and Moments clips show real gameplay, since Moments clicks lead straight to play (T17681). [trailers-and-video-marketing]
- [ ] Place the hook in the first two seconds of every Short. [trailers-and-video-marketing]
- [ ] Budget the animation upload (750 Robux) and item publish fee (1,500 Robux, Xaiyne 2025-08); verify the current amount first. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] Bind every InputAction for gamepad, keyboard/mouse and touch; use InputActionLabel for hints. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Before building custom hotkey hint text per device, check whether the Input Action Label has shipped and whether it covers your controls. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Route all on-screen control hints through one component keyed by action name so the hint source can be swapped later. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Skip UICorner on progress fills that can get very small; test the smallest fill width. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Enable click-to-skip on dialogue only after the typewriter finishes. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Confirm tutorial arrows do not overlap notifications or other HUD elements in testing. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Watch the first 48 hours after each update [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] After each update, compare D1, D7, session time, payer conversion, ARPPU, revenue, new users and play-through rate against baseline. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Deploy time-sensitive content behind a config and flip the value at release time; allow about 5 minutes for config deploys. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Allocate part of each content release to new-player onboarding. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Add a .gitignore that excludes .rbxlx before the first commit. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] If the Rojo plugin fails to connect, read the Studio Output window first. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Run git init at project start and make a first commit; decide explicitly whether the .rbxl is versioned (see disagreements). [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Keep emitters disabled and call Emit(count) for one-time bursts; test counts first with the Emit() Plugin from the Creator Marketplace. [vfx-and-particles]

## 5. Playtest and soft launch

### Must
- [ ] Graybox and playtest the layout before final art x2 [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Test UI in Studio Device Emulator with Actual Resolution across Phone, Tablet, Desktop and Console presets. x2 [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] If Early testing is live in your Creator Hub, invite up to 10 age-checked friends of similar ages before publishing. x2 [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Test in Pose mode with R, Alt+R and Clear User Transforms; play walk, run, idle from several angles. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Test each bone with R-rotation and Pose mode across several poses before exporting. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Test layered accessories in the AFT preview on sample characters before generating; use Cage Brush only for minor fixes. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] For dynamic heads: outer cage with left eye, right eye and mouth landmarks; 17+ FACS poses; pass all five facial tests. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Test graphs with AJU enabled; keep clips in ACE and use the Graph Editor only for logic. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]

### Also consider
- [ ] Test IK limbs when the character turns in world space and with held items. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Test every animation in-game after keyframe edits. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Make small Y-offset adjustments and re-test in-game after each change. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] For matchmaking tests, use 100% rollout, and only 1 may run per universe [ab-testing-and-experiments]
- [ ] Test title and thumbnail as pairs [ab-testing-and-experiments]
- [ ] Re-pull analytics a few days later before comparing thumbnail tests [ab-testing-and-experiments]
- [ ] Record a confidence level for each split test alongside the Roblox gates (full duration, SRM check, /stats after 24h). [ab-testing-and-experiments]
- [ ] For ad creatives, generate a matrix over duration and aspect ratio and test the grid. [ab-testing-and-experiments]
- [ ] Pick the objective: Maximize Plays for launch/testing [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Test search ads with relevant, high-volume keywords (max 10 per Ad Set) [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Start with a small test budget and a 5-7 day run; reassess by about 2 weeks. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Produce 20-50 new concepts per test cycle and size volume to monthly spend. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Test a new country with a small paid campaign before influencers. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Stress-test ROAS targets against a 30% CPM drop. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Do not pay the fee for 16+ horror, fighting or hangout games; reserve it for kid-oriented games needing younger testers. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Take heap snapshots only in Studio playtests (T6325). [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] During a beta, check the age mix against the target and aim for at least an 80% like ratio after closing. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Remove your own test purchases from developer product totals. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Roll back an update if retention and engagement worsen after release, then re-test. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Each cycle, generate an AI summary of in-game player feedback, then check the top themes against raw comments (T17612). [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] If you have Play Test Agent beta access, write test cases for core flows and run them before each release instead of replaying flows by hand (T17745). [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Where tracking is consent-limited, survey opted-in users and apply results to the cohort using one method across all channels. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Tune all anti-cheat thresholds per game and test against vehicles and custom physics. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Review how untrusted-Android players are handled once platform Hardware Attestation trust levels roll out (announced Late 2026). [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] At expiry, remove IP and licensed items from sale within 3 days and self-attest [brand-deals-and-ip-licensing]
- [ ] Anchor platforms and static geometry before playtest. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Playtest and check from several camera angles; rotate the camera during sculpting. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Test-mode after every stage from player eye level; press F and check the side view for floating buildings. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Keep jump gaps near 2 studs on jumpable or climbable platform paths, then playtest the route [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Test every enclosed structure with a player inside to find unintended survival spots [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Check that every joined stage has matching rotation values (for example 0 vs -180) before testing connections. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Test every UI in the device emulator [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Set Selectable on buttons and test controller navigation [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Test accessories on Classic, Rthro Normal and Rthro Slender bodies, with layered combinations and animations. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Test every auto-skinned accessory on several body types and animations; trim outer cages to the needed region (e.g. head only for beards). [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Try Edit-Time Procedural Models (File > Beta Features): write only into targetContainer, then clear Generator to bake before hand edits. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Tune viewmodel offset as a CFrame attribute live in playtest; set LocalTransparencyModifier to 1 on the real tool's parts. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Set ExposureCompensation to about -3 and Brightness to about 65 for daytime; then playtest sun rays and reduce their intensity. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Test accessory fit in Avatar > Accessory: scale until the box turns blue, then raise to clear the head. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Confirm the game still has gameplay feedback before accepting a low-budget art direction. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Iterate with GetTags() and guard against missing modules before creating components. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Keep utility modules in a separate folder from game modules and reuse tested ones. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Use a tested quaternion/rotation utility module for orientation problems instead of hand-written rotation math. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Set shop stock limits and a recovery path for money-loss soft-locks. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] When a tool won't equip, confirm it has a child named Handle; if still stuck, test the tool alone in StarterPack. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Test on a phone; keep buttons few and off the left movement side [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Test console and gamepad support at launch [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Schedule updates at players' peak hours and test them [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Click Stop at the end of every playtest; use Limited > Playtesters rather than Private (T15332, T13383). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Iterate backward when removing; use a pairs counter for dictionary sizes (T14569, T10590). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Use a ~300s cooldown in production and ~5s only for testing (T12396, N4698). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Playtest with a fresh player who has no briefing and add explanations for obvious mechanics (RoyalDev). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Before planning income from DevEx, check the latest announced end date for your region (US: mid-2027). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] After copy-pasting setup functions, check which function is called and confirm the prompt appears in a test run. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Before submitting to a contest or jam, confirm the place is Public, loads without errors, and was built new for that event. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] For contract work on another's game, quote a fixed fee and playtest thoroughly before signing. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Before testing eggs or planting, confirm every imported model (pets, crop models) is inserted into the game and matches its config (T18816, T18873). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Each seed or item in config needs a matching model; check this before testing to avoid breaking gameplay (T18873). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] After configuring drops or eggs, re-test them and check that placed objects are anchored (T18843). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Test point-to-point traversal and movement (e.g. hoverboards, levitation) before release (T18817). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Test data saving and loading before any holiday or major update (T18806). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Re-test after each fix before pushing it live (T18766, T18843). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Create announcement, suggestions, feedback and playtest channels [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Recruit 8-10 core playtesters [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Filter feedback for fit and retention [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Run community contests; credit/pay contributors [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Offer community-only perks, codes or beta access [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Respond to accessibility feedback from disabled players and fix issues on an ongoing basis. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Strip personal information from feedback before sharing it with the team. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Share a private playtest link with up to 10 testers aged 9+ before publishing. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Ask other developers to playtest and return serious feedback. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Ask viewers for feedback in comments to gauge interest before building a Discord. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Tag @username in comments to route feedback to specific people. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Before playtesting, set up a Discord thread, Google Form, or Slack channel that all testers can access. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Gate risky test modes behind community ranks for trained players. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Add an in-game prompt that asks players to join the group and send feedback through a defined channel. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Before running a community contest, decide the entry size or format limit and state the disqualification rule in the published rules. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Estimate contest review workload from past volume (for example ~500 entries led to a 50-60 shortlist) and schedule reviewer time accordingly. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Add instant feedback (sound, VFX, number pop-ups, orbs) on every core action. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Test automated actors against the allowlist to confirm they cannot change core systems. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Describe how each key button should feel before building it; a single press with no feedback is boring (MonarchDev, Q1832). [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Survey the community for cosmetic requests before release; deprioritise items they ignore. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] For any stat that scales off another object, test it with zero of that object present. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Add a return-to-base or equivalent shortcut during early playtests. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Adjust difficulty from playtester feedback, not assumption. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Test UI when the player switches input mid-session (Bluetooth controller connect, trackpad to mouse). [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] VR: configure OpenXR before launching Studio; configure only the headset under test. [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] VR: test each hand separately (disable headset and one controller); use Reset Positions when stuck. [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] Test visuals with Editor Quality Level set to both lowest and highest. [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] Enable Studio Access to API Services before testing; verify final behavior in a published build. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Give test builds their own store index so they never share live storage. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Soft-launch with small ad spend; watch players for 7-14 days [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Use Git and staging; test before pushing updates [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Triage feedback by severity, breadth and cost [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Use AI step by step with playtests between prompts [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Test each mechanic with a print or a Play run before building on top of it. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Put tunable values in one GameSettings module with rough starting values, then tune in playtests. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Finish one phase (layout, test map, code, polish) before starting the next. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Playtest after each small step, not only at the end. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Publish the game before inviting friends or playtesters. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Break each feature into numbered steps before building it, and test each step in isolation. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Before showing testers, check fun, challenge and error-free; keep pre-feedback fixes to small single changes. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Plan a 7-30 day beta and track D1 and D30 retention before the wider release. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Test risky systems in a small standalone place before building the full map. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Install UI Labs to iterate on UI without launching the game. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Cut mechanics that testing shows hurt controls or tempo. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Enter a game jam or hack week to test a concept's fun quickly. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Playtest your build during the work session, not only at scheduled reviews. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Label any early release as beta on the store page and state what is missing. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Confirm traction before raising money or spending on UA; keep soft-launch spend focused on learning. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Test title and thumbnail pairs for CTR, comparing clicks per impression. [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] Build an admin command to trigger events for testing [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] Test an unconventional themed event with players before committing the full build (T18506). [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] Add immediate feedback to every action [first-session-onboarding]
- [ ] Playtest with non-gamers and kids without instruction [first-session-onboarding]
- [ ] If checkpoints exist, disable each one after it is touched or allow forward-only progress, then playtest backtracking. [first-session-onboarding]
- [ ] For a competitive shooter, test controls, hitbox forgiveness and matchmaking on touchscreen before tuning for PC or console. Cite T17477. [genre-mechanics]
- [ ] If the obby includes swings or ropes, playtest momentum, release timing and landing before release. [genre-mechanics]
- [ ] When using procedural level assembly, playtest multiple generated outputs, not just one seed. [genre-mechanics]
- [ ] Keep always-on campaigns in core countries; test new markets [influencer-and-creator-marketing]
- [ ] Enable ATC and run Studio playtests to capture strings [localization-and-international]
- [ ] Test with Player Emulator Pseudolocalize/Elongate and per-locale play [localization-and-international]
- [ ] Test new markets with paid UA before scaling [localization-and-international]
- [ ] Set --!optimize deliberately (1 in Studio testing, 2 in live games by default). [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Never insert into a table while iterating it; iterate array events in reverse when listeners change mid-fire. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Test teleport behavior in a published game; TeleportService does not work in Studio playtests. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Anchor static parts; check Awake parts visualization in playtest to find moving assemblies. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Test particle-heavy effects for emission rate, lifetime and speed; avoid high emission with long lifetime and low speed. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Test SLIM assets for Z-fighting, corroded metal and UV issues, and confirm with a Highlight in Workspace. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Include a maximum-inventory test account in load-in and load-time tests. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Test vehicles with multiple players, since constraints may not replicate. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Tune shortest-distance (example 300) to set chase range. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Test doors closing on NPCs and confirm the module unsticks them. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Raise volume on high-priority feedback (e.g., Volume 3 on collect) rather than on routine sounds like jumps. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Run the Xbox test on gameplay footage before publishing. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Start camera at smoothing about 16 and FOV 60; change one value at a time to an extreme, play-test, then settle. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Keep bob amplitude small and test it with breaks, since over-attention exaggerates bob. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] If pets give a speed multiplier, test it against the casual-versus-tryhard tier rule. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Log deaths and session-end points in playtests and use them to adjust difficulty. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Expose throw boost as a tunable value and playtest throw distance with it. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Review monetization feedback from players for signs of feeling manipulated and adjust before retention drops. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Build a rough playable prototype and test it early [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Test solo and in groups separately [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Playtest as average, casual and engaged personas [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Recruit testers from Discord, QA Central and fan groups [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Use a separate published test place [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Wait for repeated feedback before acting [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Add a feedback board or in-game mailbox [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Check the creator dashboard Feedback tab [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Test colours in grayscale and with colourblind players [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Test every update, and avoid last-second updates [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Recruit disabled and varied testers and confirm controls and UI with them [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Run small group playtests with friends, family or team to check comprehension and fun [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Announce scheduled private test sessions in advance on Discord and YouTube [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Run a weekend or one-week limited public test to measure D1 retention and session length, then remove the game [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Offer temporary paid closed-beta access to your most engaged users and label it as a beta [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Check AI feedback summaries against raw feedback before setting priorities [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Compare analytics to KPI targets, change, retest, and repeat until targets are met [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Invite friends or outside testers to play before release, and reserve a day of fixes per round. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Run a short daily survey asking motivation on a 1-7 scale; plot it to find dips. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Set sportsmanship rules before group playtests. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Playtest graybox: can players move without getting stuck, is size right, any floating or misaligned parts. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Test VFX in the real game flow, not only the viewport. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Test classic clothing on a Block Avatar Rig in Studio before uploading. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] For obby checks, use Test or Test Here, not Run, so your avatar is inserted and jump distances can be checked. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] After each change, playtest again; a config error can block progress entirely. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Self-play every number and the full flow before inviting any testers. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Otherwise use the Invite early testers flow so others can preview the unpublished game. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Start with a closed access beta via a Roblox group with role-based access. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Before any open beta, confirm the game is playable end to end on all target platforms. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Before any open beta, assign people to handle feedback and tag the title Beta. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Let testers set severity, and do not discourage duplicate reports. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Retest after every fix and do not release until a full pass shows no critical issues. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] After each release, check update ratings for dislike spikes and read Create > Analytics feedback. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Word in-game surveys neutrally and have someone clean the responses. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Drop enemies or NPCs off cliffs and onto terrain to test physics breakage. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Log each suggestion with the observed problem it solves; implement only those that fix one. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Give art feedback with reference images of the target style. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Verify the camera does not occlude enemy attacks during playtests. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Start NPC test runs at roughly 50 agents per batch and review results before scaling up. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Revise NPC test prompts after each batch based on what the results show. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Before invites, limit early testing to about 10 age-checked friends of similar ages. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] When a test build behaves unexpectedly, play it through and note whether it is fun before filing a fix. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Use NPCs to run pre-release sessions that check the game flow before real testers join. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Schedule a time-limited demo between test rounds so players can try the game without a full release. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Run a full end-to-end playtest and log every dead-end, unmarked fail state and gauntlet that feels too long. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Before a large playtest, set a maximum group size for any lobby teleport and test that the cap holds. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] After a survival or resource playtest, check hunger/drain rates and resource spawn counts, then adjust and retest before release. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] For contests or community submissions, define a fixed rubric before judging and score every entry against it. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Recruit a 80-100 person NDA'd super-fan panel before a feature preview, and collect qualitative feedback on the preview. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] When playtest feedback points to a problem, draw a logic tree of causes before choosing a fix. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] After building each new stage or hazard, playtest it and lower hazard speeds if it cannot be cleared. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Before scaling ad spend or headcount, run a soft launch on one channel with a few thousand users and check for engagement. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Compare play days per month for linked vs non-linked players to test the claimed 1.5-day lift in your own game. [retention-d1-d7]
- [ ] Test multiplayer runs with a full party (3 to 4 players) to see real run length. [retention-d1-d7]
- [ ] Test TextChatService before May 2027 [roblox-platform-changes]
- [ ] Enable required Studio Beta Features (Video Uploads, CCL) [roblox-platform-changes]
- [ ] Test visuals/lighting at low graphics settings [roblox-platform-changes]
- [ ] Test with 60 ms latency 10 ms jitter [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Check fast movers with sub-step position tests, not just frame endpoints. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Order error rules by priority, test regex, and keep under 100 custom rules. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Enable Workspace PlayerCharacterDestroyBehavior and test cleanup on PlayerRemoving. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Test on a midrange Android tablet, not just a gaming desktop. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Move drop tables, crafting odds and pricing into pure modules that take data as arguments, then add command-line unit tests. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] For large NPC counts, benchmark CCL v2 against Humanoid on target phones if the beta is available in your Studio build. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Label CCL v2 and navigation v2 figures as beta or speaker-stated when citing them. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Design clippable moments and test them [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Join dev Discords, feedback groups, and playtest exchanges to find interested players. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] For private playtests, invite up to 10 friends via link; playtesters must be 9+ with a Roblox account. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Test the invite prompt against the 10-second auto-dismiss timeout and the re-invite cooldown. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Set up Studio's Share button > Copy Link to send the game to testers and friends. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Test YouTube against TikTok per item or event before choosing where to spend promo budget. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Test the web-player join path from a link on a clean browser before promoting it. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Set up a Discord or community hub for feedback and friend-finding, and link it from the game with age-gated buttons. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Check Roblox rules on giveaways and prizes before running any contest (see gaps). [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] If you want player-made promo videos, set up a contest with written rules in Discord, a fixed entry window, and a Robux prize. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Add a feedback sound to every player interaction. [sound-and-music]
- [ ] Test effect chain order; wire several players through one effect instance. [sound-and-music]
- [ ] Playtest on device speakers and headphones. [sound-and-music]
- [ ] Keep loud climax sources at Volume about 2 or below, then test on device speakers and headphones. [sound-and-music]
- [ ] Run a small test ad (about 1,000 Robux) to check daily revenue [studio-business-funding-and-publishing]
- [ ] Test clothing in Studio before paying for Avatar Shop upload [studio-business-funding-and-publishing]
- [ ] Identify your title's revenue stage (stable earner ~couple hundred thousand per year; enterprise seven to eight figures per year) before choosing a testing process. [studio-business-funding-and-publishing]
- [ ] Once a title is stably earning, set up structured A/B tests instead of ad-hoc changes. [studio-business-funding-and-publishing]
- [ ] Before each playtest, clear Output (Ctrl+K / Cmd+K) and enable Show Source and Show Timestamp. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Playtest with Test or Test Here (not Run) when an avatar or interaction is needed. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Stop the playtest with Stop or Shift+F5; press 1 to regain mouse control. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Test an exported accessory with the Accessory Fitting Tool and equip it to a humanoid before publishing. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Playtest a character by renaming its Model to StarterCharacter and moving it into StarterPlayer. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Playtest a vehicle after building it; stop the playtest with Shift+F5. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Test the uncopylocked testing place file for quick access to test environments. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Test cross-sold items in a private copy with Studio's free purchase feature; collaborators use a separate copy with test IDs. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Before moving to the new library, test slopes, ladders, swimming and MoveTo thoroughly. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Test bot erasure handling with a test POST carrying a correctly HMAC-SHA256-signed footer before going live. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Append :Mock() to ProfileStore:LoadProfileAsync for test sessions; remove it before publish. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Playtest with real people rather than relying only on AI bots. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Use UI Labs with a *.story.lua Vide story to iterate on UI components. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Automate regression tests for core multiplayer features and re-run them after each change. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] If you run nightly automated iterations, make every test automated so the nightly run covers it. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] After an AI-generated edit, playtest death and respawn, then read Output for errors. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Test gamepass gating with the real owned ID and with a fake (non-owned) ID. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Test two-player mechanics on live Roblox with a second account. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Before spending on ads, complete a full-game playtest with several players and record results. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] After adding or editing quest, dialog or button text, playtest and confirm no text overflows its button. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] After configuring egg odds or multipliers, open a test batch (about 8 eggs) and verify multipliers and pet spawns. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Run a $25 minimum test ad to measure CTR [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Change one element per test; identical budget and duration [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Keep the winner and test the next element [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Test 2–3 new thumbnails a week [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Test entirely new concepts too [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Use high-resolution images and get community feedback [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] At each major update, test several new thumbnails, then leave them unchanged until the next update. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Test the Shorts thumbnail after posting and swap if it underperforms. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Prepare two thumbnails plus one icon for each ad A/B test; the icon can double as a candidate. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Set a test budget of about $20 per icon or thumbnail variant before scaling spend. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Test a new thumbnail with a new ad creative, since the thumbnail cannot be swapped on a live ad. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Compare any AI-generated thumbnail against your own baseline with a real test ad before adopting it. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Run a half-second glance test on any AI-assisted icon before committing it to A/B tests. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Even when icon and thumbnail share a composition, still A/B test them separately. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Run a front-page test: put each candidate icon beside current trending icons and check it stands out. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Cut out the subject, add a soft shadow on the canvas only, and avoid stacking effects. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] A/B test 2–3 text colours and pick the one that stands out most. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Run 5 icons, 5 screenshots and 5 feature banner tests each month. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Write a curiosity-prompting icon concept and compare it against a generic flashy version in an A/B test. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Log the best thumbnail CTR and the ad creative CTR side by side for every test. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Test 2+ name candidates with copies/ads [titles-descriptions]
- [ ] Test the title with someone unfamiliar with the game: can they state what they will do within about half a second? [titles-descriptions]
- [ ] Use one freshness bracket such as [NEW] or [BETA] during active update windows only, and remove it afterward. [titles-descriptions]
- [ ] Test an odd but iconic name against a literal one next to the icon, then keep the one that stands out and is still clear. [titles-descriptions]
- [ ] Upload trailer and mechanic footage before the Today's Picks survey; nominate per notable update [trailers-and-video-marketing]
- [ ] Pick editing software: CapCut, DaVinci Resolve, or Premiere/After Effects [trailers-and-video-marketing]
- [ ] Budget a small ad test (10k-30k Robux) [trend-research-and-validation]
- [ ] Test a before/after style icon adapted from YouTube thumbnail formats against your current icon. [trend-research-and-validation]
- [ ] Heads: include all 17 FACS base poses; test dynamic heads. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] Equip test clothing and accessories in Studio under varied lighting and skin tones; use the Accessory Fitting Tool. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] Check contrast and add a stroke or background behind white or dark text; test over busy 3D scenes. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Test UI scale on phone, tablet and monitor sizes early, before building further layers (about 5 hours were lost fixing scale late). [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Test any new native text shadow or gradient on the smallest target device and in the lowest graphics setting used by your players. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] After an AI assistant edits UI, ask it to verify its own changes and then test in Studio. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Use experiments to test feature versions on player groups before full rollout. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Confirm Team Create membership and playtest access before converting to 17+. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Post the game publicly early to gather feedback, then target the next update at the critiques. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Trigger timed events (egg raids, weather, dark hour) from an admin panel to test engagement spikes without republishing. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Use branches (production, alpha, beta) to isolate test code. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Import openapi.json into Swagger or Postman to test Open Cloud calls. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Test each effect at the lowest and highest Editor Quality Level. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] Playtest after each property change and keep iterating. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] For each key moment, add a visible payoff (for example thrusters on takeoff) and playtest whether it lands. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] Tune the noise multiplier by playtesting; the source gives no value. [vfx-and-particles]

## 6. Packaging: icon, thumbnails, title, description

### Must
- [ ] Keep one icon/config template for pets and duplicate it for each new pet. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Use a pre-post dashboard comparison when traffic is too low for an A/B [ab-testing-and-experiments]
- [ ] Prepare 2+ thumbnails/creatives and compare CTR (target ~2.5-3%+, min 1%) [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Finalise thumbnails and icons before launch; cancel and restart to change creatives on an active ad. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Measure CTR (below 1% suggests a weak thumbnail) and play-through (plays divided by impressions, aim 1.5-2%+). [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Use a proven thumbnail, with a cutout figure and play button for new variants. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Compare O18-segment day-7 retention and Robux per user against the all-player baseline before scaling spend. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Review trailers, thumbnails, merch and promo art for age-appropriateness, not only in-game content. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]

### Also consider
- [ ] Segment retention by Platform, Country, AcquisitionSource and payer status, not just blended D1/D7/D30 (T9191). [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Track average playtime per stage: 10+ min during ads, 20+ once on the algorithm. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Tag every creator-reported figure as self-reported in the knowledge file (N7791–N7794, N7799, N7811, N7840, N7841). [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Tag every creator post as giveaway or non-giveaway before reading uplift. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Tag every player-created instance with the creator's UserId at spawn time. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Add an icon, symbol or outline wherever colour carries meaning. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Show button icons matching the player's device (InputActionLabel or GetImageForKeyCode). [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Size textures to the stud area (256 for 5x5, 512 for 10x10, 1024 for 20x20) and compress images before upload. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Export Figma icons as white PNG and copy gradients and corners into UIGradient and UICorner. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] For thumbnails, place the model on a green background in Studio to cut it out later, and remove the name tag (DisplayName none). [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] For growing crops, pivot at the base (Model > Edit Pivot), name stages STAGE_1, STAGE_2, and drive colour from one growth parameter. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Use only move and rotate tools when posing renders and cameras for thumbnails or promos (T18425). [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Model click-to-use consumables and key interactables in 3D instead of using plain icons (T18862). [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Use Canva to recreate existing game logos as an icon-design portfolio piece (T18780). [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Send deltas, changed items and arrays, not whole tables; pack frequent data or use buffers (T5127, T5728, T6261). [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] When a stage changes, shut down the old handler before initialising the new one. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] On CharacterAdded, remove mode tags and re-add the default tag. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] On hit, clear old creator tags, then tag the victim with an ObjectValue pointing to the attacker before TakeDamage. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Expire combat and kill tags with Debris:AddItem instead of tables. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Use a custom thumbnail [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Check that each title delivers on its promise; cut long, boring intros. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Tweet ~10 days before events with a hashtag and date/time [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Before adding group-member tags, confirm Player:IsInGroup behavior and set tag tiers with an if/elseif chain. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Limit hashtags to a few relevant ones per post, such as #RobloxDev or #RobloxUGC; avoid tag spam. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Shorten padded segments; don't restart the whole section after one miss. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] After editing any drop table or egg weight, sample real drops and compare them to the configured percentages. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Compare the planned count of icons, models, or areas against your usual per-update output before committing to scope. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Record build progress during work so you have montage footage for social posts without a separate filming session. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Upload a custom thumbnail (multiple distinct variants) [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] Add gameplay video previews [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] Pull play-through rate (RFY icon impressions to Play presses) weekly; no threshold is published, so track the trend. [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] Make passes and dev products presentable with clean names, icons and value statements in case they surface before entry. [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] Create the event with the Events and Updates API (title, start/end, visibility) [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] Make sure the title and thumbnail explain the game [game-ideas-and-genres]
- [ ] Scale PvE difficulty per stage [genre-mechanics]
- [ ] Tag NPCs and detectables via CollectionService [genre-mechanics]
- [ ] Start pre-launch marketing on YouTube/TikTok/Instagram [influencer-and-creator-marketing]
- [ ] Outsource editing/thumbnails for your own channel [influencer-and-creator-marketing]
- [ ] Ask for raw or multiple cut lengths (e.g., short vertical clips) so the footage can be repurposed across ad placements. [influencer-and-creator-marketing]
- [ ] Tag influencer traffic in internal analytics and compare retention and spend against other media sources. [influencer-and-creator-marketing]
- [ ] Use TextLabels, not text in images; use icons where possible [localization-and-international]
- [ ] Segment paying conversion and ARPU by region (for example US vs China) before changing monetization for a market. [localization-and-international]
- [ ] Before cloning many items (around 100 or more), batch or stagger the clones across frames rather than spawning all at once. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Make the icon and title promise something the gameplay already delivers. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Tie carried cash to a speed penalty and add a periodic percentage deduction to keep money moving. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] On on-rails or fixed-track shooting segments, limit camera turn range to roughly 100 degrees. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Before adding a paid item, check whether it gives any gameplay advantage; if it does, assume players will call it pay-to-win and reconsider or tier it. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Run A/B experiments on risky changes with a control [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Use conditional configs in experiments to compare outcomes across player segments [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Check map layout for team advantage, orientation, choice overload and bypassable areas. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Tag lapsed players (no login for a set window) and run a win-back flow; track their return rate and spend separately. [retention-d1-d7]
- [ ] Tag accessories with CollectionService so new hats need only a tag. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Pair GetInstanceAddedSignal with GetTagged and filter to Workspace. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Build a hashtag and puzzle teasers before launch [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Register capture recommendation items with tags and attributes as soon as they are created. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Cross-post devlog clips to Instagram Reels as well as TikTok. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Compute investment percentage as investment / total worth [studio-business-funding-and-publishing]
- [ ] Below enterprise scale, keep experimentation lightweight; reserve dedicated experimentation infrastructure for seven-to-eight-figure titles. [studio-business-funding-and-publishing]
- [ ] Make thumbnails at 1920x1080 with a single clear focal subject [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Keep icon and thumbnails thematically consistent [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Enable thumbnail personalization with 3–5 active thumbnails [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Check impressions and QPTR per thumbnail weekly [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Days 2–4 after launch, replace low-CTR thumbnails [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Refresh thumbnail/icon with updates; max once a week; revert on dips [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Set ad winner as main thumbnail [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Add thumbnail camera to asset models [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Export thumbnails at 1920×1080 (16:9), under 3 MB, with no reliance on auto-crop. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Keep titles, logos and key subjects off the bottom edge where player count covers them. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Use bold, large fonts and check the thumbnail on both light and dark themes. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Preview the game icon at about 150×150 px to confirm details stay clear. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Upload a square 512×512 game icon; supply one icon per locale. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Keep 2–5 personalization thumbnails active; never run with one. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Upload a real, current gameplay video to the Videos tab; keep edits to camera work, light post-processing, logo overlays and highlights. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Track the 3-per-month video thumbnail quota and pre-check footage before uploading. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Create a unique event thumbnail that differs from experience thumbnails. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Keep badge, pass and developer product icons at 512×512 with key content inside the circle. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Keep UI icons consistent and minimise text. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Allow time for icon moderation before the planned launch or update. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Shrink the icon to about 150×150, look away for 2 seconds, then write the game and its feel in one sentence. Redo it if you cannot. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Remove raw in-game screenshots and AI-generated art from the icon; use a composed image. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Capture the thumbnail scene in Studio with UI hidden at a larger size, then upscale and polish in Photoshop or Photopea. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] For premise thumbnails, link two contrasting subjects with an arrow. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Make at least two thumbnail variants per game and compare them. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Compare ThumbnailQualifiedPTR and ThumbnailImpressions per ThumbnailAsset and export before the 30-day window closes. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] For sponsored ads, supply a 16:9 thumbnail alongside the icon. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Change the game name in GameSettings so the in-game billboard matches the unique title. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Grade the thumbnail on focal point, contrast, intrigue, simplicity and uniqueness before release. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Make a group banner for the group page, designed to match the icon and thumbnail. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Before a Marketplace asset is published, create its custom thumbnail with the Custom Thumbnail Tool plugin. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Confirm the accessory Handle covers the entire item before generating its thumbnail. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Export asset thumbnails at 150x150 and 420x420, the supported Asset-type sizes. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Make only small, controlled icon changes so existing players still recognise the game. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Upload a new game icon via Creations > your game > Configure > Game Icon; Roblox reviews it and approves after a short wait if appropriate. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Check targeting and campaign settings before replacing a thumbnail that underperforms. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Before publishing or re-skinning a thumbnail, compare your title and thumbnail side by side with the games you used as references and confirm no name, logo, or signature art is copied. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Keep the AI thumbnail prompt short: reference image, game name, 'same look, my content swapped in'. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] If reframing a 16:9 thumbnail into an icon with AI, output a 512×512 square and verify it at 150×150. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Try the ThumbMaker plugin from the Toolbox for marketplace thumbnails and preview before saving. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Log which thumbnails were AI-assisted so their CTR can be compared against human-made ones. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Lay out the icon and wide thumbnail from one shared composition, then export at 512×512 (icon) and 1920×1080 (thumbnail). [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] If the icon uses a game location, check that the subject still reads clearly as a single focal point at 150×150. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Place bold game-name text on the icon and verify legibility at 150×150 before uploading. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Make the icon before the game: create 3–5 icon/title variants and pick a front-runner before building. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Score each icon on stands out, looks good, and simple. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Render icon source at 1000×1000 with a transparent background, then downscale to 512×512 for upload. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Check the final icon at 150×150 before upload. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Hide the name tag (DisplayDistanceType None) and set camera FieldOfView to about 30 for avatar shots. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Export item icons as PNG at quality 100 and upload via Creations > Decals; verify the current menu path. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Check that no icon or thumbnail content breaks Roblox rules. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Put the pre-blend item shot as the main visual of the thumbnail. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Before scaling a high-CTR thumbnail, confirm retention and player metrics are healthy. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Title readable at ~3rd-grade level, easy to spell [titles-descriptions]
- [ ] 0-2 emojis max in title [titles-descriptions]
- [ ] Check keyword competition/visits in Creator Xchange [titles-descriptions]
- [ ] Ask friends to pick most clickable title [titles-descriptions]
- [ ] Title and thumbnail together convey core fantasy [titles-descriptions]
- [ ] Keywords natural, no keyword lists [titles-descriptions]
- [ ] Before building, draft the title, icon, and a couple of thumbnails so marketing is planned from the start. [titles-descriptions]
- [ ] Give songs a meaningful title and set the Song Artist field to credit composers; a blank field defaults to your name. [titles-descriptions]
- [ ] Use trending hashtags such as the game name on videos (single anecdotal source; effect unproven). [titles-descriptions]
- [ ] Before transferring a game to a new owner, confirm the title will stay exactly the same. [titles-descriptions]
- [ ] Run a name check on the game title before launch, not after copycats appear (T17623). [titles-descriptions]
- [ ] If you need custom character art for a title or thumbnail, budget for commissions. One indie creator (self-reported, low-advice) paid about $70 plus an $80 tip for an anime-style character portrait. [titles-descriptions]
- [ ] Pair the game icon with a memorable, distinctive title. [titles-descriptions]
- [ ] Run a name check on the title before committing to it. [titles-descriptions]
- [ ] Remove voiceover, narration, promo text, lyrics music, real-life footage from Roblox page videos [trailers-and-video-marketing]
- [ ] Cut external trailers to 60-90s with on-beat music and end title card with CTA [trailers-and-video-marketing]
- [ ] Reuse the style guide across trailers, thumbnails, and Shorts to keep brand consistent. [trailers-and-video-marketing]
- [ ] Pick one YouTube series format for the build (for example, a numbered build log) and keep the same title pattern so viewers can follow it. [trailers-and-video-marketing]
- [ ] When a popular title declines, check whether the underlying mechanic is rising in other titles before writing it off. [trend-research-and-validation]
- [ ] Add relevant emote tags at publish time. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] Add UIAspectRatioConstraint (or RelativeYY) to every icon, button and square image. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Pair colour cues with icons or shapes for colour-blind players. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Use TextScaled labels with transparent backgrounds for floating overhead tags. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Icons: use an ImageLabel with AnchorPoint 0.5,0.5, scale-based size, BackgroundTransparency 1, the image set to the asset ID, and a UIAspectRatioConstraint. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Before release, audit every icon for all state variants (normal, darkened, disabled, owned). [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Show the contents of eggs and containers on their icons before opening. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] For UGC items, limit post-publish changes to fixes (fit, cages, small mesh or texture, thumbnail). [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Set the game icon and thumbnail before running ads. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Have a Discord status post ready for outages: post during downtime and confirm the return. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Publish reusable plugins with a 512x512 thumbnail. [version-control-and-tooling]

## 7. Monetisation setup

### Must
- [ ] Make productType match the configuration block [ab-testing-and-experiments]
- [ ] Use earned Robux (better rate) or ad credits; choose payment method before publishing [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Calculate Robux returned against Robux spent on every campaign. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Write win criteria before launch: Robux beat, returning players, and ad payback at day 7. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Decide product: Sponsored Items for Marketplace items; Ads Manager or the API for experiences. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Budget in ad credits (1 credit = 285 Robux) and convert all spend to Robux per play before judging. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences] **[AUDIT 2026-11-01 OUTDATED-UNSURE: reported 2024 rate; other reports say about 250 Robux per credit; Creator Docs state no fixed rate, check the live conversion (Creator Docs ads-manager, fetched 2026-11-01)]**
- [ ] Before buying credits, confirm game passes or dev products work and day-1 retention is healthy. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Track CTR, cost per play and joins per click, plus Robux returned, not just spend. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]

### Also consider
- [ ] Record cost per activation for every campaign and compare it with Robux return before scaling. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Check that the Robux spent is matched by retained players, not just CCU spikes (N8079). [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Expect loss of Limited per-unit fees and Robux from removed infringing items. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Never give out your password; report anyone who asks for it. Distrust free-Robux offers. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Before public publishing, confirm ID verification, a qualifying purchase since Jan 1, 2025, or a completed DevEx request in the 12 months ending Dec 10, 2025. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Remove simulated gambling (virtual chips, simulated betting); keep bingo, arcade-style luck and paid random item generators only if they follow the rules. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Review rare-item loss mechanics and Robux-spend prompts in any experience that may reach under-13 players. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Audit every feature (chat, social, purchases, user-built content) against the age groups your rating reaches via estimated age. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Define engagement, retention and monetization KPIs before development. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Use My Transactions for true income and exclude your own purchases. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Attribute promo traffic via the Traffic Sources and monetization tabs (T6241). [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Track 18+ earnings as a separate line and watch its share of Robux. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] If conversion is poor, check price against the demographic actually attracted before blaming the game. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Convert gross Robux to net (e.g. ×0.7 or current DevEx rate) before comparing to ad spend. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Track ARRPPU and PTR alongside ARPPU when reviewing monetisation and discovery. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Store a timestamped public API snapshot (players now, lifetime visits, favorites, votes) every reporting day. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Compute monetization per engaged hour for each age bracket (under-13 vs 18+). [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Build a payer histogram by spend bucket and compare it to current price points. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Track price, volume, equip rate and spend depth for each gacha or item SKU. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Keep a raw event copy you own and reconcile it against MMP and store dashboards. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Never share your password; treat off-platform links and free-Robux offers as scams. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Name the game passes and developer products that will use the IP [brand-deals-and-ip-licensing]
- [ ] Respond to any offer within 14 days (accept, dispute or mark changes done) [brand-deals-and-ip-licensing]
- [ ] When proposing a brand integration, consider offering the brand a character or NPC role inside the game, not only a logo or banner placement. [brand-deals-and-ip-licensing]
- [ ] Offer UI scaling and a contrast slider [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Put ScreenGui objects in StarterGui; they are cloned into PlayerGui for each player on join. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Filter Toolbox by the Roblox account when sourcing starter models, then vet them. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Throttle directional sends with a dot-product check and a trailing delayed send. [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Key gamepass ownership checks on player.UserId, not player.Name. [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Store per-item data in a table and write one lookup function; expose only the changing values in the settings module. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Wrap raw data-store access in a manager module with helpers such as GetData, GiveMoney and GetItems. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Store common permission combinations as shared ReplicatedStorage modules. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] When you have many entries of one type, store them in one table keyed by name and add new types by adding an entry. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Use StarterPack/StarterGear/ReplicatedStorage+Clone for tool distribution. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Store NPC state as attributes on the character model; keep the attribute count under 64 per Instance. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Reset per-character stats on CharacterAdded and clear stored data on PlayerRemoving. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Store item level requirements in one dictionary keyed by item name, sourced from leaderstats. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Verify purchases via receipts, never prompt-finished events [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Use MarketplaceService for prices [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Avoid P2W; lower overpriced items after launch [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Limit monetisation methods [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Keep movement and camera free of purchases; gate only optional content behind age checks (T12472, T13336). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Use a session-locked data library such as ProfileStore in production (T12157). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Replace or hide every shop button that does nothing before release (Cumin). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Report monetization per hour, split by age cohort, next to DAUs and hours. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Offer value before asking top devs for help [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Post commission listings with service, price, and DM contact. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Run a Discord poll before committing to monetisation or mission design. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Offer accessibility options (volume, keybinds, shake slider). [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Tune quests/dailies to fit within a session; no Robux-gated tasks. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Offer a free sample of each content type before any a la carte paywall. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Use UpdateAsync for production writes; reserve SetAsync for demos or pure overwrites. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Fix the production store/ProfileStore name before launch and never change it afterwards. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Store positions as integers (x1000, rounded) and flatten arrays before adding more encoding. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Store binary data with Buffer:ToBase64(), not buffer.tostring output. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Store CFrame as position and look-vector numbers, and rebuild with CFrame.new(pos, pos + look). [data-persistence]
- [ ] Change store names only for a deliberate wipe, after confirming with the user. [data-persistence]
- [ ] On Players.PlayerAdded, check ownership of each gamepass and grant its perks, because the purchase event will not fire for existing owners. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Trace each shop or pack read to its authoritative data source when an item looks stuck refreshing. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Write a light GDD (concept, features, monetisation, controls, UI, roles) [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Set milestones with buffer (solo ~2x estimates; 6 productive hours per day) [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Set up a board with columns for concept, systems, monetisation, building/art and paid costs for each game. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Run projected Robux earnings through the DevEx calculator before starting a long monetised build. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Build a monetised side project to show in job applications. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Store Open Cloud keys on disk, not in chat, and set standing instructions for the agent. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Publish the place before creating any gamepass. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Track spend days and Robux per user alongside session quality. [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] Set launch and monetisation timelines with a buffer beyond announced roadmap dates. [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] Pair seasonal gamepasses with a capped set of limited pets or items to make the offer concrete. [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] Before announcing any Robux prize pool, confirm it is funded and check current platform rules on Robux giveaways. [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] Don't prompt favorites/purchases too early [first-session-onboarding]
- [ ] Offer a concrete perk such as free admin [influencer-and-creator-marketing]
- [ ] Use SocialSeed: verify account, filter price, check views/reviews/past videos, send offer with due date [influencer-and-creator-marketing]
- [ ] Budget fair rates; avoid lowball offers [influencer-and-creator-marketing]
- [ ] Call restoreDefaults (or reset config to default) before applying module settings. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] If a collectible is meant to be scarce, use a timed map-wide spawn window rather than a permanent shop. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Add confetti and a sound effect to shop purchases. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Verify group-gated access, shop and price behaviour [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Add timed events, rare drops and limited shop restocks [retention-d1-d7]
- [ ] Verify 2FA, age verification and Premium/fee for all-ages release [roblox-platform-changes]
- [ ] Call RankProductsAsync once at join and cache for the session. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Store transferRequestId from PromptRobuxTransferAsync and correlate with receipts. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Check MembershipType before Premium logic; debounce the Premium modal (about 5 s). [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Use task.defer or task.spawn, store the thread, and task.cancel unneeded work; avoid spawn(). [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Throttle AI sight to ~1 s intervals and check FOV with a dot product before raycasting. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Store original Position values before moving parts so they can reset. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Set the prize size against your budget. The only example in the source is 10,000 Robux (indie, self-reported). [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Use the Creator Store's free-to-use audio or your own imports; check each asset's terms. [sound-and-music]
- [ ] When the camera enters water, lower an EqualizerSoundEffect's high gain; restore it on exit (T18153). [sound-and-music]
- [ ] Define monetization and deal structure [studio-business-funding-and-publishing]
- [ ] Use groups for separate studios (limit 100 for Premium) [studio-business-funding-and-publishing]
- [ ] If distribution is your weak point, compare publisher offers against self-publishing, and review the revenue split and rights terms before signing. [studio-business-funding-and-publishing]
- [ ] In Studio, remove the pass from your inventory to reach the non-owner purchase prompt. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Add a comment-inviting or avatar-item CTA to Moments clips where it fits, since comments and avatar item purchases are enabled from videos (Roblox, September 2026). [trailers-and-video-marketing]
- [ ] Start trailer production earlier than other promo work (T17806). [trailers-and-video-marketing]
- [ ] Check App Store and Google Play charts [trend-research-and-validation]
- [ ] For UGC: Marketplace Best Selling with a price filter [trend-research-and-validation]
- [ ] Confirm account eligibility: Plus/Premium for layered or rigid accessory listing; 17+ and ID-verified for Today's Picks. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] Budget the publishing advance for each non-free item; free Limited costs 100 Robux per unit. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] Buy the Avatar Creation Token with Robux before in-experience creation; match token type to asset type. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] Emote: 80 Robux fee, Avatar Item content type, Emote category, Generate asset ID. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] Enable Distribute on Creator Store to allow copies; keep dependencies under 15,000. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] Offer customizable skin tones for human-like avatars. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] Display UGC items in your game and prompt in-game purchases to earn commission. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] Plan a try-on experience with an avatar loader to show outfits before purchase. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] Before publishing, estimate the listing fee (profile-only ~80 Robux vs Marketplace ~1,000 to 1,500 Robux, self-reported) and confirm it in the Creator Hub. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] Label any Robux-to-USD conversion as an estimate derived from creator-reported figures, not a platform rate. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] Blur the camera on menu open, store the default blur value and restore it on close. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Hide the buy button once the player owns the product or all tiers. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Give each main-flow button a clear label (Play, Select Mode, Shop). [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] When several shops or vendors serve the same purpose, consider one central hub and add a sign or map pin to it. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Put item name in ProximityPrompt ActionText and price in ObjectText. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] For a shop, add a toggle button for the ScreenGui and a hidden product template per item. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Wire purchase prompts to a visible button, not to a timer or an automatic trigger. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Check the game pass or purchase entry point is large and easy to find in the shop. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Plan monetisation six weeks ahead [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Use FixedTime bundles for holiday or month-long offers that end at a set UTC time. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Replace legacy Open Cloud API calls with current Open Cloud endpoints for production. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Store WALLY_AUTH_TOKEN as a GitHub secret. [version-control-and-tooling]

## 8. Community and pre-launch marketing

### Must
- [ ] For eyelashes and eyebrows: RBX_Leader/RBX_Follower at 1.0 weight, Custom Properties and Only Deform Bones on, Maya Skins on. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] For group-owned games, check revenue under the group. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Confirm influencer attribution method (link, code, or deep link) before paying. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] If the channel is TikTok, budget Spark Ads on top of the creator fee. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Keep influencer spend steady rather than cutting it abruptly. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Review all avatar, asset, group and game content against Community Standards and Marketplace policy before upload. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Confirm TTS text and STT audio meet Community Standards and Terms of Use. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] As group owner, review group audit logs regularly and act on harmful behavior. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]

### Also consider
- [ ] For group games, confirm the owner meets the Plus requirement and that authorized members have 2FA. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Group errors by MessageHash before pulling log details. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Set a minimum 7-day click window on all creator and influencer tracking links. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Add separate sliders for music, effects and speech using sound groups. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Keep gameplay trailers real: every shown part must exist in the game. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Make Ctrl+D (duplicate in place) and Ctrl+G (group) default habits; use single-axis moves with Collisions on. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Import OBJ with 'split by group' in Blender to delete body parts individually. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Group new map assets into folders, then scatter trees and rocks with the Brush tool to fill areas quickly (T18755). [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Group new assets into folders before placing them in the map (T18755). [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Require grouped modules with ./ and ../ paths so the folder can move without path rewrites. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Declare ability conflicts with Blocks/Stops/Suspends/ExclusiveGroup and Consumes. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Announce every update on social, Discord and group [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Send group/community notifications on updates [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Be active in Discord yourself [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Budget real effort to grow Discord membership; it does not come automatically. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Name the Roblox group after your studio and add YouTube, Discord, or X links to the community page. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Keep at least one verified 16+ collaborator on group- or community-owned games to avoid 90-day link deletion. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Check community merch requests before ordering merchandise. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Recruit community translators to review automatic translations. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Check group audit logs regularly for guideline-breaking collaborator activity. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Route Roblox notifications to a Discord or Slack webhook for your team. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Use GroupService:PromptJoinAsync to prompt in-game players to join the group. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Check follower and group spikes for bot accounts before celebrating. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Ask specific questions in comments or Discord about what to add or fix next. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Track conversion from booths and community posts before investing more time in them. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Join Roblox dev Discords such as Hidden Devs or RoDevs to find work and collaborators. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Budget dev time for community management before launch. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Sync Roblox group rank to Discord roles via a verification bot. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Organise Discord categories per game and add a management bot. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Post sneak peeks every few days; reserve pushed announcements for major updates. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Host any community wiki on wiki.gg, not Fandom. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Have a written plan for handing the community to fans if you stop. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Keep promoting between content drops so lurking Discord members have a reason to return. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Before a reveal, post a blurred screenshot in Discord and ask members to guess the content. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] When a Discord member's concept art ships in the game, credit the artist by name in-game or in the Discord announcement. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Keep a simple record of which community submissions were used and who made them, so attribution is consistent. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Before a deadline, list content items and group them into fixed batches; build one batch at a time and drop the batch that will not fit. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Seed with friends, YouTubers or small spread-out ads (2-4 weeks) [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] Do not announce unshipped events [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] Use a group event within a seasonal update to pull players back for return visits. [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] Find YouTuber business emails [influencer-and-creator-marketing]
- [ ] Group creators by vertical; need ~10 videos and ~500k views before judging [influencer-and-creator-marketing]
- [ ] Set setSeekingPermissions so only allowed places, users or group ranks can trigger event timing. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Set group-content minimums explicitly; a 15-player campaign needs different queueing than a 4-player PvE group (N6407, N6554). [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Use NoCollisionConstraint for a specific pair such as wheel and body instead of broad collision groups (T8443, T8444). [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Put trains in a separate collision group from scenery. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Add social account linking (e.g. Discord social SDK) and compare D28 retention for linked vs non-linked players. [retention-d1-d7]
- [ ] Check DevForum announcement threads for each feature you depend on [roblox-platform-changes]
- [ ] Group related config values into one JSON config and add them ahead of time. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Set collision groups so doors and NPCs do not collide with NPCs. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Post short TikToks/Shorts regularly during and after development [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Announce updates on Twitter/Discord [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Add follow-event touch part and group-join prompt [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Make cosmetics visible to other players during group play. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] If a new TikTok account gets zero views, repost proven older content from an established account. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Create at least one mode that attracts a distinct sub-community, such as speedrunning, build or roleplay. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Add a Discord social SDK so players can link accounts and invite Discord friends, after checking its current official docs and cost. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Track how many players reach the game through Discord friends and compare it with other invite channels before counting it as a discovery source. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Compare your D28 retention for players who linked Discord against those who did not, using your own data. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Retweet, reply to and ask questions of your Twitter followers each week (T18473). [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Post devlogs on Twitter to show progress and attract followers (T18478). [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] With zero ad budget, post clips on TikTok and YouTube Shorts before spending on paid promotion (T18712, Q1928). [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Track concurrent players; when CCU nears 1,000, have a playable build and outreach list ready for YouTubers (T18720, N8026). [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Use AudioFader for group control and AudioAnalyzer to inspect levels. [sound-and-music]
- [ ] Replace legacy SoundGroup mixing with Audio object equivalents when you touch an old project. [sound-and-music]
- [ ] If educational, apply to the Community Fund and book Education Open Office Hours [studio-business-funding-and-publishing]
- [ ] Avoid permanent group removals to protect ownership privileges [studio-business-funding-and-publishing]
