- [ ] Re-upload any old YouTube-embedded videos as files [trailers-and-video-marketing]
- [ ] Post short-form clips on TikTok/Shorts early in development [trailers-and-video-marketing]
- [ ] Announce on Roblox and Twitter, not only Discord [trailers-and-video-marketing]
- [ ] Prioritize game quality before heavy trailer spend [trailers-and-video-marketing]
- [ ] Check whether Details Page videos are showing in Moments now that the rollout has passed its announced start (T17681). [trailers-and-video-marketing]
- [ ] Ask creators for trailer or promo videos 1-2 weeks before launch (T17806). [trailers-and-video-marketing]
- [ ] Produce lo-fi developer diary clips for TikTok-style organic short form (T17951, Q1839). [trailers-and-video-marketing]
- [ ] Scroll TikTok, Shorts and Reels for trends and verify their cause [trend-research-and-validation]
- [ ] Select the correct group before publishing; publish as an Avatar Item, not a Developer Item, so it reaches the marketplace. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] Announce a Limited drop only if you will release it as stated. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] Before running paid media on UGC, decide whether the content is commissioned from creators or is organic influencer posting, and treat them as separate plans. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] Fade groups by tweening a CanvasGroup's GroupTransparency rather than each child. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Build hype on Wednesday; announce the date in advance [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Announce in Discord, Twitter, group, in-game bulletins and with influencers [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Write each announcement with specific new content, within 60 characters, and no more than one every 3 days. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Announce events in advance and promote both externally and in-game (login pop-ups, UI alerts, lobby). [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Announce schedule delays promptly with a reason and a new date. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] After a group transfer is accepted, check permissions and release the game publicly again. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Give at most one breaking change per week and announce it a day ahead. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Build a backlog that mixes community requests with your own roadmap items. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Add selene (lint) and StyLua (format) to external projects; verify community tools before installing. [version-control-and-tooling]

## 9. Compliance and platform requirements

### Must
- [ ] Start from the official R15 Rig_and_Attachments or Combined template and the body cage template. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Confirm 15 body-part meshes, 19 attachments and one cage per body part before skinning. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Name inner/outer cages to exactly match the mesh name; never delete cage vertices. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Fit accessories in the Accessory Fitting Tool and check across several bodies and animations before generating. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Start from Roblox's official templates and reference cages, and use Classic, Rthro Normal or Rthro Slender mannequins for proportions. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] For layered accessories: name cages _InnerCage and _OuterCage, keep outer cages just outside the mesh, and never delete cage vertices. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Give every pet at least a basic animation; triage complex rigs with simple rules such as legs-or-wings. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Treat about 80% confidence with no negative ratings or retention signals as a candidate for rollout only after the existing gates pass. [ab-testing-and-experiments]

### Also consider
- [ ] Check eligibility: 13+ account, ad account, payment method [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Disclose ads clearly; check AreAdsAllowed per player [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Register in-experience ad campaigns 5+ days ahead and submit assets for moderation [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Check the live Ads Manager UI for current settings; guides go stale [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Confirm the universe is eligible with campaign-options before creating any campaign. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Stop or pause the campaign once the engaged-player target is reached. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Upload creatives as Open Cloud image assets and reference them in creativeAssetIds (up to 10 per campaign). [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Set in-experience ad fallback images and avoid clickable placements that send players away. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Confirm the account is 13+ and verified before creating ads. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] In Ads Manager, pick the destination experience before submitting, since ads cannot be edited after. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Vet agencies with case studies before engaging them. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Send chat with TextChannel:SendAsync() and use TextChannels so messages stay reportable and visible to moderators. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Before any messaging request, check CanUserChatAsync, CanUsersChatAsync or CanUsersDirectChatAsync. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Call SetDirectChatRequester() on every TextChannel created for direct chat. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Age-check users (Facial Age Estimation or ID verification) and match similar ages before enabling dynamic or directed-conversation presets. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Keep gameplay-only, confirmation and basic-reaction presets available without an age gate. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Confirm every avatar asset and outfit has finished moderation (AvatarAssetModerationCompleted, AvatarOutfitModerationCompleted) before use in-experience. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Report non-IP avatar violations via Report Abuse on the catalog page, a support ticket, or the DSA form for EU residents. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Appeal revocations on the appeals page, incorrect moderation decisions with moderation staff, and IP-related removals via the IP infringement notice form. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Before publishing to 16+ or all-ages, complete age check and enable 2-step verification; re-verify on every publish. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Confirm the account is at least 2 days old before 16+ publishing. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Complete the Content & Maturity questionnaire accurately at the most mature content level; fix mismatches before evaluation. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] If targeting 17+, verify owner and filler 17+ and age-verify all collaborators 17+. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Design chat assuming monitored and filtered; default under-9 chat off and 9-12 on with filtering. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Gate cross-age chat and Trusted Friend adds behind age checks and parental approval for under-13 and under-18 users. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Keep ban messages free of usernames, handles and direct links; brand or platform names only. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Keep moderation history in good standing to preserve directory eligibility and publishing-fee refunds. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Before paying a publishing fee, confirm the current amount and refund terms in Creator Hub. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Do not retain age-check or ID selfies in your own storage. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Check the age rating of any external service you link to, since most are 13+. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Route all in-game chat through filtered TextChannels, and do not assume guest or special-guest chat is filtered. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Screen AI-generated and user-supplied images for moderation risk before publishing. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Confirm the chat eligibility rules for age-checked versus unverified users in official docs before designing chat-dependent features. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] If you are under 18 and verifying, check whether Facial Age Estimation is available as an alternative to ID verification. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Before publishing 16+ or all-ages, confirm the age check and 2-step verification are complete. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] If your experience has no restricted content and targets 16+ in most regions, check whether the single-question questionnaire path applies. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] If you have any restricted content, complete the full Experience Guidelines questionnaire at the most mature level. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Add in-game nudges or timeouts for disruptive players to supplement platform moderation. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Keep a record of mechanic changes and moderation actions in case a design-change request or appeal follows. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Track average session time, DAU, MAU and DAU/MAU. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Use 28-day overlays and 7-day moving averages; check seasonality. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Review Ads Manager at day 30 and Creator Analytics for retention impact. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Keep ad budget alerts at 80% and 100% in view; lowering budget below month-to-date usage throttles the service (T8150). [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Check the Anti-cheat page estimates by trust level before enforcing a policy (T8622). [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Check Learn & Explore eligibility: like ratio above 50% and at least 15 CCU (N3123, N3124). [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Track highly engaged players (500 in 60 days to qualify; below 100 loses eligibility). [analytics-metrics] **[AUDIT 2026-11-01 OUTDATED-UNSURE: superseded: Kids/Select entry bar is 250 HEP in 60 days (cut from 500 on 2026-08-19); maintenance bar is 25 (cut from 100 on 2026-07-16); entry drops again to 100 in Nov 2026: CONFIRMED as announced by RDC26 staff post (2026-09-11) and 2026-10-12 publishing post, NOT live as of 2026-11-01 (docs still say 250) (Creator Docs kids-and-select + DevForum 'Highly Engaged Player Threshold Drops to 250' (staff, 2026-08-19), fetched 2026-11-01)]**
- [ ] Use 7-day moving averages on ad dashboards and wait for full retention windows (D7 needs 7 days). [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] After an ad burst, compare new versus returning players and average session time. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Prioritise the next feature by usage data rather than intuition. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Log daily min and max CCU alongside the average, not a single snapshot. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] When the analytics agent is available, ask it a plain-language question about the last update and check the answer in the raw dashboards (T17632). [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Use Ban API with user IDs for moderation; reserve Kick for immediate temporary removal. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Keep a watch list separate from confirmed bans and log every moderation action with its source. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Enable 2-Step Verification and add a passkey, verified email, and backup recovery method. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Open License Manager in Creator Hub and review the IP's revenue share, scope and content standards [brand-deals-and-ip-licensing]
- [ ] Set the start date at least 3 days ahead, in midnight UTC terms [brand-deals-and-ip-licensing]
- [ ] Avoid negotiation or off-platform contact in license messages [brand-deals-and-ip-licensing]
- [ ] Rights holders: upload 10+ verifiable, distinct images with citations [brand-deals-and-ip-licensing]
- [ ] Rights holders: file infringement via Rights Manager only as the rights holder [brand-deals-and-ip-licensing]
- [ ] Pick one colour palette per new area before decorating it. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Name layered clothing cages exactly and export selected objects only [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Apply UIAspectRatioConstraint to framed images and shapes. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Choose the light type by source (PointLight, SpotLight, SurfaceLight) and tune Range rather than Brightness for coverage. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Start clothing and caging from Roblox templates; never edit cage UVs or add cage vertices. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Set ImageLabel ResampleMode to Pixelated for pixel art shown at low canvas resolution. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Back up assets before generating textures, do not edit an asset while generation runs, and use Quality mode (4K PBR) when detail matters. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] After generating a procedural model, add custom parameters (e.g. colour) and confirm existing placed instances keep their position after a code update. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] When mixed assets look inconsistent, run retexture with one reference image across all of them. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Use a plain white-background reference image when generating a 3D model with AI. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Name the pickup child 'pickup'; put item models in ReplicatedStorage Objects and weld them unanchored. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Clone prefabs from storage instead of building properties at runtime. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Package reusable systems with no game-specific dependencies; keep game logic in a separate implementation folder. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Put each object's permission module beneath it and route checks through one central manager. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Define a typed Props table with defaults for every reusable UI component in ReplicatedStorage. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Before standardising on string-based requires, confirm the exact syntax in the official require documentation. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Debounce damage per target, removing the debounce via Debris after 1-4 s. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Check hit.Parent (and grandparent) for a Humanoid before damage. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Clamp damage to humanoid.Health and keep damagePerHit as an attribute. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Use >= for every level requirement check so higher-level players keep access to lower tiers. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] After generating or spawning guns, visually check that the weapon is attached and held in the hand; floating guns were seen in generated output (T17911, low-advice). [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] When a Touched hit comes from an accessory, resolve the owning character before applying damage. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Answer the maturity questionnaire on the most extreme content [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Filter all player-visible text through GetNonChatStringForBroadcastAsync inside pcall. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Do not edit a Build game in Studio; make changes in the Build chat. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Check upload fees, moderation guidelines and asset IDs before paying to upload. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Put roles, royalty splits and exit terms in writing before any collaborator starts (T10743, T10744). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Plan the platform-integrated chat migration before May 2027 (T14033). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Check coordinate origins: CanvasDraw starts at (1,1), EditableImage at (0,0) (T11276, T12924). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Submit moderation reviews early; queues can exceed 5 hours and pending is not a ban (Xaiyne). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Keep your account in good standing; Terms of Use violations can hold or forfeit pending earnings. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Do not display like or engagement counters that do not reflect real player engagement. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Set written rules and moderation guidelines before growth [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Stay within 3 social links on the game page, one per link type, and verify age 16+ before adding them. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Migrate any legacy chat to TextChatService and prefer Quick Words over custom presets. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Give moderators read-only chat permissions for live events. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Use Join Requirements (Verification Level, Account Age) rather than custom gating. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Keep voice chat optional. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Set a moderation bot with a warning ladder before removal. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Write an in-context reason every time chat, content, or a report is moderated. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] If voice chat is enabled for 13+ users, evaluate subtle nudges to reduce abuse reports (reported drop of more than half). [community-and-discord]
- [ ] For quick reactions (e.g. dodging, responding to the player), use the human-like agent model. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] For longer-range planning or simple puzzles, start with behaviour-tree logic before adding human-like agents. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Put budget into long-engagement systems before cosmetics. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Plan a fallback experience for console and VR players on the age check flow. [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] Confirm the experience loads and plays through Web Play in Chrome via a direct link or Details Page URL. [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] Set the source language before starting localization, then add supported languages. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Re-take the questionnaire and resubmit whenever an update changes an answer. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Allow up to 24 hours of moderation before marketplace items can be enabled for sale. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Budget rework time when integrating a third-party framework into an existing game. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Write the feature's plan with AI in plan mode, then read the generated plan markdown and fix it before the agent builds. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Commit reusable agent skills to the project repo for teammates to reuse. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Check Highly Engaged Player status for Kids/Select against the reported 250 threshold, and confirm it with Roblox before relying on it. [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] If a new game has engaged players but low CCU, check Home recommendation traffic in the acquisition tab before changing the game. [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] Do not use player buyouts to end a challenge unless the format is disclosed up front and the policy check is complete. [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] Use the 8–12 min average session figure (T17471, self-reported) as a reference point. If retention sits at or below it, suspect a novelty-only hook. [genre-mechanics]
- [ ] Use local agencies for non-English markets and hold regular meetings [influencer-and-creator-marketing]
- [ ] Use hyperlinked anchor text and an FTC disclosure for affiliate links [influencer-and-creator-marketing]
- [ ] Check Creator Affiliate eligibility (4M+ MAU game, 65% likes, age 13+) [influencer-and-creator-marketing]
- [ ] Hold a weekly strategy meeting with local agencies running non-English campaigns. [influencer-and-creator-marketing]
- [ ] Set source language and target languages [localization-and-international]
- [ ] Verify in-game text per language for auto-translation overrides [localization-and-international]
- [ ] Check player geography to prioritize languages [localization-and-international]
- [ ] Use colon syntax for all instance methods; keep self-typed class methods. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Check syntax: commas between parameters, two commas in for headers, no code after return, consistent indentation. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Enable voice chat before expecting the Voice Chat signal to affect matchmaking. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Turn off CanCollide, CanTouch and CanQuery on walk-through foliage and decorative parts. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Cap MaxVelocity on AlignPosition for carried packages and use AlignPosition instead of per-frame Position (T5201, N2431). [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] In CreatePath, set AgentHeight, AgentRadius and AgentCanJump to match the NPC rig instead of defaults. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Use a pathfinding module (e.g. Forbidden) in ReplicatedStorage instead of raw PathfindingService for chase and barrier navigation. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Use TakeDamage (not Health) so ForceFields work. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Avoid hard rejection; gate features or route players, and write a custom message if rejection is unavoidable. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Make higher difficulty change enemy behaviour or layout, not only damage. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Chat with players in-game to ask what is broken and what they enjoy. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Use experience notifications (13+, 1 per day) at meaningful moments [retention-d1-d7]
- [ ] Wrap friend-join and return-to-game teleports in a capped retry (example: 5 attempts, 1 s delay), then surface a clear failure message. [retention-d1-d7]
- [ ] Use milestone letters or notes instead of constant on-screen dialogue to re-engage players. [retention-d1-d7]
- [ ] Plan launch for the 16+ gate until 500 highly engaged players [roblox-platform-changes]
- [ ] Update AvatarUnificationMode usage before Dec 1, 2026 [roblox-platform-changes]
- [ ] Set content ratings and review content for restricted themes [roblox-platform-changes]
- [ ] Check whether a social feature requires kid-to-adult communication; if so, redesign it for same-age peers. [roblox-platform-changes]
- [ ] If your game is under the 16+ lock, log the count of unique highly engaged 16+ users over rolling 60-day windows against 500 (N8013, unverified). [roblox-platform-changes]
- [ ] Before requesting a non-16+ rating, confirm Roblox Plus status and ID verification, and allow about 2 months (N8016, unverified). [roblox-platform-changes]
- [ ] Remove unique IDs from error messages before they are logged. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Cap the number of active pathfinding agents. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] WaitForChild the Humanoid and Head before iterating character parts. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Use assert(condition, message) for required values. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Add a fallback message for userId-based friend joins when the friend is offline. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Track Share Link visits against engagement and earnings in Creator Analytics. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Enable team voice with the new voice chat APIs and tune distance attenuation curves. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Link social accounts on the Game Detail Page and post regular teasers. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Plan social mechanics (trading, parties, guilds, leaderboards) to extend engagement after the ending. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Do not assume cross-age chat works for every player; check Trusted Friends availability by country. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Verify the status of in-game friend text chat in Studio and the platform docs before building around it. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Restrict unfiltered text and voice to trusted connections; keep stranger chat filtered. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Use pre-set Quick Words in mixed-age lobbies once they are available. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Before counting on Roblox program money, confirm the current terms: the source says support is mentorship, promotion, and planned incentives, not equity or grants. [studio-business-funding-and-publishing]
- [ ] Build the studio budget without Roblox cash unless the current program terms explicitly include it. [studio-business-funding-and-publishing]
- [ ] Use breakpoints with log-message or boolean conditions rather than repeated print-and-edit cycles. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Vehicles: each speeder model needs a Body part, no extra nested models, and must sit inside the Garage folder. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Run cloud device simulators for Apple, Android, console and VR coverage. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Include variable names in warn messages for missing values. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Check detail page CTR under Acquisition [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Fill the game detail page with images and videos (up to 10). [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Confirm ID or phone verification before relying on asset listing media (1 video, up to 5 images). [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Add social links on game details page [titles-descriptions]
- [ ] Compare each short against your channel average [trailers-and-video-marketing]
- [ ] Match content language to your playerbase [trailers-and-video-marketing]
- [ ] Verify Moments placement and eligibility in Studio or Creator Hub after the rollout; the sources do not state the criteria. [trailers-and-video-marketing]
- [ ] Track Moments retention and view changes for your own clips against your channel average before drawing conclusions from the reported gains. [trailers-and-video-marketing]
- [ ] Link each series episode to the game's Experience Details Page and any promo posts so build viewers can reach the game. [trailers-and-video-marketing]
- [ ] Track series views and clicks to the game against the channel average before deciding whether to keep the format. [trailers-and-video-marketing]
- [ ] Before buying a game, compare peak and average CCU [trend-research-and-validation]
- [ ] Record the date of any platform benchmark you use, since the 2026-09 figures will age quickly. [trend-research-and-validation]
- [ ] Never delete cage vertices or faces; keep outer cage tight to clothing. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] Restock free Limited from configure page only if never resellable. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] Plan for up to 24-hour moderation before sale. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] Confirm pilot eligibility (one Marketplace publish in the past 30 days, 12 months clean moderation, clean DMCA) before planning animation or background sales. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] Grant collaborators Use & View, not Edit, for consumption; add the universe ID under Permissions > Experiences for restricted packages. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] Check the current fees page for the upload fee per item type before budgeting catalog size. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] Draft private unfinished UGC assets with Asset Iteration once it is live, to avoid moderation delay. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] Use TextChannelWindow for custom chat after disabling default chat windows. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Clear the chat input box after sending a message. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Check the current Studio version for native text shadows, gradients, blur and animated image containers before adding stroke, duplicate-label or gradient-image workarounds. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] For chat moderation UI, use contextual real-time nudges and log whether they reduce reports in your own game. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] If sales show repeated error messages per item, fix the flow to batch selections rather than adding more error text. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Early-return on gameProcessed in InputBegan so chat doesn't trigger game actions. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Rotate loading screen art from a set of images so returning players see variety. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] For Off-Platform Featuring, submit at least 7 days ahead, target 7-30 day runs, and do not edit after submission; confirm creator is 13+ with a linked email. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Re-engage lapsed players with a concrete new-content release plus a notification. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] After an event underperforms, check the entry path (eligibility, card info) and patch it quickly. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Check the count of highly engaged players over a 60-day window if pursuing Kids/Select eligibility. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Plan at least one timed rare-drop event (e.g., meteor shower, limited cage) between major updates to drive return visits. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Publish a dated roadmap and record how many items shipped and how many were re-dated in each update, so slippage is visible. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Between big content drops, ship one cheap low-maintenance system to keep players engaged. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Before editing a game published before the age-verification change, list every planned change and drop any non-essential ones to limit verification triggers. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Enable Drafts Mode in File > Experience Settings if collaborating in Studio (off by default). [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Map folders to services in default.project.json using path; map Packages for Wally. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Write meaningful publish messages for rollback and audit. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Before any risky edit, commit to Git with a clear message and push. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Keep the 'local globals' marker intact in plugin-managed modules. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Turn on auto-update for PackageLink. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Add the package to wally.toml and run wally install. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Confirm the package is listed in default.project.json before running rojo serve. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Run wally-package-types after installing Wally packages so their types are available. [version-control-and-tooling]

## 10. Launch week: ads, analytics, live ops

### Must
- [ ] Set every MeshPart Transparency to 0 and one matching AvatarPartScaleType per upload. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Remove custom attributes, extra helper head bones and non-character objects before upload. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] For dynamic heads: neutral on frame 0, one FACS pose per frame, at least 17 poses, under 50 face joints. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Re-export animations after every change; the in-game version only updates on export. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] For head accessories, place attachments on the visible head surface; for grips, orient 90,0,0 in I-Pose. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Check that a head-only Avatar Setup includes the body; delete FaceControls before rerunning setup. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Set animation type to Curve before upload (reinforces existing rule). [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Add an Attachment named exactly as the rig's head attachment (e.g. FaceFrontAttachment). [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]

### Also consider
- [ ] Confirm retention and playtime are good before buying ads [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Open Creator Hub > Ads and use the 3-step flow; start from recommended budget and duration [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Use a daily budget (~$5-20/day) and run 7-10+ days [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Monitor Acquisition analytics: home recommendation impressions vs sponsored impressions [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Stop ads once home recs exceed sponsored impressions [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Post teasers and hype content before launches/events [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Scale spend only while ROAS/retention holds (ROAS >100% for Earnings) [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Pause any ad showing CTR near 0.3% with a play rate near 0.07. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Judge the campaign by day-7 retention and sessions, not impressions or clicks. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Fix stability and retention before any paid spend. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Use PAUSED, not CANCELLED, for temporary stops. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Raise budgets gradually because increases apply immediately. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Before any ad spend, read Roblox's current ad documentation; the 2018 guides are dated. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Use a 7-day or 1-2 week run rather than a short burst, and re-run on major updates. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Set a custom daily budget and a fixed run length, and disable auto-reload. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Set bid per play at roughly 0.02 for small budgets; never below 0.01. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Exclude console in the ad set if you do not want console players. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Split large budgets across days and review between tranches. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Watch day-1 retention to confirm the ad promise matches the gameplay. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Check for in-game events, admin actions or promotions in the same window before attributing a traffic spike to ads. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Before budgeting off-platform acquisition, check whether Roblox co-marketing or UA support programs apply to your game. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] When forecasting pre-roll ad revenue or reach, exclude Roblox Plus subscribers, since they won't see pre-roll ads. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Confirm pre-roll status is still 'coming soon' or live in the current docs before promising creators revenue from it. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Do not treat creator-driven download spikes as proof that paid ads are profitable. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Spend a small UA budget early to compare CPI with projected LTV and track retention. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Track churn per 1,000 impressions for players acquired through ads. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Upload only content you own or have permission to use. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Strip alcohol assets labeled beer/wine/alcohol, graphic violence, severe injury and real-world events. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Track D1, D7, D30 retention and map each to its likely cause. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Use up to 3 custom fields instead of many event names or currencies. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Compare each release against previous releases (DAU, conversion, playtime, retention). [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Break down retention by acquisition source before blaming the game. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Compare event windows: week before, during and after. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Document experiments and learnings; revisit dashboards periodically. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] After any update, log the peak CCU and the settled CCU a week later; judge the change on the settled number (T6861, T6466). [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Compare periods with the Analytics tab date range (previous day to last 90 days) (T8106, N3338). [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Track CTR, conversion rate and cost per play for each ad (T5859, N2782, N2783). [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Check the six overview stats against the 50th percentile before adding ad spend (T6536). [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Set benchmarks by region before judging retention or ARPPU for a player base (T6663). [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Run the four-signal scorecard a few days after release, then decide after 1-2 weeks (T6319, N2833). [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Use Benchmarks endTime one day later in Analytics than in Benchmarks (T7538). [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Use analytics only as far as budget allows; paid analytics services can be costly. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Track D1 and D7 retention together with playtime; D7 is weighted in Home Recommendations. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Check the Video Service dashboard after adding or changing videos. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Scope the Analytics API key to universe.analytics:read and send it as x-api-key. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Convert reporting days to UTC before comparing with dashboard buckets. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Check Statistics > Analytics > Acquisition for Home Recommendations after an ad run; if present, stop ads. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Export sales CSVs monthly, since dashboard history is limited. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Review safety analytics report-abuse breakdowns against the 90th percentile. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Open the Creator Analytics dashboard and compare per-game signals against the published signal list and relative weights to see where the game is strong or weak. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Compare your DAU and hours growth with platform DAU and hours for the same quarter before crediting an update. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Check the abuse-report analytics dashboard daily for takedown counts and violation reasons, and compare with similar games (T17729). [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Compare platform-reported acquisition with MMP data (AppsFlyer, Adjust, or Singular) before moving UA budget. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Compare verticals on CPM, view-to-install, LTV and retention, and name the failing metric before changing creators. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Write the target KPI down before designing any live event. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Track KPIs beyond the target during any revenue push, including retention. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Review analytics weekly across a 60-day improvement plan. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Investigate when visits look healthy but the like ratio is low, before spending on ads. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Re-measure consent and opt-in rates each quarter instead of reusing old figures. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Scope Open Cloud keys to needed scopes, restrict by IP and expiry, and read them with HttpService:GetSecret. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Run commands only after the profile is Ready; cap inbound payload size. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] On an avatar callback, log the event and add the user to a deny list instead of expecting further action. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Check the partner's reputation and past event reception [brand-deals-and-ip-licensing]
- [ ] Update your game before the start date [brand-deals-and-ip-licensing]
- [ ] Give players 2-3 entry points per building and keep some dead ends. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Clip-check the map from the player's perspective before release. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Use sensor parts for signal triggers instead of unioned track parts or Object Values pointing at unions. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Merge scattered small terrain patches into one dominant terrain area where possible. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Start a new theme set with a few base plates (for example three) and add more later instead of building every theme at once (N7682). [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Use a ramp instead of a ladder for any carried object that can't reach the ladder [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Make ladder parts visually read as ladders so players recognise the climb [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Confirm every new area has its egg or interaction platform and that each pad's main part is set up before release. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Surround key event NPCs with scenery so they are not missed on join. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Budget time for building event maps from reusable asset kits such as ladders and fences. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Put spawn parts in one folder spread across the map, anchored, CanCollide off, Transparency 1. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Pick a random spawn part for each spawn instead of one fixed spawn. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Read GuiService.PreferredTextSize, PreferredTransparency and ReducedMotionEnabled. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Limit haptics to explosions, collisions, attention events and notifications. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Keep audio uploads under 10 seconds if your account is verified. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Upload each mesh once and duplicate it in Studio; don't reimport identical meshes as new IDs. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Author clips in Blender, Maya or Moon Animator, but upload and assemble bundles in Studio; all 7 clips in a pack must be original. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Position parts relative to a rotated part with part.CFrame:PointToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(...)) instead of adding to Position. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] If a camera jolt persists, flip the camera angle at the end of the move so it reads as intentional. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Confirm AI-generated textures are 2K or smaller and the mesh is under 4,000 triangles before upload. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Build the lobby early and budget roughly 1 hour for it. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Deliver story through in-game documents and in-world characters instead of static cards. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Split ad creatives into an intro, an outro and swappable content blocks so length can be changed fast (T18429, low-advice). [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Plan the full asset list ahead and build in themed batches (e.g. candy cane, town, train station) (T18857). [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Use a limited colour palette for liquid models so they read clearly (T18865). [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Estimate payload per second and keep it under roughly 25 kB/s, with half reserved for Roblox replication (T5417, N2330). [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Use Camera:ViewportPointToRay for mouse position instead of the deprecated Mouse object. [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Expose a narrow public API (getValue, updateValue, ChangeValue) and hide internals behind underscore-prefixed members. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Auto-load each module folder into a table keyed by name, and check that no two module names are the same. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Use a custom signal module when event deferral behaviour matters. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Keep shared player state in an atoms module; use effects to react instead of polling. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Clone template accessories at runtime from that folder instead of placing them in the world. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Track ammo, reject shots at 0, refill only at reload end. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Keep hit targets in one folder (for example Alive) and search only that folder. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Set IK weight to 0.999 rather than 1 so the head gives up at extreme angles. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Re-check the capacity value after any Roblox platform update, since it is a platform setting and may change. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Set uploads to Restricted before selling [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Freeze the character until data loads; never wait for data inside PlayerRemoving. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Preview Avatar Settings defaults before publishing a live game. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Watch CCU over several days after each update; roll back quickly if an update is game-breaking. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Verify uploads are set to Public and audio levels are normal after publishing. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Judge a launch on retention after the spike, not peak CCU (T13674). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Remove vanity buttons and empty windows; reset players in-session instead of kicking on death (T11011, T11016). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Check for Z-fighting on floors and NPC sitting animation glitches before release (LovJak). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Replace per-second world scans with event-driven detection (Lnor). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Confirm the game is polished and retains players before sponsorship or paid ads (PolarBear, Poke). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Give every event enough original content; do not reuse a few minigames across many eggs (ThatGuyEvan). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] If using a clone or asset kit, replace its assets with original art and character animations before release. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Default NPC and AI behavior to cheap rules; escalate to dynamic behavior only for events that need it. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Explain removals and changes in update logs [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Run regular themed event days [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Track people waiting for updates, not just plays [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Pick one platform and post devlogs consistently for about 2 months before release. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Track application review cadence (1-2 months, 3 months or weekly depending on program). [community-and-discord]
- [ ] If your game has been quiet, post a short status update even if the timeline is uncertain. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] After each update, check that in-game wikis, info boards, and linked Trello boards match the game. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Release 2-3 playable items per update; check new content changes behaviour. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Define the exact events that can trigger any dynamic or automated behaviour. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Assume every new power is overpowered; schedule a nerf pass before wider release. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Read UserInputService.PreferredInput to adapt on-screen buttons and menus. [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] Apply gamepad conventions: ButtonA confirm/jump, ButtonB cancel/dodge/sprint, Thumbstick1 move, Thumbstick2 camera, R2/L2 primary action. [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] Android TV: design D-pad-friendly controls (2D, simple movement, menu-driven). [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] Android TV: detect micro gamepads via Gamepad1 with no Thumbstick1/2, and check ButtonCenter, not keycodes alone. [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] Android TV: map ButtonCenter to ButtonA, ButtonBack to ButtonB, D-pad keys to DPad directions; separate navigation and gameplay input contexts. [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] Use BestForDevice drag style: TranslatePlaneOrLine (mouse/gamepad), TranslatePlane (touch), 6DOF (VR). [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] Replace mouse-only click handlers on GUI buttons with Activated-style events. [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] Before choosing between one cross-platform build and separate pure-play builds, check your own analytics for how many players use multiple devices. [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] Wrap every GetAsync, SetAsync, UpdateAsync and IncrementAsync in pcall; warn(err) on failure. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Only overwrite session data when the load pcall succeeded and data exists. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Decrement the shutdown counter on every early-return path, including when the player left mid-load. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Keep values in a local table and avoid writing on every gameplay event. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Verify failed writes with a fresh read (UseCache=false) before retrying. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Define every default in ProfileTemplate and call Reconcile() after each load. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Call AddUserId(player.UserId) on load and define RTBF deletion templates such as User_{UserId}. [data-persistence]
- [ ] On PlayerRemoving call EndSession()/Release(); kick on OnSessionEnd and on a nil load. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Use UpdateAsync for writes that depend on current data; SetAsync only for independent new values. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Always pass metadata with SetAsync, IncrementAsync and UpdateAsync; use IncrementAsync for counters. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Never yield inside an UpdateAsync callback; return nil to cancel a write. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Wrap each PlayerAdded load so handlers only run once the profile is loaded. [data-persistence]
- [ ] After changing profile.Data, update matching leaderstats values in the same code path. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Clamp integer fields to template bounds on load and cap array lengths. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Keep a content backlog and a 3-month rolling roadmap [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Limit cadence releases to 2-3 weeks with no new systems [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Run a retro after every update and track KPIs [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] After each update, check the target metric and revert if not improved [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Schedule sustainable hours; cancel events that aren't ready [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Write asset and tech requests before each content cadence; split evergreen and seasonal assets. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Create events as private and switch to public only when you promote them. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Build reusable quest, collection and event systems to cut future update time. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Plan 2-3 week sprints; at sprint end move unfinished items out instead of extending the sprint. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Keep Studio updated; outdated versions lack the newest APIs and features. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Check the Early Access opt-in in account settings before editing a live game or Team Create place. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Before a big update, design data structures for all systems up front. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Budget for slips on big updates: plan a 2-month update as possibly 6-7 months. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Keep a live game running unless it is game-breaking. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Add a post-launch update budget (time and cost) to the full-cycle plan before launch. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] If a live game's base is outgrown, plan a foundation rebuild rather than a full game replacement. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Track the share of roadmap or sprint items shipped on time and compare to the 79% platform benchmark. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] If AI-generated fixes keep failing after a few tries, stop prompting and write the code by hand. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Compare retention, playtime and CCU between releases to confirm each update improved the game. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] When setting roadmap dates, plan for roughly 21% of items to slip past their target (79% on-time base rate). [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Stop ads when organic Home traffic sustains lobbies [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] Update regularly to extend content life and D28 retention [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] Split retention into day 1, day 2-7 and day 8-28 cohorts rather than one blended figure. [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] Plan content updates to sustain the 28-day window rather than relying on one launch spike. [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] Confirm whether game-item surfacing on Home is live before counting on it in launch plans. [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] Check that D1, D7 and D28 retention are tracked as the signals the speaker says the algorithm weights (T17946), noting this is unverified. [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] Target roughly 40-50% watch-time retention (self-reported target). [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] Stop ads when the game holds about 50+ players after the ad period ends and Home recommendations are present. [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] Review a week of retention and session-time data before pushing a feature update. [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] Gate event modes behind play [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] Set a fixed launch time for major updates [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] Use PATCH to reschedule events [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] Schedule sales up to 30 days ahead [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] Add a pre-launch sign-up pad [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] Ship small regular updates [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] Give events a story and a gated finale [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] Provide fallback logic for sound-triggered events [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] Add random events every ~90 seconds during idle phases [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] Avoid covert odds boosts near event end [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] Staff events with a dedicated team [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] Publish a recurring event calendar in advance and keep fixed dates for limited-time events. [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] Track how existing event collections gain value over time before judging a new event's staying power. [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] Split a story event into multi-session quest steps so each return visit has a next objective. [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] Schedule anniversary or milestone events around platform dates (Roblox 20th anniversary event began 2026-09-17). [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] If running an elimination challenge, publish the cut schedule (time and build timer) before the event starts. [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] Before a seasonal update, open a sign-up and plan on roughly half of sign-ups joining (N7970). [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] When making a limited badge, award it only after the player completes the event, keyed to a specific owned item. [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] For a holiday update, reskin some existing pets with seasonal accessories before building new ones. [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] When a timed event starts, fire an on-screen notification to create hype. [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] Plan a calendar where a classic event follows each seasonal event and reuses old spawns and pets. [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] If a collection event uses combine steps, use a fixed N-to-1 ladder (Mulch used 10:1) and show progress toward the next tier. [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] Show the drop rate and guarantee threshold in the event UI before players open crates. [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] Use beams/arrows/trails instead of text [first-session-onboarding]
- [ ] Run Roblox ads on major updates, especially weekends [influencer-and-creator-marketing]
- [ ] Plan a gradual reduction of creator spend rather than an abrupt cut, since installs decline faster when spend stops. [influencer-and-creator-marketing]
- [ ] Use auto-translation only as a stopgap and audit it [localization-and-international]
- [ ] Download translation reports [localization-and-international]
- [ ] Replace every wait() with task.wait(); use task.spawn/task.defer instead of spawn(). [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Return the cloned and changed table from an update function, not the original. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Connect events with a bare function reference (no parentheses). [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] When you need an integer result from division, write a // b instead of a / b. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] For surface-relative placement, use PointToObjectSpace and read the axis components instead of per-face branches. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] For each Connect call, inline the anonymous function only if that handler is used by that one event; otherwise name it and pass the reference. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Treat any other TeleportResult as invalid and abort instead of retrying. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Read join data with Player:GetJoinData().TeleportData on arrival. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Set a queue time limit so the queue starts with the current count instead of waiting forever. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Create sounds at runtime instead of storing them in SoundService. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Destroy references to unused animations and audio so they unload. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Estimate load time at about 1 second per 10,000 instances and keep total join load under about 15 seconds. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Deparent parts you want unloaded instead of parenting them under Lighting or hiding them. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Release animation references for dead characters and NPCs. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] For a one-time speed or spin, set BasePart.AssemblyLinearVelocity or AssemblyAngularVelocity instead of adding a constraint (T9738, T7899). [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Put physics-affecting writes in PreSimulation and physics reads in PostSimulation (T9436, T9437). [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Use event-driven hitboxes via Touched and enter/leave events rather than per-frame detection (T5842, T5018). [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Use Humanoid:Move per waypoint with Heartbeat arrival and handle Path.Blocked only for waypoints ahead (T8948, T8949). [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Keep path requests under 3,000 studs straight-line and watch the 20,000 node budget (N3607, N3608). [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Set ControllerPartSensor.SensorUpdateType to Manual and add GroundSensor.HitFrame (T6023, T6024). [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Connect the Path Blocked event and recompute only when the blocked waypoint is ahead of the NPC. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Use SimplePath beyond about 20 studs and Humanoid:MoveTo inside it; set stopNearPlayer around 3 studs. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Throttle NPC think Rate (about 0.1 s) and MaxDistance; use distance-based update rates for far chasers. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Raycast about 3 studs overhead before allowing a character to stand from crawl. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Confirm the NPC has a Humanoid, a Head and a HumanoidRootPart (or a PrimaryPart) before the module runs. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Set target offset (about 3 studs) so NPCs stop short of the player. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] During a knockback push, set Humanoid.Sit = true briefly to stop walking, then clear it. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Add a live progress tracker for competitive games. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Lerp picked-up objects toward screen centre (alpha about 0.2) instead of teleporting them. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Schedule difficulty spikes in rotation and track retention after each spike. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Read downvote comments for specific suggestions [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Before paid ads or public launch, confirm the game loads and is playable from a clean join. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Ship a fast follow-up update that visibly addresses the named criticisms. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Confirm analytics SDK events fire correctly during that non-dev playthrough, not only in dev builds. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Plan a day-8 update/event before launch [retention-d1-d7]
- [ ] Ship weekly or biweekly updates [retention-d1-d7]
- [ ] Target D1 ~12-15%, D7 ~2-3% [retention-d1-d7]
- [ ] Avoid manipulative daily pressure; don't reset player progress on updates [retention-d1-d7]
- [ ] Confirm every death or wipe state leads to a restart, revive, or return-to-lobby option. [retention-d1-d7]
- [ ] After a major update or event, watch daily player count for sharp drops and treat a steep fall as a signal to ship a fix or new content quickly. [retention-d1-d7]
- [ ] Review library dependencies for dynamic updates [roblox-platform-changes]
- [ ] Compare impression and retention data before shifting strategy toward larger projects in response to reported algorithm changes (Q1908). [roblox-platform-changes]
- [ ] Connect UpdateAvailable to Refresh for config snapshots, and use GetValueChangedSignal. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Check each API member's thread-safety tier before using it in parallel code. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Count UpdateAsync as two requests; check GetRequestBudgetForRequestType before request bursts. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Use entry/exit sensors instead of GetTouchingParts polling for track or block occupancy. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Guard player data reads with an is-loaded check or loaded signal. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Connect named handler functions once instead of per-player lambdas. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Use GetPropertyChangedSignal for single-property watches instead of .Changed. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Change IntValue.Value to update leaderboard numbers; do not rely on renaming objects. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Pair limited-time items or events with a trading system. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Use the Creator Dashboard to copy the event details URL when promoting events. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Add an in-game prompt inviting players to the next update. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Avoid physical print promotions on limited budgets. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Track breakout timing. Budget several months of near-daily posting before expecting a breakout. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Re-check naming and safety settings after each platform update, since these change often. [social-features-and-virality]
- [ ] Use volumetric emitters for rain, rooms, waterfalls and cities; verify the volumetric setting is Enabled. [sound-and-music]
- [ ] Tune Threshold, Ratio, Attack and Release by ear on real source levels. [sound-and-music]
- [ ] Use a licensed-catalog picker for radios and boomboxes instead of ID playback. [sound-and-music]
- [ ] Check your monthly audio upload quota before planning a large audio batch. [sound-and-music]
- [ ] Listen to AudioPlayer.Ended and debounce volume triggers instead of relying on IsPlaying. [sound-and-music]
- [ ] Use volumetric audio with SoundService to switch soundscapes by area or state. [sound-and-music]
- [ ] On Audio objects, use AudioPlayer.Ended for the same gating instead of Sound.Ended. [sound-and-music]
- [ ] Write a 1-2-3 month roadmap covering improvements and marketing [studio-business-funding-and-publishing]
- [ ] Do not accept a lowball or bad deal [studio-business-funding-and-publishing]
- [ ] Add a temporary print inside each new event handler (Touched, etc.) to confirm it fires; check event name capitalisation. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Check UI with rbx-device-simulator-lua presets, including safe areas, before release. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Read the publish reminder for uncommitted drafts before publishing. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] After publishing, check leaderboards on the live place, not only in Studio. [testing-debugging-and-qa]
- [ ] Make separate sets for new vs returning players and acquisition vs retention [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Upload authentic gameplay preview video [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Use 1–2 click ad prompts with a concrete CTA. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Upload up to 10 ad creatives per campaign and disable underperformers after comparing per-creative results. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] If CTR is high but PTR or players are low, fix the game before scaling ads. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] No repeated words, bloat, or monetary lead [titles-descriptions]
- [ ] Keep update blurb short [titles-descriptions]
- [ ] Upload preview video in Creator Hub [titles-descriptions]
- [ ] Upload videos via Creator Hub > Configure > Places > Videos [trailers-and-video-marketing]
- [ ] Ramp to full promo cadence only near release (T17806). [trailers-and-video-marketing]
- [ ] Compare with similar-player experiences in Analytics [trend-research-and-validation]
- [ ] Before building, write down the market void, the unmet player want, and the update objective (live-ops, new content, or retention hook). [trend-research-and-validation]
- [ ] Set a hard time box (e.g., hours, not weeks) for any build aimed at a short-lived trend. [trend-research-and-validation]
- [ ] Pick Body suit/Full mask only for full-body or full head/face items; otherwise Shorts, Dress/Skirt, or Pants. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] Keep accessories as separate objects from head and body geometry; keep clothing as modular layers. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] Archive development items only after confirming no live experience uses them, because archived assets vanish from your own games. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] Confirm emissive texture support in Studio before planning glow effects for a UGC release. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] Model UGC revenue as a declining curve unless new items are released; do not assume passive income from old uploads. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] Mock up in Figma or grayscale blocks before building; rebuild rather than patch a poor UI. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Keep the Missions and Layers hierarchy intact; update code that finds objects when you move them. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Re-check touch button overlaps in Studio Emulator or on a physical device after the April 2026 touch update. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Restyle any free template before release and replace placeholder emojis with real art. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Set DisplayDistanceType and MaxDistance on BillboardGuis to limit overhead clutter. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Keep per-frame GUI updates light and check MicroProfiler for dynamic GUI cost. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Use auto-reload or one-button reload for consumable weapons. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Bind text properties to state so only changed properties update. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Before adding menu blur, UI shadows, gradients or text shadows as workarounds, check current release notes for native support. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] If base walk speed is low (creator cites 20), add a sprint button for touch and a bindable sprint input for gamepad and PC. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Set overhead BillboardGui StudsOffsetWorldSpace to about 0,3,0 and enable AlwaysOnTop. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] On join, read the current value of any shared state the HUD shows and set the label or bar before connecting to .Changed. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Check that a newly joined player sees correct values without any change event firing. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Pick a cadence you can sustain and write it down [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Plan live ops before building the game [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Plan releases roughly three updates ahead; keep a backlog [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Schedule a major update about quarterly and big expansions every 4-6 months [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Keep cadence running during expansion development [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Release on Friday; cut off dev Wednesday night or Thursday midday [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Set up an Events and Updates entry for each update [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Run a 1-2 week in-game countdown for big updates [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Plan holiday updates a week early; release about 60% in week one [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Use DateTime or UTC (not tick()) for event triggers [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Scope events to about one month of build time and keep them from feeling dead [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Step back periodically to remove bloat; daily-update in week one if you are a small indie game [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Split planned work into minor updates (every 1-2 weeks, or 2-4 weeks on existing mechanics) and major updates (every 2-3 months). [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Estimate effort for each cadence update and confirm it is under three weeks. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Build cadence content on existing systems (maps, pets, quests, weapons, vehicles) and art variants. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Keep a 6-12 month roadmap scoped to team capacity. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Define the target KPI for each event before building it. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Keep published events to 10 or fewer ongoing or upcoming at once. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Use configs for live tuning and schedule timed events by switching config values. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Plan a pre-update follow notification so players are notified when the next update ships. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Deactivate leaderboards instead of deleting them so data is preserved. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Run Audio Discovery on the published place after updating Studio before audio-dependent releases. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Track milestones in analytics to qualify for Developer Awards. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Schedule notable updates and seasonal tie-ins to align with Today's Picks criteria. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Fix failed dynamic heads, publish the fixed head as a Development Item, then submit the asset ID. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Rotate Open Cloud secrets with PATCH and delete unused ones. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Before launching a new game, check whether the existing game can receive the content instead. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Start recurring mini-events with the easiest gameplay tweak (fog, low gravity) before adding complex ones. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Put the next update and its event calendar on a schedule before buying ads. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Track ranking or chart position monthly; if it falls while the game has not shipped content recently, schedule a content drop before adding more events. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Schedule code drops between major updates to keep daily logins active. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] If the game is anime-tied, check the anime season calendar and time a major update to its launch. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Do not treat a long-running seasonal event as a substitute for regular content updates. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Count roadmap items per update (shipped vs re-dated) and review the ratio before promising new dates. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] For a limited egg or drop event, list the removed items being returned and confirm the event window before announcing. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Run rojo build to a .rbxlx and check the structure in Studio before live sync. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Avoid x-roblox-* extensions in long-lived tooling. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Check versionNumber in the publish response and verify in Studio or the Creator Dashboard. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Close other Studio instances of the game before a mass update. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Plan Branch and Merge adoption for after its early 2027 release, and verify its availability in Studio before changing workflow. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Adjust Speed only on new particles; use Acceleration, Drag or VelocityInheritance for live ones. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] Before raising Rate, check whether the effect is already at the particle cap; if so, adjust Size, Speed, or Emit counts instead. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] For directional emitters, keep Velocity spread at 0; for omnidirectional effects, use 180 or 360. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] When reconstructing legacy-era emitter setups, use the baseline defaults (Rate 20/s, Speed 5 to 10, spread 0, rotation 0) and verify them in current Studio. [vfx-and-particles]

## 11. Other

### Must
- [ ] Set Blender Transform > Apply Scalings to FBX Unit Scale, uncheck Add Leaf Bones, Path Mode Copy, Embed Textures. x2 [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Disable unused Humanoid states on NPCs x2 [genre-mechanics]
- [ ] Apply all transforms, set mesh transform 0 / scale 1 and joints scale 1,1,1 with rotation 0,0,0. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Enable Bake Animation only for animated exports; set NLA Strips, All Actions, Force Start/End Keyframes off and Simplify 0.0. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] For Maya: Smooth Mesh, Units Automatic, Up Axis Y; enable Animation only for animations. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Parent with Automatic Weights (armature last), then paint manually for moving parts; select armature then mesh before Weight Paint. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Check body triangles against 10,742 total and per-part caps; leave headroom for Avatar Setup. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Export normal maps OpenGL Tangent Space; albedo/normal RGB 24-bit; masks 8-bit grayscale; UVs in 0:1. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]

### Also consider
- [ ] Keep correctives to 3 or fewer base poses and 3 or fewer control names. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Use CurveAnimation for emotes on an R15 rig. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Set Motor6D Transform changes in PreSimulation and AssemblyRootPart for custom rigs. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Check the model against Roblox specs before publishing. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Name the 15 body meshes per convention (e.g. LeftUpperArm_Geo) and cap every limb so each part is watertight. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Set the pose to I, A or T before export, and export joint scale [1,1,1] with zeroed mesh transforms. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Cap skin influences at 4 bones per vertex, never weight Root or HumanoidRootNode, and set LowerTorso and Root to 0,0,0. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Turn on Auto Normalize and paint at brush strength 1 for neck, wrist and elbow, and about 0.25 for shoulder and torso. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Import rigid accessories as a Model and convert with AFT; do not import third-party attachment objects. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Replace MeshPart.TextureId with SurfaceAppearance; set AlphaMode to Transparency where trim maps have alpha. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Wire normal maps through a Normal Map node (Blender) or Tangent Space Normals on Bump 2d (Maya). [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Set Marketplace MeshParts to Transparency 0, Material Plastic, VertexColor 1,1,1. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] For Marketplace avatars use the Platform Avatar preset and set each mesh Type correctly. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Set animation timeline Start and End to cover all keyframes before export. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Verify animations in-game before polishing, including direction and timing; cycle common facial animations in Studio. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Keep emote and animation clips under 10 seconds. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] For IK Control: set ChainRoot to the furthest moving joint and EndEffector to the outermost reaching part. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Set IK Control Target to an Attachment or Part and Type to Transform for position plus rotation. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] In Rig Edit, select parent then child, press Create Joints, and move pivots to the rotation point. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Start Rig Edit step value at 25 and lower it for finer joint control. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Space keyframes apart to slow motion; avoid packing them tightly. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Use larger leg and arm swings for walk cycles. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Export Blender animations via the RBLX add-on and convert in Studio. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Use only R6 textures on the smooth R6 render rig. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Add a camera and set an output folder before rendering in Blender. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Use R15 for emotes (matches existing rule). [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Wrap the accessory mesh in an Accessory and name its child Handle with a capital H. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Copy the rig attachment name character-for-character; any mismatch breaks alignment. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Fine-tune fit by changing the Attachment CFrame position in Properties, not by moving the mesh. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Keep the Handle size the same across fitting iterations. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] After exporting a hat or accessory, count the textures the export produced and select the highest-resolution one as the source texture. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] For a new pet, set the primary part so the mesh connects correctly before tuning anything else. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] For a new seasonal item, start from an existing Blender asset you already have and modify it. [3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]
- [ ] Move the tunable value into a config [ab-testing-and-experiments]
- [ ] Set variants: exactly one isBaseline true, at most 2 treatments [ab-testing-and-experiments]
- [ ] Set duration (14–60 days), exposure percent and weights [ab-testing-and-experiments]
- [ ] Set MDE and make sure you can enroll at least 10,000 players [ab-testing-and-experiments]
- [ ] Review everything in draft, because it is immutable after start [ab-testing-and-experiments]
- [ ] Optionally set up a webhook for early harm alerts [ab-testing-and-experiments]
- [ ] Wait at least 24 hours before pulling /stats [ab-testing-and-experiments]
- [ ] Wait for the full duration and daily results before picking a winner [ab-testing-and-experiments]
- [ ] Complete with variantId to ship a winner, or omit it to revert [ab-testing-and-experiments]
- [ ] Verify config drafts with GET before POST publish [ab-testing-and-experiments]
- [ ] Make the variant a meaningful change (roughly 50-100% on a value, or a reskin of a high-traffic screen) rather than a ~5% tweak. [ab-testing-and-experiments]
- [ ] Re-check shipped features at about 3 months against the original forecast. [ab-testing-and-experiments]
- [ ] Check cost per play (~$0.008 target) on day one [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Don't restart or panic over low early stats or bot-like lobbies [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Create separate share links per off-platform channel [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Call GetAdAvailabilityNowAsync right before showing the button [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] After about one day, check CTR (aim roughly 2.5%+) and playthrough rate (aim over 1%). [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Send an idempotency key on every campaign creation request. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Poll batchGetStatus until SERVING, NOT_SERVING or REJECTED. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Use durationInDays for run length and keep it under 3650. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Compare paid players with home-recommendation players each day; reduce credits as organic players rise. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Cancel early when results are clear to recover unused full days. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Promote to users over 16 to support Kids/Select evaluation. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Target verified players and confirm clicks can actually enter the game. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Schedule the run for your peak days (weekends or Friday to Sunday). [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Let the campaign run continuously; avoid pulling and reposting it. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Reconcile credits bought against credits spent, since unused credits are not refundable. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Before buying cold traffic, plan for low click-to-play: a 2.5% CTR with about one play per three clicks means large impression volumes are needed. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Create a separate lapsed-player campaign for existing games and measure the share of returners within 7 days. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Judge each campaign over a multi-day window across repeated exposures, not on last-click conversions. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Launch each challenger creative in its own campaign; never add it to a champion campaign. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Build a persona-by-trend matrix before producing creatives. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Record CTR and IPM (impressions per install) for every creative. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Check watch time and completion per placement, not only install rate. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Re-check CPI vs LTV monthly, since CPI rises over time. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Expect paid installs to grow slower than impressions and wait for larger samples. [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Before launching any campaign, list all active campaigns and confirm none overlap unintentionally (N8106). [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Measure creator marketing with install or play attribution, not reach alone (T18798). [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] When a platform signal changes, rebuild campaign mechanics rather than rerunning the old setup (T18734). [ads-and-sponsored-experiences]
- [ ] Filter every user-entered string with Class.TextService:FilterStringAsync() before display, and filter every preset before sending. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
