- [ ] Review the AI-training data sharing setting on Creator Hub. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Ask the rights holder (or their authorized representative) to file any takedown. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Do not rely on archiving to avoid IP takedowns or penalties. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Teach students reporting tools before online play. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Do not create UGC items that closely copy Roblox-created Limiteds. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Keep UGC items free of sexual, political, religious, gory, violent, drug or self-harm themes; avoid excessive text. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] State game rules visibly to all players and apply them consistently. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Review local regulations before launching in a new market; check current restricted regions. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Configure automated right-to-be-forgotten processing; verify all matching player data is removed. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Avoid suggestive emotes, poses and animations (for example twerking or 'sus' emotes). [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Account for the roughly 30,000-game Kids and Select catalog when estimating discovery competition for Kids-targeted games. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Before running a giveaway or prize promotion, have legal check the rules for each country you target, especially for tangible prizes. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Write giveaway wording specifically: who can enter, what the prize is, and how winners are selected. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Do not launch a prize promotion in a market until its rules have been reviewed. [age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]
- [ ] Check Creator Hub > Overview benchmark scorecard weekly (needs 100+ DAU). [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Track payer conversion, ARPPU and ARPDAU together. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Set alerts: baseline 7-14 days, thresholds above normal, duration 5, one alert with Breakdown. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Check MDE and run the full 14+ days; act only on significant results. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Export profiling data as JSON to compare sessions outside Studio (T8857). [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Check network stats in the Developer Console; add buffers only above about 50 KB/s (T6159, N2548). [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Keep timestamps on every exported dataset so periods can be compared. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Set alerts with consecutiveOccurrences to avoid firing on one-off spikes. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Use PeriodOverPeriod with a 7-day offset to compare each day with the same weekday last week. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Keep alerts at or below 20 per experience; delete unused ones before creating new. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Stay within 60 requests/min per key per endpoint; on 429, shorten the range or coarsen granularity. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Use granularity None with a limit to rank top-N series, then query those values for display. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Do not sum daily uniques into weekly or monthly totals; buckets are distinct-user counts. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Preview matchmaking weight changes on mock data before publishing. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Check the like ratio over a recent post-fix window, not lifetime totals. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Track visits versus unique players to gauge repeat play. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Watch the like ratio early as a warning before scaling spend. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Break DAU growth down by country and region, especially US/Canada vs Japan/India and APAC. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Check whether session length includes AFK or auto-mode time before reporting it. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Track favorites per 1,000 visits and like ratio weekly, and compare against a percentile band. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Estimate DAU/MAU for your game and compare with platform DAU/MAU (about 38%, derived from Q4 2025 figures). [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Label every tracker or press figure with its source and date before using it in a report. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Compare quarter-on-quarter and year-on-year figures with awareness of 2025 hit-driven base effects. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Plot CCU and DAU weekly or monthly for at least 8 weeks before judging a game's trajectory. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] For idle or hatch-based games, log income per second before and after each hatch, booster, or large spend. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Label every figure as official, third-party tracker, or creator self-reported when you cite it. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Verify third-party estimates (Newzoo, Sensor Tower, creator-exchange) before quoting them. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Record plays, visits and CCU as separate fields when noting a game's scale (N7791, N7792, N7794). [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Keep speaker earnings guesses out of benchmark tables (N7841). [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Report creator installs at day 30, day 90, day 365 and lifetime, not only day 7 or 30. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Log organic installs per market for the weeks before, during and after each campaign to build a proxy uplift model. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Require 10+ videos and about 500k combined views per vertical before comparing verticals. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Score the feature backlog as estimated LTV impact divided by effort (1-10) and sort descending. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Log the game type, packaging and traffic source that coincided with any traction spike, so the cause can be repeated. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Model ROAS at campaign and creative level rather than relying on blended ROAS. [analytics-metrics]
- [ ] Use FloorMaterial and airborne timers for fly checks; do not check PlatformStanding or Running (T5588, T5585). [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Keep a suspicion score and escalation ladder; delay visible consequences (T9137, T9138). [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Use BanAsync only for confirmed persistent offenders; use reversible actions otherwise (T9140, T9129). [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Sandbox third-party models: Workspace SandboxedInstance Experimental, then Sandboxed=true (T7024). [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Print GetFullName() of all LuaSourceContainers and show hidden objects in Explorer (T5242, T5243). [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Never send secrets via TeleportData; use Secure within universe only for restricted places (T8480, T8477). [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Verify webhook HMAC signatures and timestamps; dedupe by NotificationId (T8966, T8967). [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Make repeated actions idempotent so slow connections cannot double-submit (T9674). [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Before granting a pickup, check item exists, player loaded, parent ~= nil, and distance (about 5 to 10 studs). [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Call SetNetworkOwner(nil) on physics NPCs and items; anchor HumanoidRootPart on NPCs that need no physics. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Do not nil a character's network owner; it makes the character unresponsive. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Use game:GetService for every service lookup. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Clamp dash velocity to fixed values and key cooldowns by UserId. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Destroy or hide admin-only GUIs (e.g. Freecam) from PlayerGui on every respawn for non-privileged players. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Reject double-control requests on any seat, desk or station. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Move dev and key accounts off SMS 2FA to an authenticator app or hardware security key. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Remove phone numbers from critical accounts where possible, and add a PIN with the carrier to block number transfers. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Use a unique email per service and protect the primary email with 2FA plus security keys. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Do not poll the Safety Callback API; handle the pushed callbacks. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] On an unattributed callback, delete or hide the instances returned in the report. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Evaluate the Reviewable Content API (early access) for pre-screening player creations before publication. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] For block place or break requests, reject immediately if the player's character is missing. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Measure reach by magnitude from HumanoidRootPart to the target position, not with DistanceFromCharacter. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Set a max placement and break distance constant (25 studs was the example value) and tune it per game. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] After the grid check, run a ground or collision check so blocks cannot be phased into terrain. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Before acting on a block, confirm it is a BasePart, has the expected name, and sits inside the blocks folder. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Before publishing, audit place access and team member permissions; if every member can access the place, anyone with a member account can enter it. [anti-exploit-and-security]
- [ ] Confirm your game is public [brand-deals-and-ip-licensing]
- [ ] Choose a full-game or in-game sales license (perpetual or time-limited) [brand-deals-and-ip-licensing]
- [ ] Confirm no conflicting IP license on the game (one IP per game) [brand-deals-and-ip-licensing]
- [ ] Attach references, mockups or sketches [brand-deals-and-ip-licensing]
- [ ] Do not use the IP before the start date [brand-deals-and-ip-licensing]
- [ ] Cancel at least 72 hours before start if you cannot make it [brand-deals-and-ip-licensing]
- [ ] Make requested conditional changes and wait for rights holder approval [brand-deals-and-ip-licensing]
- [ ] Track the expiry date [brand-deals-and-ip-licensing]
- [ ] Apply to Brand Developer Directory or Brand Link if you have brand experience [brand-deals-and-ip-licensing]
- [ ] Keep a dated record of your original designs, characters and logos to support any clone or infringement claim. [brand-deals-and-ip-licensing]
- [ ] Write a lore and backstory document during development, not after launch. [brand-deals-and-ip-licensing]
- [ ] For avatar item brand deals, get design credit, distribution scope, royalty rate and term in writing before designing. [brand-deals-and-ip-licensing]
- [ ] Collect a few dozen reference photos before building. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Greybox with basic parts; no textures or props until the map plays fun. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Import heightmaps as .jpg or .png no larger than 4096x4096 px; plan 1 px = 4 studs. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Keep part sizes small enough to convert to Terrain; check size before converting. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Align builds to the 4-stud voxel grid and set snap values to the kit (e.g., 4 or 7.5 studs, 45 or 90 degree rotation). [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Set one consistent pivot on every modular piece and move one mesh at a time. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Set LightingStyle Realistic, make indoor walls at least 1 stud thick, and tint Ambient warm. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Use PointLight, SpotLight or SurfaceLight depending on the source shape. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Verify lighting fixes persist across the session, not only at one ClockTime. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Add cover, second floors and balconies so each view has something to look at. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Use invisible walls at map edges and mark off-limits areas with coloured bricks. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Keep height steps of 30+ studs between jump tiers and check each jump from multiple angles. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Color-code team zones and indoor/outdoor materials so players can orient themselves. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Use folders for sections and models for sets; rename everything on creation. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Decide symmetry by game type: duplicate-and-rotate for competitive arenas, varied layouts for scenic maps. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Use Explorer, not the viewport, to select parts. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Work from top view with arrow handles; keep increments on; if an object won't snap, delete and duplicate a fresh one. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Audit every room systematically for missing assets, not just the one reported. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Verify doorways are at least 10 studs wide and walls at least 10 studs tall. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Give spawn zones two exits; cap combat pockets at three exits or fewer. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Add elevation changes at key sight lines and elevated walkways in PvP maps. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Build one half or one plot, then duplicate it for equal access. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Sculpt with Add, Subtract, Smooth and Paint; use Ctrl+Z for mistakes. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Break hard straight terrain edges and vary slopes with curves. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Set plot size to a multiple of grid size; set SmartDisplay false unless grid is under 5 studs. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Set procedural noise: abs octaves, then square; colour by height threshold. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Flatten procedural water with inverse lerp; set Part Y size to y*2+1 for solid columns. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Use Terrain for outdoor ground; convert blocks with Part To Terrain. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Anchor welded props and floating triggers; name spawns 'spawn' and region parts after matching sounds. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Set team spawn TeamColor and turn SpawnLocation Neutral off. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Cut part counts with wall shapes and unions; check wireframe triangle counts. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Batch GeometryService unions and re-parent results to the main part's parent. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Check the map has room for the target player count before scaling past 50 users. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Set every invisible zone part to Anchored, CanCollide false, Transparency 1, and tall enough to block escape above. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Keep Atmosphere Haze (0-10) and Glare (0-10) in range; Glare needs Haze and Lighting Brightness above 0. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Plan chunk size (one creator used 1024 studs) and key chunks by x/y/z index before the map grows large. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Delete inactive welds in models that never need to be destroyed. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Build destructible walls from many small blocks, not one part. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Stack parts for large walls when a single part hits the size limit. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Set conveyor velocity on the actual belt axis and flip the sign if needed. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Add transparent collidable Truss Ladders behind visible ladders and WedgePart ramps over tall stairs. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Match door visuals to their collision; no ghost doors. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Name door folders to match PathfindingLink labels. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Place fast-travel points before major bosses and across large maps. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Add on-screen objective markers and keep fog light enough to preserve direction. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Make every hub prop functional or purposeful. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Theme and recolour each map to the game concept. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Add many spawn zones and space bases apart to limit stealing. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Sketch a top-down HTML blueprint with at least three escape routes before placing parts. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Consider a level-select menu on join and a build mode for player-made maps if the game supports them. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Measure visible triangle count from main player viewpoints, not whole-map totals. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Re-check visible triangle count after adding walls or buildings, since occlusion can hide geometry. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Keep draw call count in mind when adding extra terrain pieces. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Split external meshes into named parts when parts need separate behaviour or textures. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Build one plot or enclosure as a reusable template with fences and a sign, then duplicate it around the map to fill space fast (T17797). [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Once the objective is shown to players, check whether any part of the map is never visited and cut or shrink it (T17828). [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Multi-select placed block parts and anchor them in bulk so obby platforms don't drift or fall [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Make roofs destructible when houses are part of a survival or elimination game [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Check elevator speed against the time players need to reach the top or escape a hazard [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Add floor markers in large lobbies to guide players to the next location [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Apply the lighting setup early, before finishing the map, then judge builds under that lighting. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Separate door models into parts and define hitboxes before animating. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Scatter low-poly grass with the brush and adjust density visually. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Place secrets or collectibles in each new area. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Keep static map parts as unions, separate from physics objects. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Place enough spawn parts per map. AlvinBlox used 11 in a desert map example. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Set the size limit before building and design directly to it. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Furnish spawn-in interiors fully and to scale. [building-and-level-design]
- [ ] Write an art bible and UI style sheet (palette, fonts, padding about 15px, corner radius 6px) [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Pick palette, lighting and texture style early [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Check silhouettes by filling models flat black [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Keep touch targets large and actions in thumb zones, clear of Roblox restricted areas [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Set TextScaled with min/max size limits [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Scale interactive UI about 1.5x for TV [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Use string tables and localization format strings [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Check contrast with a WCAG calculator and avoid colour-only signals [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Use Atmosphere and post-processing lighting [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Use simple collision fidelity and avoid partial transparency [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Keep UGC items under 4,000 triangles and unwrap UVs early [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Reuse and reskin existing assets [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Organise asset packs with a ReadMe and a preview experience [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Verify AI-generated UI and builds visually [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Caption or visually signal every gameplay-relevant sound cue. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Replace TextScaled with AutomaticSize and TextWrapped where text should grow. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Check text contrast; small text needs higher contrast than large or bold text. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Use ScrollingFrame on large displays to reduce clutter. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Add console shortcuts for essential actions. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Play selection and navigation sounds for controller menus. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Collapse secondary buttons into a menu on small screens. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Compact-format numbers from roughly 1,000 upward (e.g. 5,000 to 5k). [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Keep item and pet text in data tables with generic fallbacks. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Keep 3D accessories simple, non-animated, single-attach-point. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Prototype menu flow and input controls on paper before Studio. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Before export, check triangle count and bounds: 12 to 2,000 triangles for ordinary props; under 10,000 per MeshPart and under 2,000 units per axis. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Put Textures and decals only on faces players see; prefer SurfaceAppearance or built-in materials. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] In Blender: Unit System None, apply transforms and modifiers, delete lights, cameras and mannequins, and embed textures in the FBX. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Verify root orientation (green up) before rigging; check welds after import. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Anchor every placed or spawned model; re-anchor after any physics action. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Disable CastShadow on small or rarely seen parts; set CollisionFidelity to Box on non-interactable parts. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Convert unions copied several times to MeshParts via Export Selection. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Enable Expanded Terrain before custom terrain materials; replace a terrain material before deleting it. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Generate the base terrain, then refine with Sculpt at Strength 0.1 to 1. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Set LightingStyle on purpose and set only one of ClockTime or TimeOfDay. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Tint Ambient, OutdoorAmbient and ColorShift_Top together to hold one palette. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Start PBR materials from the baselines and keep Metalness at 0 except for true metals. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Build variants by reskinning one base model (for example three pet variants or many crate skins). [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Pick one grid increment per task (0.2/0.25 fine, 0.5-1 detail, 5-10 big walls) and keep it until the piece is finished. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Offset added detail by 0.04 studs on a 0.2 grid or 0.05 on a 0.25 grid; never leave coplanar overlaps. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Place the Build Rig dummy and confirm doorways and windows are human-scale before building further. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Before a new area, write the palette as 2-3 base colours plus dark and light variants. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] For item previews, put the unit model in a WorldModel inside the ViewportFrame template; clone a basic character with no accessories. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Export each UI element as a separate PNG, colour symbols white for tinting, and clean dark edges with Pixelfix. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Split large rotations into steps of about 72 degrees and add buffer keyframes past start and end markers. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Weld the assembled tool and animate the whole assembly rather than tweening individually welded pieces. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Resize a Handle by editing its Size property rather than with the resize tool. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Stay inside Marketplace limits: body parts under 10,000 tris, rigid accessory a single mesh at most 4k tris, textures at most 2048x2048. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Name the accessory's central part exactly 'Handle' and its positioning attachment 'HatAttachment'. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Weld all accessory parts together before publishing; unwelded parts are dropped on equip. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Check triangle count with the Statistics overlay; keep UGC meshes at or under about 3,990 tris. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Dissolve flat-surface edges and run Merge by Distance (vertex select, A, M) to trim triangles. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Delete hidden back faces and parts buried inside other geometry before exporting. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Lower hair curve resolution (about 12 to 6) before converting to mesh; merge by centre to close gaps. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Turn on Face Orientation to find red inverted faces; fix with Shift+N. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Rotate UVs 90 degrees if a texture appears flipped on the mesh. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Export textures at 2K in FBX format. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Verify the fit in the Avatar Editor: the Face-bounds box should turn blue before Generate Mesh Part Accessory. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Export the R6 block dummy as OBJ for a fitting guide. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Bind accessories to bones: select item, shift-select bone in Pose Mode, Ctrl+P > Bone. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Re-check lighting at every time of day after any lighting-tech change. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Use SpotLights, not SurfaceLights, where shadows matter (verify current support). [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Use high-quality references for photobashing; low-resolution sources give poor results. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Match colour on a flat-tone layer before blending photobashed pieces. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Model low-poly and push detail into baked normals and textures to keep draw calls low. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] For primitive-based builds, keep simulation parts simple and use upres only for visual detail. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] For repeated trees, fences, or buildings, build one procedural model with attributes and vary parameters. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Design interactive props with their behaviour defined, not just their shape. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Verify availability of procedural, retexture, upres, and collision features in Studio before advising a team to depend on them. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Set the accessory Handle to the same size (about X≈1.45) on every item in a set. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Create colour variants by tinting one mesh (colour lerp or hue cycle), not by cloning meshes. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Enable an orientation indicator before building oval or template parts, and set template pivots at the joint. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] When code resizes a part, recompute PivotOffset from half the new scaled length. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Build placement previews as cloned parts at Transparency 0.5 with CanCollide and CanQuery false. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Offset placed parts along the raycast Normal by half the block size. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Give repeated obstacle tweens random durations and place them on a set platform interval. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Check that interactive highlights do not hide the object and that rooms are not too dark to navigate. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Tie smoke and particle effects to vehicle state. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Bind custom camera code at Camera.Value + 1 via RenderStep. [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] For jelly-themed pets, use slight transparency and add face textures in Studio (T18562, low-advice). [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Colour simple props directly in Studio; open Blender only for shapes Studio cannot handle (T18615). [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] For a basic map, recolour, scale and clone vetted studded Toolbox parts by hand (T18643). [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Set PrimaryPart on each pet and vehicle model, then verify eggs/pets or vehicles configure and move correctly in Studio (T18772, T18832). [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Check each imported model's facing direction and rotate it (e.g. 90 degrees) if it is wrong (T18759). [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Texture each model separately rather than baking textures together across assets (T18830). [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Keep window and glass parts white in Blender and apply colour and glow in Studio (T18861). [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Simulate glowing neon with the Neon material and colour variants, since Roblox lacks emissive textures (T18863). [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Use Substance Painter for logo and text decals on detailed items, and texture stylised props in Blender (T18864). [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Allow extra modelling time for complex mesh shapes that are harder to build (T18866). [building-art-and-assets]
- [ ] Add per-player rate limits (last-fired timestamp, 0.1 s example) and kick or drop spam (T5610). [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Replicate player profile data with ReplicaService or a generic module; mark sensitive keys private (T5578, T9850). [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Disconnect connections with Disconnect(), Once() or Trove when no longer needed (T5500, T5738). [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Under rollback, detect contacts with GetTouchingParts in BindToSimulation rather than Touched. [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] On PlayerAdded, send current world state (weather, timers, camera lock) to that player only. [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Check ResetOnSpawn before assuming PlayerGui persists across respawns. [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] For async turn-based games, request push-notification opt-in and fall back to in-game turn indicators when declined (T17675). [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Clone template tools to give them to players; do not move the source copy. [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Replace direct game.ServiceName references for non-main services with game:GetService('ServiceName'). [client-server-and-remotes]
- [ ] Before adding inheritance, ask whether composition (has-a components) works; default to composition. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Don't inherit from Roblox Instances; wrap the model in a reference and pass it into new(). [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Give every object a Trove, Janitor or Maid, and make destroy() a single cleanup call that disconnects connections. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Wrap yielding APIs (TweenService) in Promises with onCancel and cancel on Destroying. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Use attributes for per-object tunables created in Studio; use config modules when many objects share values. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Decide on Knit or a module loader only after weighing singleton coupling; Knit suits service/controller organisation. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Use Trove, Janitor or Maid for all cleanup; prefer Wally installation over copy-paste. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Keep methods on the class table and per-object data on self; export the class type from the constructor. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Subclass only for a shared behaviour family, and call the parent __init explicitly. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Clone definition data per instance and never write to the shared data table at runtime. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Before coding a feature, list its subsystems and the state machine, then write one set_state with early return on same-state. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Gate actions with a state check at the top and return early. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Add Promises to a Trove with AddPromise and run cleanup in finally. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Use Janitor :Add(obj, index) when you may need to remove one item early. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Document preconditions, postconditions, return values, side effects and yields for each public function. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Use table.insert for arrays and table.remove(queue, 1) for a FIFO queue. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Model permission checks as functions taking a Player and returning a boolean; chain them with one/all operators. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Create UI state inside the component function so instances do not share it. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Scope Fusion instances and state with fusion.scoped and alias repeated Fusion calls to locals. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Check the installed Fusion version before choosing peek() versus :get(). [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Place doc comments directly above each function with no blank line. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Check a vetted utility set (e.g. RbxUtil) before writing common helpers yourself. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] When reusing an internal framework, confirm it has no game-specific logic left in it before copying it into a new project. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Put effect code in a Changed handler on the state value, not in each trigger block. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] When a random pick breaks a rule, move selection into its own function and re-roll rather than nesting ifs. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Join related rejection conditions with 'or' in one if block. [code-architecture-and-frameworks]
- [ ] Define attack phases (anticipation, contact, recovery) and tune each separately. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Decide whether attacks are cancellable; tune enemy aggression to match. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Use hold and directional inputs to expand one attack button. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Add stun-lock protection: poise, stamina, multiple attackers, or uninterruptible attacks. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Telegraph enemy attacks with silhouette, colour, wind-up and sound/visual cue. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Ensure no enemy is killable by only one move. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Filter Touched with IsDescendantOf for self-hit protection. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Use FastCast or predictive raycasts for bullets; handle nil raycasts with origin + direction. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Cap inventory with a count check against capacity. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Give each ability a unique Name; never put inputs in RunsWhile. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Share counterIds across tasks that track the same progress. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Only add an anti-combat-log if dying carries a real penalty. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Set CanTouch=false on any part that collision detection should ignore. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Make ability hitboxes non-collidable, non-anchored, massless, transparent and welded to HumanoidRootPart. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Clean up temporary hitbox clones with Debris after about 6 s. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Set a per-hitbox DebounceTime (for example 1 s) and keep a per-attack hit table. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Check Humanoid.Health > 0 before an NPC chases a target. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Destroy spawner marker parts after reading them. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Destroy existing spawned NPCs or items before respawning. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Use weighted random picks: sum weights, roll math.random scaled to the total, walk cumulative weights. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Set Player.Team directly by unchecking AutoAssignable on forced teams. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Cap equipped tools and abilities (2 each in cited examples) and require unequip before equip. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Check that optional objectives do not occupy slots needed for healing or other core survival items. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Confirm stamina regen and ammo supply are scarce enough that dash, push and ranged attacks are worth using. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Make each gear tier change behaviour, not just stats, so higher tiers do not simply outclass earlier ones. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Gate stat gain behind proximity to training objects if you want to block idle farming. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Add a per-player post-match cooldown on arena entry if you run queued matches. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Make a successful block fully negate the hit; do not let ordinary moves bypass it (T17808). [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Show a brief pause and sound cue before each QTE prompt; keep one input type per prompt (T17837). [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Restrict stealing to set times of day and return stolen items to the owner's plot when the thief is hit (T17803, low-advice). [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Add conditional passives and gated choices to skill trees to add build variety (T17955, low-advice). [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Give players a cheap knockback tool for PvP defence with a sound effect (T17802, low-advice). [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] After a knockback ends, cast a short forward ray to decide whether to apply a wall stun. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Give every uncollected loot drop a Debris lifetime (example: 20 s). [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] For stackable resources, grow a visual stack and display the count. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] When tuning a boss, define each player hit as a fraction of its max health (example: 1/3) rather than a fixed number, then check it against your fight length. [combat-and-gameplay-systems]
- [ ] Anchor parts and use original assets, not free models [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Add referral limits and cooldowns [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Avoid third-party IP; get licences [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Run experiments for the full planned duration [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Use GetConfigForPlayerAsync and GetValue correctly [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Get contracts for collaborators; avoid company partnerships [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Link a verified email and secure the account [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Keep UI consistent with a limited palette, and places under 100 MB [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Use ipairs for ordered arrays and pairs for sparse tables; never rely on pairs order for leaderboards. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Clear all references and call Instance:Destroy() on instances you are done with; disconnect connections. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Replace spawn() with task.spawn, task.delay or coroutines. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Anchor each placed map part and re-check positions and orientations after building. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Add invisible boundary walls at launch so players cannot fall through the map. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Set team spawns explicitly so teammates do not spawn together by default. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Disable Team Create before reverting a place version. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Check Open Use and game permissions before setting assets, since Open Use is one-way. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Parse both error shapes from Open Cloud and wrap calls in pcall. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Set pathfinding material costs and keep requests under 3,000 studs straight-line. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Before building, write down who will find the game and through which channels (T13738, Q1420). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Verify every audio and model licence; 'royalty free' is not a licence (T11574, T11575). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Migrate CameraAction to GetCameraState and FilterDescendantsInstances before deprecation (T13566, T13277). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Unanchor every NPC BasePart and remove player-specific Animate hooks (T11414, T11548). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Delete old effects and clone connections on respawn (T12111, T12200). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Set PrimaryPart to a transparent hitbox and name attachments uniquely (T10748, T10778). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Name the leaderstats folder exactly 'leaderstats' in lowercase, or the leaderboard will not display it (Amora). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Fusion: check for stray or extra commas and make sure the component function returns the instance (Possible Panda). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Keep every accessory part unanchored, or the character gets stuck (Emancyphur). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Pay for a game transfer only after ownership is confirmed, or use escrow (LovJak). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Ask what each object needs to do before modelling systems; prefer one raycast where it suffices (Roox4). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Check that each map and mission template has distinct objectives and visuals (Cumin). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Agree scope and asset approval rules with sponsors before work starts (Last Level Studios). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Gate admin commands and tell players why; CORSO saw many quits after admin tools arrived (CORSO). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Explain the game with clear in-game communication rather than relying on fan wikis (Cumin, CORSO). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Before publishing a concept close to a top game, change the core mechanic and replace borrowed art, not just the name. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Audit any external video, social or link embeds against Roblox's current approved list before adding them. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Label any bookings-based figure as bookings, and don't compare it directly to revenue-based margins. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Schedule a recurring review of safety filters against new bypasses such as coded speech. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] For any one-shot or irreversible mission action, show the consequence before the player commits. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Avoid forced full-restart punishments for a single mistake in missions or quests. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Treat claims about platform recommendation rules from creators as unverified until checked against official docs. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Accept simple, working hidden scaffolding; polish only what players see. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Before adding cosmetics, confirm there is a social layer where status and player interaction matter. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Do not count on cosmetics for revenue in single-player games. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Keep each channel focused on 1–3 games. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Watch the output console from early builds onward and fix error floods at their root line (T18764). [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Define the intended play area and keep core gameplay, objectives and spawns inside it. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Block or clearly warn players who leave the intended play area, rather than letting core play happen outside it. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Check competition or judged entries for gameplay outside the defined bounds before submitting. [common-mistakes]
- [ ] Pick one main social platform [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Run polls and Q&As; ask what players want next [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Block trolls; remove hateful content [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Notify affected players with apology, fix and compensation [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Watch creator videos to find clippable moments [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Plan a content buffer for downtime [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Ask players why they want a suggested feature before building it. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Before asking for help, search the Developer Hub, Stack Overflow, Lua.org and the DevForum. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Nominate 3-5 helpers at session start and rotate them. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Agree collaboration rules before a build, co-create them, and state consequences. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Use Studio collaboration features so each person's contributions are traceable. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Prompt players in-game to favourite the game. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Use rate-limited bulletin boards to give players a social outlet. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Make the game Public in Experience Settings before sharing links with friends. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Document your own appeal process for API bans, since Roblox redirects those to you. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Write player-facing publish notes for each version. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Assign moderators with escalation paths and keep bans and config permissions with the creator. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Pick moderators by suitability, not friendship. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Route duplicate suggestions to a merging and voting tool. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Open-source reusable modules with a backup copy and linked articles. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Collect applications through a shared form linked to a spreadsheet. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Confirm whether the Creator Hub Player Support tool is available on your account before setting up a separate support ticket flow. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] When planning a themed jam, set a short build window and a prize pool, then publish the judging criteria up front. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Pair with a peer at a similar skill level and review each other's mistakes on a fixed schedule. [community-and-discord]
- [ ] Create an allowlist of actions that automated actors may perform and block everything else. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Reserve dynamic behaviour for unusual or edge-case situations; keep normal play under designer control. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Check thresholds against current income, not launch-day income. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Scale guard count and threat with each new area. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Keep rare items hard to obtain; add rarity modifiers to common outputs. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Use spawn as the default respawn point after death. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Add sell-all and equip-best buttons for large inventories. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Plan growth-timer systems (multi-harvest crops) during core design. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Add relief pickups between threat waves. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Target threats at players who camp edges so the map stays dynamic. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] If spawn luck causes a difficulty spike, spawn threats near players. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Vary room layouts and item spawns; cut key-hunting padding. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Show a guiding marker or clear next step when players stall. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Turn-based: tick statuses and buffs at the end of the affected unit's turn. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Turn-based: use one time-based gate per action; don't stack energy and cooldown. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Turn-based: give players a persistent resource or distinct strategies. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Turn-based: make bosses change mechanics, not just HP. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Multiplayer turn-based: resolve team turns together, then enemies, with visible queued actions. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Use a volume the player stands inside for trigger zones; standing-on-platform detection proved unreliable. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Held items lose collision with the player; design stacking or standing on them explicitly. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Confirm matchmaking and respawn are instant so the session stays in flow. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] For simulator-style games, plan secret codes, exploration and a rare collectible tier beyond the main rising number. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Clone spawned items from a template list into one folder so cleanup and counting stay simple. [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Give every weighted table entry a weight value, using 0 for entries that should never be random (e.g. start room). [core-loops-and-game-design]
- [ ] Make every prompt tappable on touch regardless of ClickablePrompt. [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] Run Device Simulator across screen sizes, custom dimensions and pixel density before publishing. [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] Set KeyboardKeyCode and GamepadKeyCode on prompts so the correct glyph shows per device. [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] Add a GamepadConnected/GamepadDisconnected or GamepadEnabled fallback if PreferredInput is not enough. [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] Bind jump, sprint, shoot and similar actions through the Input Action System with multiple bindings each. [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] Set GuiService.TouchControlsEnabled to false only when you supply your own touch controls. [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] Use Delta from InputChanged (MouseMovement) for mouse-locked movement. [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] Override default controls (movement, camera, reserved keys) only when there is a specific reason. [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] Do not make grass motion the only cue for gameplay-critical information. [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] Enable every target device (phones, computers, tablets, consoles, VR) in the Devices section at publish time. [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] Keep the VR checkbox enabled if you want PC VR availability. [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] For custom VR hands, disable controller models and laser pointer, then drive hands from UserCFrameChanged. [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] Anchor HumanoidRootPart in VR experiences that should not show the default character. [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] Check AccelerometerEnabled and GyroscopeEnabled before using motion or gyro input. [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] Check TouchSwipe and TouchPinch support before relying on gestures. [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] Set emoteBarPosLandscape and emoteBarPosPortrait separately. [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] Confirm cross-play works with the shared codebase; do not fork separate builds without a reason. [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] Verify UI and input per platform before publishing, not only on the primary platform. [cross-platform-and-console]
- [ ] Convert Vector3 to X,Y,Z numbers and CFrame to GetComponents arrays before saving; do not pre-JSONEncode. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Use exponential backoff on retries (5 attempts, 2 s base, 32 s max). [data-persistence]
- [ ] Snapshot the universe's data daily and before publishing data-logic changes. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Clear per-player tables and nil Instance references on PlayerRemoving. [data-persistence]
- [ ] At the top of every data-touching handler, return early if the profile is nil. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Wait for CharacterAdded before moving a character to its loaded position. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Use weak-keyed or weak-valued tables for caches that hold player references. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Deep-copy the defaults template per player before Reconcile. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Set a player attribute for connection state and show the warning only after about 5 s. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Set session lock attempts to 3–5 and lock interval to 30–60 s. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Before deleting a pack or item, confirm its open or award action has completed successfully. [data-persistence]
- [ ] Convert the GDD into per-discipline Trello/Jira lists with owners and deadlines [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Paper-prototype UI/rules; Studio-prototype risky mechanics [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Put all text in localization tables from day one [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Create a reusable game template [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Spend ~80% on game development, ~20% on marketing [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Start with free Studio and built-in services; no upfront tooling cost is needed. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Check badge count against the 5-free-per-24h GMT limit before creating badges; batch creation. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Leave Localization auto-capture on so game text lands in a translation table early. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Use only assets you own or are licensed to use. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Hold a daily (minimum twice-weekly) team review of completed and planned tasks. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Define milestones (prototype, vertical slice, MVP) and record which one you are at. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Count content items times variants (e.g. designs x variants) before starting art. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Mark any feature likely to exceed 2-3 weeks and plan a cheap stand-in for it. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Duplicate the place or project before risky refactors; keep backups. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Keep a template place with standard structure, modules, configs and services for new projects. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Keep a shared to-do list anyone can pick up tasks from. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Write a hand-off list when passing work between developers and cross-review code. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Ask AI to simplify scope when the plan is too large; add features one prompt at a time. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Review AI-generated code and fall back to official docs when stuck. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Remember a public app cannot go back to private; plan resubmission for later edits. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Write a user story for each feature in Who / When / Why form before assigning it. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Document asset specs (hit parts, poly count, style) before modelers start, so output drops into code without rework. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Never let a project sit untouched for more than two days in a row. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Tidy the workspace into named folders at the end of every session. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Make one small change at a time and undo the last one if the project breaks. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Customise shortcuts (File > Customize Shortcuts) and use the Studio Settings search (Alt+S / Option+S). [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Report API gaps to the DevForum with a use case and a repro. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Use one main AI session for integration; don't run several sessions editing the same code. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Split the design doc into tabs or sections per area (systems, builds, animations, attributes). [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Put each task on a board with a checklist, and set a fixed deadline on each card with notifications on. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Enable Team Create per place and keep Collaborative Editing on. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Outsource a core system after your own attempt keeps failing. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Keep a working prototype small and presentable so it can be shown to a potential partner or collaborator. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Run Scene Analysis during each build session, not just before launch. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Ask AI to break down a proven reference game's systems before you start building. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] In a timed build, limit scope to one core mechanic. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] For a phone-built game, plan a single-player scope from the start. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Use the rename-symbol refactor in Studio when renaming a variable or symbol. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Write the game's selling points and what the player and game do for each before drafting the design doc. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Practise in Studio for 2-3 hours a day on buttons and simple maps, more if possible. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Once small single-skill projects work, combine skills into a bigger game. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Log total build hours per project so future scope estimates use real figures. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Build a simple lobby before the main game, and place several transparent, non-collidable spawn locations around it so players do not stack on one point. [development-process-and-speed]
- [ ] Confirm Exclude from Recommendations is off [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] Watch Home recommendation impressions daily; do not trust one day [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] Maintain at least 25 HEP with buffer [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] If no Home recs in 7-14 days, reassess or move on [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] Nominate for Standout Games; apply to Learn & Explore if educational [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] Review Creator Hub Discovery documentation [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] Treat chart and player-count snapshots as directional context only; log the date and chart type. [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] Benchmark placements against the 'Most Recommended by Roblox' table (as of Aug 20, 2026) to see which games Roblox is pushing. [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] Check Home recommendations in the acquisition tab to confirm the game has entered the algorithm. [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] Do not use concurrent player count as a ranking input; it is not a signal to optimise. [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] Wait about a week after launch or after a change before judging the algorithm result. [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] Use a rolling 7-day window when comparing stats before and after a fix. [discovery-algorithm]
- [ ] Add limited-time items, rare spawns or mutations [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] Frame return incentives as loss of a limited item [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] Prefer skill-based giveaways over random draws [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] Align drops with seasonal moments [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] Nominate seasonal items for Today's Picks before early November [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] Connect cleanup to MatchEnd [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] Run a diary study before launch [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] Consider a game jam entry [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] Consider time-limited IP licences for crossovers [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] After planning a collab, schedule follow-up content to counter the post-spike CCU drop. [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] Keep seasonal scenery running about 1-2 weeks before removing it (T18558). [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] Verify giveaway race duration before publishing, since the source is internally inconsistent (N8002). [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] When a timed multiplier is active, display each boost type and its value on screen. [events-and-seasonal-content]
- [ ] Explain why the player should care [first-session-onboarding]
- [ ] Keep lobbies short and give something to do [first-session-onboarding]
- [ ] Add glowing story notes or other diegetic cues on interactable props that point toward the exit or objective. [first-session-onboarding]
- [ ] Remove spawn forcefield protection and check that spawns look intentional. [first-session-onboarding]
- [ ] For team games: set Teams AutoAssignable on, give each team a unique TeamColor, set spawn Neutral off, and enable AllowTeamChangeOnTouch. [first-session-onboarding]
- [ ] Show each ability's effect in the menu and in the battle UI before players spend turns or energy. [first-session-onboarding]
- [ ] Add an info button that re-displays the next objective and points to it (T18619). [first-session-onboarding]
- [ ] Before launch, verify the next-objective pointer from spawn, after respawn, and on every target platform (T18619). [first-session-onboarding]
- [ ] Define the core fantasy: role, actions, feeling [game-ideas-and-genres]
- [ ] Pick one hook, not five features [game-ideas-and-genres]
- [ ] Combine 2-3 inspirations, including non-Roblox ones [game-ideas-and-genres]
- [ ] Decide at the start whether the game is public or friends-only; friends-only expects about 4 players versus about 10,000 typical reach. [game-ideas-and-genres]
- [ ] Attach an avatar, social or cross-platform element to a proven mechanic to form the mash-up. [game-ideas-and-genres]
- [ ] Choose one distinctive visual style choice that makes the game recognizably Roblox-styled yet unique. [game-ideas-and-genres]
- [ ] Re-check GamesBeat program claims against current official Roblox sources before building around them. [game-ideas-and-genres]
- [ ] For extraction/collection games, check that real trade-offs exist: vary item weight or fragility and confirm the highest-value item isn't always the obvious pick. (T17826) [game-ideas-and-genres]
- [ ] Before building a copy of a hit's mechanic, identify a second layer (endgame, differentiator) that a copycat can't trivially match. (T17980, T17985) [game-ideas-and-genres]
- [ ] When making a sequel to a hit, add one clear differentiator, such as night-time stealing from other players' plots. (T17796) [game-ideas-and-genres]
- [ ] Identify a mechanic from a top-grossing game that has proven itself, and copy the mechanic rather than the whole game (T18447). [game-ideas-and-genres]
- [ ] Check that Up and Coming games in the same style exist before committing to the style (T18638). [game-ideas-and-genres]
- [ ] When copying a trending format, add a clear twist so the result is not a direct clone (T18688, T18702). [game-ideas-and-genres]
- [ ] Before building around a current trend, confirm it is still rising. T18896's grow-a-garden-style example dates from 2025-07, so it may already be stale by late 2026. [game-ideas-and-genres]
- [ ] Horror: avoid big pure-dark zones, build gradually, sparing jumpscares [genre-mechanics]
- [ ] Make final objective visible (neon part) [genre-mechanics]
- [ ] Name difficulties Normal/Hard/Extreme [genre-mechanics]
- [ ] Add easter eggs with badges [genre-mechanics]
- [ ] Vary enemy behaviours and dungeon designs [genre-mechanics]
- [ ] Add target priority modes in tower defense [genre-mechanics]
- [ ] Keep status effects simple [genre-mechanics]
- [ ] Use Raycast for projectiles [genre-mechanics]
- [ ] Use constraints, not BodyMovers [genre-mechanics]
- [ ] Keep jumpable obstacles low (~3.5 studs) [genre-mechanics]
- [ ] Configure ChargeRate for charging weapons; use cooldowns [genre-mechanics]
- [ ] Provide real practice mode and bestiary [genre-mechanics]
- [ ] In chase sequences, mark wrong paths with visible cues (lighting, signs, props, barriers) and avoid invisible walls as the only signal. [genre-mechanics]
- [ ] Set a maximum enemy count per map and confirm each enemy has a distinct role before adding more. [genre-mechanics]
- [ ] Plan at least one additional area beyond the starting zone so the map does not exhaust once players have seen it. [genre-mechanics]
- [ ] For obbies, build a sequence of challenges that increase in difficulty and consider one cooperative mechanic, such as a shared-momentum zone. [genre-mechanics]
- [ ] Treat swing feel as a core obby mechanic to tune, not a cosmetic detail. [genre-mechanics]
- [ ] Make sure the game is good before promoting [influencer-and-creator-marketing]
- [ ] List big creators whose videos already feature your game and DM them [influencer-and-creator-marketing]
- [ ] Use tracked links for every creator [influencer-and-creator-marketing]
- [ ] Let creators choose giveaway prizes [influencer-and-creator-marketing]
- [ ] Measure organic uplift before/during/after campaigns [influencer-and-creator-marketing]
- [ ] Evaluate on day-365 value, not just day 30 [influencer-and-creator-marketing]
- [ ] Keep creators with slow starts [influencer-and-creator-marketing]
- [ ] Keep your Talent Hub profile complete [influencer-and-creator-marketing]
- [ ] Before signing a creator activation, request in writing the rights to reuse cutdowns and short-form clips in your own paid UA. [influencer-and-creator-marketing]
- [ ] Show creator-themed in-game content (art, sales, calendars, daily tasks) only to users who came through that creator's tracked link. [influencer-and-creator-marketing]
- [ ] Limit your own dev channel to 1-3 games and post 3-4 videos per week (or daily Shorts plus one long video weekly). [influencer-and-creator-marketing]
- [ ] Evaluate your own channel's results only after 30-60 days of consistent posting. [influencer-and-creator-marketing]
- [ ] Add source text before inviting translators [localization-and-international]
- [ ] Manually add rare text; set AutoLocalize=false on usernames [localization-and-international]
- [ ] Use Context/Example/Key via CSV where needed [localization-and-international]
- [ ] Reserve space for longer text (Spanish +30%, German more) [localization-and-international]
- [ ] Rewrite dynamic strings with parameters (num/fixed/translate) [localization-and-international]
- [ ] Wrap async translator calls in pcall with fallbacks [localization-and-international]
- [ ] Reapply rich text formatting per locale [localization-and-international]
- [ ] Hire or vet native translators; use contracts [localization-and-international]
- [ ] Enable Use Translated Content [localization-and-international]
- [ ] Check whether top-tier items are grind-gated. If they are and you target East Asian markets, evaluate a day-one spend path for those regions. [localization-and-international]
- [ ] Monitor churn of high-value players separately by region after adding regional spend paths, and compare against the unchanged baseline. [localization-and-international]
- [ ] Annotate every function's parameters and return types, and annotate Instance-typed variables for autofill. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Avoid any; if you must cast, cast once in a helper, not at every call site. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] After a nil guard, create a new local cast to the non-optional type (official 2026 note). [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Use typeof() for concrete types and IsA() to downcast Instances; type external input as unknown. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Use rawget/rawset/rawequal inside metamethods that could re-enter themselves. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Have arithmetic metamethods return new tables and call setmetatable on every result. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Use weak tables (__mode) for caches; don't rely on GC timing or __gc. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Use table.freeze for constants and deep-freeze nested config recursively. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Remember arrays are 1-based; use #array, table.insert and table.remove (mind the shifting). [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Use tonumber() before comparing numeric strings and check nil on the result. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Use backtick interpolation or string.format for building strings; add spaces explicitly with .. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Escape magic pattern characters with % and anchor with ^ and $ when matching whole strings. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Use int64 for Roblox IDs and check integers with math.floor(x) == x before passing to integer APIs. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Use Random.new():NextInteger(1, #array) for random array picks. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Remember 0 and empty strings are truthy; check them explicitly. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Use camelCase for locals and functions, PascalCase for classes and services, LOUD_SNAKE_CASE for constants. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Use variadic ... only at the outer level of a function; pack into a table before nested closures. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Replace legacy wait/spawn/delay with task.wait, task.spawn, task.defer or task.delay; choose spawn for immediate, defer for next-in-frame. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] When timing matters, capture task.wait's return value rather than assuming the requested duration. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] For coroutines, check coroutine.isyieldable() before yielding and use coroutine.status() and coroutine.close() for control. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Put shared methods on the class table and set Class.__index = Class; chain subclasses via setmetatable(Subclass, {__index = Base}). [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Define __eq and __tostring on value-like metatables. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Annotate class inheritance with intersections, wrap parent constructors, and set the child metatable after the child type. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Initialise required table fields; reserve optional '?' for fields where nil is valid. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Type callback parameters to the specific class (OnTouched, BasePart), not generic Instance. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Pass optional arguments in one info table. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Use Promise.all for independent async work and promise:cancel with onCancel when UI or abilities end. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Multiply motion by deltaTime; use TweenService:GetValue for custom per-frame easing. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Set instance properties before parenting to workspace. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Reassign RaycastParams.FilterDescendantsInstances after editing the filter table. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Use table.concat, table.pack/unpack and table.clear for array operations. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Use 'x or default' for nil fallbacks and remember 0 and '' are truthy. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Use guard clauses to flatten nested ifs and delete unused variables and redundant checks. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Use consistent dictionary key types; use Instances as keys with brackets. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Define an exported type for player data and cast the data template to it. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Define an exported type for each registry or data entry and cast the entries to it. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] For atom or state tables, declare an explicit type (for example item ID string, quantity number) and cast to it. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Mark bindings that never change with const; add table.freeze when their table contents must also be locked. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Write large literals with underscore separators, e.g. 100_000_000. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Replace cond and a or b with an if-expression or explicit if when a can be false or nil. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Prefix module-internal variables with _ and expose them through functions. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Add a doc comment above functions with annotated parameters so hover shows type and purpose. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Write early-return guards as full multi-line if blocks with an indented body. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] When yielding for a frame, pick RenderStepped:Wait() for early-frame work and Heartbeat:Wait() for work that can wait until later in the frame (T18515). [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Check every conditional uses == (or ~=) rather than = when comparing values. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] When comparing Instances, compare the object reference; when comparing by name, compare the Name string. [luau-language-and-types]
- [ ] Wrap every TeleportAsync in pcall; retry up to 5 times with about 1 second delay on Failure. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] On Enum.TeleportResult.Flooded wait about 15 seconds before retrying. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Handle TeleportInitFailed separately for teleports that fail after the call succeeded. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Set the callback in both the casual and ranked places when routing by trust level. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Split the game into Lobby, Queue and Gameplay places and fill the _places table with each place ID. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Keep the _places table identical in every place and paste it into Gameplay and Queue files before publishing. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Start queue countdowns only after the minimum player count is reached (template: 3:00). [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Keep weights non-negative; use 0 to exclude a signal; tune by ratio. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Confirm the Current Configuration for each place; no config means default matchmaking. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Set Permissions to Public in Experience Settings, since new games are private by default. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Use manual queue requests rather than auto-queuing players. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Use Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter when you have a character and need its Player. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Check a player is not already in the queue folder before adding them, and cap the queue at the player limit. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Remove queued players on PlayerRemoving. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Cancel the countdown coroutine and reset timer UI when the last player leaves. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Clear queue entries and reset billboard counters after each teleport. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Duplicate queue room folders and rename them to the player count; do not edit the Q folder. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Create a separate public destination place, publish it, and paste its place ID into the teleport field. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Use same-place CFrame moves for travel inside one place; reserve TeleportService for big builds or matchmaking. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Change signal weights a few at a time and prioritise only a few signals. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Allow players to create a joinable room with chosen dungeon and difficulty; avoid making invites the only way to start (T16396). [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Add level-bracketed lobbies (e.g. 1+, 30+, 60+) to find similar-skill teammates (T16646, N6658). [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Keep a re-entry cooldown after a completed round if players must wait before rejoining (N6511, low-advice). [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Wrap each TeleportAsync in pcall with a small attempt limit and short delays. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Connect TeleportService.TeleportInitFailed and retry using the original TeleportOptions it provides. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] On Flooded results, back off longer before retrying. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Review lobby join conditions (level, host-only checks) and make them visible to players so newcomers are not silently blocked. [matchmaking-and-teleport]
- [ ] Set Model.LevelOfDetail to SLIM on static models; avoid runtime changes to them. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Set CollisionFidelity to Box on small or non-interactive parts; Hull for convex; avoid PreciseConvexDecomposition. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Anchor every part that does not move, including static NPCs. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Disable CastShadow on tiny, hidden or in-shadow items; limit light count and range. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Delete hidden parts, terrain under surfaces, duplicates and stray parts; hollow terrain with Subtract. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Merge transparent parts; avoid stacked transparent layers and decals on transparent objects. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Reuse MeshIds; avoid SpecialMesh and most UnionOperations; combine static meshes late in development. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Shrink textures 1–2x where invisible; leave MetalnessMap empty on non-metallic surfaces. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Capture MicroProfiler with Ctrl+F6 (Cmd+F6) during play, then pause with Ctrl+P on the spike before analysing. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Check draw calls under about 1,000 and triangles under about 1M on the baseline device. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Replace Neon on large part-count builds with SmoothPlastic. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Turn Haze off and avoid dynamic clouds. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Set PointLight shadows only where they add meaningfully. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Clear VideoFrame.Video when out of view and ensure no more than two videos play at once. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Set RollOffMaxDistance on positional sounds; disable AcousticSimulationEnabled unless realistic audio is needed. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Keep UIShadows near 100 on screen and set SurfaceGui MaxDistance deliberately. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Measure network with the Network Stats overlay (Shift+F3, then Shift+1); multiply Studio network numbers by player count. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Check sent traffic stays well under 50 KB/s and received traffic stays well below about 70 KB/s in the network stats. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Replace per-wheel raycasts with one center raycast and a line orientation constraint for vehicles. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Set fractional transparency values to exactly 0 or 1 on large maps. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Prefer shadowed SpotLights over shadowed PointLights where the design allows. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Check the place file against the 100 MB size limit. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Use object pools for frequently spawned, recycled models, and measure the gain. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Check the unparented instances view for models or parts that keep growing over time. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Remove unused sound instances outside runtime. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Keep base meshes low-poly and bake fine detail into normal maps. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Keep mesh imports under the 20,000-polygon limit; aim near 5,000 polygons for boat-scale props. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Re-check part-size assumptions against the October 2026 reduced limits. [optimization-and-streaming]
- [ ] Before writing physics code, check for Body* movers (BodyPosition, BodyGyro, BodyVelocity, BodyForce, BodyThrust, RocketPropulsion, BodyAngularVelocity) and replace them with the official equivalents (T7837-T7842). [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Set MaxForce/MaxTorque high enough for LinearVelocity, AngularVelocity and Motor drives; too low causes slow acceleration or never reaching the target (T9684, T7784, N3837). [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Anchor the stationary base for any spinning HingeConstraint and set ActuatorType to Motor or Servo (T7785, T8177). [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Set MotorMaxTorque to inf for player-loaded vehicles and use one motor per rotating assembly (T8179, T8180). [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Point both attachments' Axis in the same direction; verify with View > Show Constraint Details (T8519, T7892, T7787). [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Anchor the door frame, not the door; use yellow axis for pivot, orange for limits, and LowerAngle -90 / UpperAngle 90 (T10015-T10018, N3949). [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Set network ownership to the driver on Occupant change and reset with SetNetworkOwnershipAuto when vacated (T7776). [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Use RigidConstraint, not WeldConstraint, for attachments and bones (T8129). [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Keep NPC AI simple; remove Humanoid from non-moving NPCs and use constraints or CFrame plus BillboardGui health bars (T5175, T5843). [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Wait until the humanoid is grounded before ComputeAsync (T8950). [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Use finite costs (100 or 1000) for path materials; use PathfindingModifier for region avoidance (T8951, T8952). [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Verify indie jump formulas against current Humanoid docs before using them (T5011). [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Recompute a path when the target or obstacles move, and reuse paths for similar start and end points. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Check path.Status on every compute and handle anything other than Success. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Set humanoid.Jump = true when the next waypoint's Action is Jump, before calling MoveTo. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Advance waypoints on MoveToFinished with an 8-second reach timeout; do not set WaypointSpacing to math.huge. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Gate chase with a range check plus a raycast line-of-sight check before switching from patrol. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Pick patrol points at random from a Waypoints folder and pause about 2 seconds at each. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Set wheel density 2, elasticity 0.2, friction 1.5 as a starting point. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
