- [ ] Wrap MotorMaxTorque in math.abs and cap top speed as maxSpeed / wheel radius times sign(throttle). [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] For raycast suspension, cast only to rest length plus wheel radius and exclude the car model. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Reset each suspension VectorForce to zero when there is no ground hit. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Use physics hinge doors when players must ride them; use tweens otherwise. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] For closet doors that fling players, disable collisions and add an invisible barrier. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Set ServoMaxForce high enough to hold the platform (example 10000) and Restitution 0 on prismatic limits. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] For ragdolls, set joints IsKinematic=false and disable AnimationConstraints and CharacterBreakJointsOnDeath. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Avoid creating or destroying joints at runtime; use Debris for spawned objects. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Lower the direct-move switch timer (example 1 s) if chase feels sluggish. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Build PathfindingLinks with two attachments, Bidirectional on unless one-way, and a Label. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Assign PathfindingLinkReached without parentheses and return true at the end. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Use physics parts, not Humanoids, for physics-driven enemies. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Use one IK control per leg with EndEffector, Target and ChainRoot set. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Add a pole attachment per IK leg on HumanoidRootPart, oriented where the knee should bend. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Add AlignOrientation with a large MaxTorque on HumanoidRootPart for terrain tilt. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Use native Springs on constraints for suspension rather than custom spring math. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Keep teleports within about 1,000,000 studs. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Start turret turn-rate caps near 45 degrees per second and raise toward 90 only if tracking feels too sluggish (N7607). [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] For water detection, create an invisible, anchored, CanCollide-off sphere over the water volume and listen for touches on it. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Before using the sphere-sensor workaround, check current Roblox Terrain APIs for water detection, since the source is dated 2025-01. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Check that the shared NPC module stays within the existing NPC throttling and NPC count caps before scaling up the number of characters. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] For knockback, apply a one-off impulse rather than a persistent mover. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Remove any temporary force or push object about 2 seconds after it is applied. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Retune force magnitudes from the legacy BodyForce values before using VectorForce or impulses. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Use a distance trigger to start an enemy's lock-on and fire, then return it to its post. [physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]
- [ ] Check every player action (hit, pickup, checkpoint, level-up, objective) produces a visible or audible reaction. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Pair each important visual cue with a sound cue, and each audio cue with a visual, for accessibility. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Add a short camera move on attacks; add a cinematic shot for cutscenes. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Tune recoil with a spring module: shove, decay, random sideways offset, screen shake. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Use a brief contact hold (~3 frames) and a heavy recovery for combat impact. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Set pickup disabled state explicitly (transparency 0.4 enabled to 0.9 disabled; dim PointLight). [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Pick opening music that sets the intended mood on join. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Use badges and milestones for replayability. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Keep anti-cheat unobtrusive so legit players aren't pestered. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Use rare-roll cutscenes for top rarities if you have a rarity system. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Use a world-reaction hook (e.g., ships sail away) to make areas memorable. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Drive FOV from clamped normalised velocity and lerp it; drive bob from walk state, not idle. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Tween HUD bars about 0.5 s with Exponential Out; keep one-shot health and score audio immediate. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Delay visual state changes about 0.1 s so they land with the tween. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Add a short UI sound to menu open and close. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Play a chime on collectable touch and a particle effect when a task completes. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Show distinct victory and defeat text plus audio on round end. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Add HapticEffect for collisions, explosions and UI clicks, and check device support. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Tint the screen with ColorCorrectionEffect for low health or rising fear. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Smooth position only when the simulated object diverges far from the rendered one. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Animate ambient life such as swaying trees, leaves, birds, NPCs and day/night. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Apply leaver penalties no harsher than losing the fight. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Show upcoming content and unresolved mysteries to create reasons to return. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Publish drop odds and keep them fixed; do not shift odds by outcome. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Apply luck boosts as multipliers below 1 (0.1 = 10x). [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Use a 0.1 s perfect-block window that stuns the attacker on success. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Randomise enemy wandering with occasional sudden attacks. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] For horror, keep scenes lit, limit jumpscares, add safe rooms, and tell backstory through the environment. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Add a readable windup and a punish window to every heavy enemy and boss attack. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Give every chasing enemy at least one escape tool (vent, door, freeze item or NPC help). [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Replace any VR quick-time prompt with a physical gesture. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Write or commission music for each cutscene and chapter, not just the join screen. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Make early core actions fun without forced waits for crops, seeds or similar. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Set respawn timers to a few seconds for shooters and no more than about 60 seconds for MOBAs. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Cap death losses to a small gold penalty and avoid long walk-backs on top. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] If the game has idle or offline income, confirm that absence causes no reset, decay or penalty. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] If the game has gear tiers or rare catches, make sure tiers change outcomes and rare catches can be traded or shown off. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Measure input-to-visual hit latency and confirm each hit shows an immediate reaction [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Pair each instant hit with a visible world reaction where the design allows it [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] For any stalker or chaser enemy, add an audio or visual cue and keep it physically present in the scene so its position stays readable. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Check the sequence of threats in each area and insert a calm gap or safe room between encounters so the player can recover. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Add hover and click sounds to all buttons, and connect runtime-created buttons with DescendantAdded. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Place ambient sound and light cues to guide players toward the next objective. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Publish clear rules on what each paid item does and does not change. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] During periods of external stress, consider adding calm themed content or areas. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Show win streaks and leaderboard positions clearly in the UI so losing them is visible. [player-psychology-and-game-feel]
- [ ] Check 10-foot readability for console [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Track per-level progress and failure rates [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Share the prototype more widely only after several sessions confirm the findings [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Use a 50/50 split of one variant against control for large rollouts [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] For long-term questions, run a diary study (about 40-50 players over 3-4 months) or a smaller on-site plus home-play study. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Pair telemetry counts with a small interview sample to explain why behaviour happens. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Check the map is not too empty for players to find each other quickly. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Use a handout with observations and one recommendation, or 2 Stars and a Wish. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Check that tuning changes fit the design (e.g., vehicle speed feels exciting in a race) after each change. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Run through lobby, intermission and map before calling the build done. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] After a friend plays, ask how hard it was to learn, their favourite part, and whether they would play again. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Use the two Stars and one Wish format, then implement one change from it. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Re-check each feature after about a week and cut it if it is more trouble than fun. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Convert each public criticism into named tasks before reacting in public. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Check that attack telegraphs and boss cues are readable from the actual camera view. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Configure NPCs to fill dropped player slots at matching skill level so matches continue. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Run a countdown-lobby session with many real players and note every point where the game breaks. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Ask a friend who has not died in early play whether the game is too easy. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Set elimination cutoffs for each judging round before scoring starts. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Measure full completion time on a brand-new account and compare it to your pacing target. [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Before tuning other systems, play a full run until the core fail state triggers, and note how long it takes and whether the pacing feels fair (T19015). [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] If the fail state arrives too fast or too slow in a real run, adjust drain or spawn values and replay before changing more values (T19015). [playtesting-and-feedback]
- [ ] Add daily quests/challenges that reset on join and clear quickly [retention-d1-d7]
- [ ] Add offline progress or offline earnings [retention-d1-d7]
- [ ] Add collection index, base or pets for sunk investment [retention-d1-d7]
- [ ] Prompt favorites (2-5 min delay) and notification opt-in [retention-d1-d7]
- [ ] Respect the flood delay (example: 15 s) so repeated teleport attempts don't get throttled and strand returning players. [retention-d1-d7]
- [ ] Verify current SafeTeleport retry and delay values in the official docs before hard-coding them. [retention-d1-d7]
- [ ] Verify a lobby or restart path exists in every game mode, not just the main mode. [retention-d1-d7]
- [ ] Time a typical run from start to finish or to party wipe, and shorten it if it exceeds one sitting. [retention-d1-d7]
- [ ] Check how far players actually progress on your daily ladder before adding more days. [retention-d1-d7]

- [ ] Replace HapticService with HapticEffect [roblox-platform-changes]
- [ ] Migrate Avatar API V1-V3 calls to V4 before Dec 11, 2026 [roblox-platform-changes]
- [ ] Verify whether Phase 2/3 dates (Jul 21, Sep 15, 2026) have happened [roblox-platform-changes]
- [ ] Use ListUserRestrictions for place-level bans [roblox-platform-changes]
- [ ] Name leaderstats folder exactly [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Check in-camera triangles with Ctrl/Cmd+Shift+F2 (~500k) [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Keep draw calls ~500 [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Profile with Microprofiler Ctrl/Cmd+F6 [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Check recv stat Ctrl/Cmd+F7 [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Avoid RenderStepped unless needed [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Audit lights with woot3 plugin [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Reuse MeshId and material [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Return useEffect cleanup and pass dependencies [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Attach version/device metadata to alerts [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Wrap slow or independent async calls in task.spawn with pcall. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Scale UI stroke thickness by viewport height using the 1080p baseline formula. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Format numbers only in the display module, never before sending. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Gate experiment GetValue calls behind the feature check. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Check GetBadgeInfoAsync IsEnabled before AwardBadgeAsync; use UserHasBadgeAsync at PlayerAdded. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Avoid custom cameras in VR unless essential; merge VR camera fixes when you must. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Fix leaks, and check startup allocations are parented or destroyed. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Use viewer breadcrumbs and Studio Explorer to trace bloated nodes. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Set Workspace.SignalBehavior to Deferred explicitly, and yield before reading values that callbacks set. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Put heavy per-frame compute under Actors; call task.desynchronize() and task.synchronize() before writes. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Split parallel work across several Actors; never call require inside desynchronized code. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Move models with Model:PivotTo(); replace SetPrimaryPartCFrame calls. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Tween the root CFrame (TweenService directly), not Position or CFrameValue proxies. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Use Box or Hull CollisionFidelity for small or non-interactive meshes; set CanTouch false where unused. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Anchor only the assembly's root part; set AlignPosition MaxVelocity to a finite value. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Nil-check every Workspace:Raycast result; set CanQuery false on parts that should be ignored. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Destroy projectiles past a max distance and clean spent bullets with Debris:AddItem. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Profile before and after each micro-optimisation; keep only changes that show gains. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Start concurrent HTTP calls with task.spawn; honour 429 retry-after headers. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Use AudioPlayer, and trigger effects from animation markers rather than timed waits. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Pack Vector3, CFrame and Color3 into buffers; use U8 and static types where values are bounded. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Replicate per-player content only to the player who needs it. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Use physics for many moving parts; check Touched behaviour against CFrame moves. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Use OverlapParams with exclusions for ability hits; track hit targets per cast. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Migrate RaycastParams to ExcludeInstances/IncludeInstances in new code. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Scope CollectionService to a small root and avoid >, >>, has() and in-radius() in queries. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Wrap ComputeAsync in pcall with a retry cap and check path.Status before reading waypoints. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Use Debris to destroy forces, casts and temporary instances; disconnect connections on end. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Use the AnimationGraph's SetParameter for complex blends; keep LoadAnimation where it works. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Migrate Motor6D code to Attachments or AnimationConstraints before the 2026 changes break it. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Keep distant props and off-screen effects from animating each frame; gate by a configurable range. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Check Joint.Active on joints from GetJoints and delete inactive redundant welds. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Replace FindPartsInRegion3 with workspace:GetPartBoundsInBox. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Use OverlapParams with FilterType Include and the character as the filter descendant. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Benchmark zone libraries at your real entity and zone counts before choosing one. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Use WaitForChild(name, seconds) with an explicit timeout for expected objects. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Convert RGB 0-255 to 0-1 for Color3 in ColorSequences; keep NumberSequence sizes at 10 or below. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Provide a fallback when using Lighting Technology Future. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Set material path costs and add custom waypoints where default stairs paths fail. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Build doors with PathfindingLink and return true from PathfindingLinkReached. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] End enemy attack hitboxes when the animation ends. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Disconnect animator and animation-track signals before destroying a character or its Animator. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Call Player.CharacterAdded:Wait() before reading the character. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Use character:GetPivot() for the character CFrame. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Multiply per-frame rotation and zoom steps by delta time. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Use plain CFrames by default; use quaternions only to combine or blend rotations. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Tune zoom start, end, step, and offset values per game; check for end-of-zoom jolt. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Use TeleportAsync inside pcall; never the deprecated teleport methods. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Add an if-return base case to every recursive function. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Add a MAX_DEPTH counter to recursive generation over branching structures. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Snap with // and a single GRID_SIZE constant. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Shrink placement overlap boxes about 0.2 studs per axis. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Use OverlapParams Include lists, not Whitelist or Blacklist. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Create leaderstats as a Folder with the exact name 'leaderstats' under each Player. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Use alpha = 1 - math.pow(smoothness, dt) for frame-independent smoothing. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Clamp UI progress ratios with math.clamp(n, 0, 1). [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Call task.desynchronize inside parallel code before doing the heavy work. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Use Ctrl+click Go To Declaration on functions; do not expect it to work on require() lines. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Write require paths as literal full paths, not paths built through intermediate variables. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Use game:GetService for every service call, consistently. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Confirm no built-in service or leaderstats name was renamed when code fails to find it. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] In each Touched handler, resolve the player with GetPlayerFromCharacter(hit.Parent) and return early if nil. [scripting-and-performance]
- [ ] Before LoadAnimation, look for an existing track by name in GetPlayingAnimationTracks(). [scripting-and-performance]

- [ ] Wire 2D sounds: AudioPlayer to AudioDeviceOutput under SoundService. [sound-and-music]

- [ ] Add a constant 2D ambient bed so the game is never silent. [sound-and-music]

- [ ] Add per-category volume sliders (ambience, music, weapons, footsteps, enemies) to settings, not only a master volume. [sound-and-music]
- [ ] Add per-trigger mute toggles so sensitive players can fully silence specific sounds. [sound-and-music]
- [ ] Give similar objects distinct sounds so players can identify them by audio. [sound-and-music]

- [ ] Run the Audio Discovery plugin to review ownership of all audio before publishing. [sound-and-music]
- [ ] Raise RollOffMaxDistance when using Inverse or Linear rolloff. [sound-and-music]
- [ ] Use DistanceAttenuation for gradual falloff rather than a hard cutoff. [sound-and-music]

- [ ] Set SoundService.DefaultListenerLocation to Character when you want automatic listeners. [sound-and-music]
- [ ] Use 3D audio for situational objective cues. [sound-and-music]
- [ ] Use Echo for caves and walls; use Reverb for large interiors. [sound-and-music]
- [ ] Keep a quiet ambient base running during play so the game never feels silent. [sound-and-music]
- [ ] Add a combat stinger when a fight starts. [sound-and-music]
- [ ] Trigger a music cue spike when the player picks up a key to build tension. [sound-and-music]

- [ ] View at phone size; confirm main subject is clear in 1 second [thumbnails-icons]

- [ ] Use qptr.io to preview [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Keep a reference screenshot for AI generation [thumbnails-icons]

- [ ] Give buttons a coloured container and a highlight or shadow. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Make the face large and expressive with big eyes filling the frame; keep text to 1–2 words or none. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Check subject-to-background contrast with a blue or green backdrop and a vivid subject, at phone size. [thumbnails-icons]

- [ ] Keep any text bold and minimal; do not let effects crowd the focal point. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Confirm AI-generated art is allowed under Roblox guidelines and does not copy another game's art. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Use the highest-resolution textures for exported hats and models. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Face the subject toward the sun and use a bright neon-green or grass baseplate so the model is fully lit. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Turn off View Selector before taking Studio captures. [thumbnails-icons]
- [ ] Pose the character expressively; avoid stiff, bland stances. [thumbnails-icons]

- [ ] Call PlayerOwnsAsset before saving outfits; request PromptAllowInventoryReadAccess once per session before GetInventory. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] Name eyebrow and eyelash accessories EyebrowAccessory, EyelashAccessory, or HairAccessory and bundle them with a body or makeup asset. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] Do not count avatar item sales toward the 18+ rate. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] If building an in-game avatar customizer, use AvatarEditorService as the base. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] Before publishing an emote, select Avatar Item > Emote and wait until the animation shows 'valid'. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] Keep emote length at 10 seconds or less. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] Keep every body part within 5 studs of the root part and avoid very fast motion. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] Expect marketplace review to take over 5 hours and plan the launch around it. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] Track sales per item and keep releasing new items on a schedule. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] When reworking a cosmetic design, decide whether the old version can be sold as a skin before retiring it. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] When repurposing clips as Shorts, trim them to about 10-20 seconds. [ugc-and-avatar-economy]
- [ ] Build all sizes and positions in Scale; add Offset only for pixel-exact needs. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Cap pixel sizes with UISizeConstraint (e.g. MaxSize inf,44) and set MinSize 0,40 for frames that must stay legible. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Set AnchorPoint 0.5,0.5 on backgrounds and place elements via the Properties window. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Keep ScreenGuis with interactive elements on CoreUISafeInsets; use ScreenInsets None only for backgrounds. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Let UIListLayout or UIGridLayout place items; do not set Position or Size on their siblings. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Use RelativeXX inside vertical ScrollingFrames and AutomaticCanvasSize for scroll containers. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Keep UITextSizeConstraint MinTextSize at 9 or higher. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Verify tap targets are at least 9x9 mm on a real phone, not only in the emulator. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Check layouts on phone, tablet, laptop, PS5/console and TV presets with Device Emulator and ViewportDisplaySize. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Verify the on-screen keyboard does not hide controls when a TextBox is focused. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Confirm every control is reachable with the four directions and has a visible focus state. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Pair every toggle menu with an explicit close button and a back button for overlays. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Use 0-duration TweenInfo for instant hides, cancel tweens before new states and reuse tweens. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Avoid tweening UIStroke.Thickness on text; tween transparency or use a UIStroke child. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Add a Hover GuiState transition to TextButtons and keep hover tweens short (about 0.2 to 0.3 s). [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Define tokens in one StyleSheet and keep the BaseStyleSheet and StyleDerive intact. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Check PreferredInput (not TouchEnabled) at startup and reconnect the PreferredInput change signal to swap layouts. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Keep custom UI about 60px in from each screen side and out of the bottom-left and bottom-right corners. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Set ScreenGui.ScreenInsets to DeviceSafeInsets for HUD elements that should sit at the edge. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Set ResetOnSpawn deliberately per ScreenGui: true for health-style HUD, false for menus and persistent progress. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Clone a hidden template per row or item and sort with LayoutOrder. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Set a fixed-size, scale-based corner radius on frames and buttons and set CornerRadius before per-corner overrides. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Add UIShadow or a subtle semi-transparent stroke for depth; avoid heavy borders. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Add toggles for camera shake, bobbing, particles, animation and brightness. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Add a reduced-motion branch using the @ReducedMotionEnabledTrue style query. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Set CanQuery true on parts that carry interactive SurfaceGuis. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Set ElasticBehavior to Never on touch scrolling frames where overscroll is unwanted. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Pass true as the third BindAction argument for actions needing a touch button. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Keep reserved keys (Esc, F9, F11, F12, PrintScreen) out of rebinds. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Use Activated for all cross-platform button logic and SecondaryActivated for secondary actions. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Use Scale-only sizing for frames and text, and set TextScaled for labels and buttons. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Set ResetOnSpawn to false on any ScreenGui whose state must survive respawn. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Build menu category tabs as separate frames and toggle Visible so only one list shows. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Show where vehicles, mounts or key items are kept, with a visible pickup prompt. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Show crafting materials in the inventory. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Warn on low durability or ammo with HUD and sound cues. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Show a Coming Soon sign for unfinished features. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Give every NPC a useful role. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Add a background-music mute toggle. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Provide a touch-friendly action to recover lost items. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Attach reference screenshots to every UI item in the design doc. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Smooth long-text scrolling with a smoothing module or equivalent. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Build one configurable button component with variant props. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Preview components in a story harness before wiring them into the game. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Re-enable missing Studio panels via right-click on the top bar if the layout is lost. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] If players sell multiple items, add a sell-all option and an inventory selection menu so bulk selling is one step. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Wire the sprint input through the existing rebind rules; do not hardcode the key or reserve default inputs. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Consider a UI pack as a starting point for consistent components when prompting AI. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Reset the flyout target position before each tween so window resizing doesn't break it. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Shrink the flyout clone to size zero on arrival and pop the HUD counter. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Hide the dialogue box by default and toggle Enabled from the dialogue function. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Do not play click sounds when a TextBox is clicked (check IsA('TextBox')). [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Set ProximityPrompt HoldDuration to about 1 s and activation distance to about 20 studs. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Show exact numbers next to resource bars such as fuel. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] State buff and status-effect values numerically (e.g. heals X% of max HP). [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] On touch-only devices, set ScrollBarThickness to about 2% of parent AbsoluteSize.X. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] For a minimal visual system, pick one heavy sans-serif display font for headings (the source uses Montserrat ExtraBold) and keep body text in one family. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Limit a new screen to text buttons, text labels and frames before adding extra element types. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Add a low-graphics option (shadows off) and a music volume slider to settings. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Give quest buttons their own animation rather than a generic shared one. [ui-ux-design]
- [ ] Ship a small themed drop every 1-2 weeks (monthly minimum) [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Start teasing a few months out; post renders/clips 3-7 days before launch [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Add end-game systems after day 30 and refresh dailies [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Buff rather than nerf; ease difficulty over time [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Sprint Monday and ship Friday if running a weekly rhythm, as one indie team does. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Schedule at least one week of rest between seasons. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Plan seasons around one-month lengths as a starting point. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Set notification strings within 99 characters. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Add missions with each new feature; consider double XP in the final week of a season. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Use category repeatDelaySeconds for recurring dailies and weeklies. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Poll Open Cloud async operations until done is true before reading results. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Plan for Roblox review of preset content and be ready to change or remove presets. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Add each fix to a public changelog after it ships. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Start audio and third-party dependency review early, since queues and approvals can run long. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Set startUtc and endUtc for each season before launch. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Count shutdowns per month and cut any that are avoidable, since each one risks losing players. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Stack a small fix or revamp and a larger content drop on consecutive days to show steady activity. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Audit underused areas for rework before committing to a new region. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] When adding a new map, place a countable set of collectibles and link them to a seasonal quest with a visible counter. [updates-live-ops]
- [ ] Install Rokit and declare Rojo, Wally and Lune versions in rokit.toml. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Commit only after sync and MCP are verified. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Publish before major terrain edits; wait about 15 seconds after toggling Expanded Terrain in Team Create. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Create Open Cloud keys with only the permissions the endpoints need. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Use list_roblox_studios to target the right place when multiple Studios are open. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Open the synced project in VS Code beside Studio and commit with GitHub Desktop. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Map $path into a dedicated child folder, never directly into a service. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Create a feature branch per feature, rebase main in often, then merge into main. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Run StyLua --check and Selene in CI before allowing merges. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Keep pack.rbxm in .gitignore and build it in CI. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Bump the wally.toml version before pushing to main. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Confirm a library is still maintained before adopting it. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Persist shell environment variables in .zshrc or .bashrc. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Split parallel AI work by file and assign one integrator. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Push the repo to GitHub as origin for backup and team access. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Install Rojo and Wally via Rokit, then run rojo init and wally init in a new project folder. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] After a Wally install, restart rojo serve and reconnect Studio. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Optional: run the editor and rojo serve in separate tmux panes to keep context. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Optional: install GitLens for per-commit reverts in VS Code. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Log repeated forum requests for tooling gaps and track their upvote counts before deciding to build a plugin. [version-control-and-tooling]
- [ ] Pick Beams for hazards and laser or lava lines, and ParticleEmitters for explosions, smoke and sparks. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] Set Lifetime in seconds, at most 20. Use about 0.5 s for flashes and about 2 s for lingering effects. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] Give every Lifetime, Rotation and Speed a min/max range to reduce repetition. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] Animate Transparency with a NumberSequence ending at 1 so particles fade out. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] Use a grayscale PNG with transparent edges and tint it with Color. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] Set LightEmission 1 and LightInfluence 0 for glowing particles, and use 0.4 to 0.5 for faint glow. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] Add PointLights in invisible parts for light; do not rely on particle brightness. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] Enable Bloom with threshold about 0.85 for glowing beams. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] Attach each Beam to two different Attachments, and use CurveSize0/1 for arcs. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] Set Width0/1 for taper (negatives clamp to 0) and TextureMode for tiling. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] Use an invisible anchored part with Touched for beam hit detection, transparency 1. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] Keep Sphere and Cylinder emitter shapes on BaseParts, not Attachments. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] Use SpreadAngle 360 for all-direction bursts and 180,180 for spheres around a part. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] Use Acceleration for continuous force and Speed for initial velocity. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] Layer two emitters with different flipbook or size values to hide repetition. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] Sync bone-attached VFX each frame via PreSimulation and CollectionService. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] Render cosmetic effects in RenderStepped and be ready to undo mispredicted ones. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] Multiply per-frame projectile motion by dt. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] Verify old DevForum patterns (Emit, LockedToPart) against current Studio before copying them. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] Pair short Lifetime (0.2 to 0.6) with higher Speed (20 to 40) and Drag around 10 for explosions. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] Give every Trail two different Attachment objects. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] Set FaceCamera on Beams and Trails that disappear from some angles. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] Choose TextureMode per effect: Static for stamps and arrows, Wrap for flowing water. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] Hide helper parts with Transparency 1 rather than deleting them. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] Set ZOffset to about 2 on textures that sit on a surface. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] Check each effect's orientation and rotate on the correct axis. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] Set a PointLight Brightness and Range for real light from flares; particles and Neon do not light surroundings. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] Pair Roblox Clouds with an Atmosphere object. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] Tune Atmosphere Haze, Glare and ClockTime for sunset looks. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] Scale particle Size up when you raise Squash. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] Use Build with Assistant for starting particle values, then tune colours and NumberSequence curves by eye. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] Reuse one texture asset across several effects and vary the look with emitter settings. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] Add leaf particles near trees as a low-cost final detail. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] Use spherical point distributions for RPG spell effects; attach a trail to one point and move it through the sequence. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] Study ParticleEmitter-based VFX packs to learn how customizable effects are tuned. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] Use one shared TweenInfo for all menu tweens and tune easing style and direction for feel. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] Add polish with particle emitters and global lighting only after gameplay works. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] Expose adjustable timing values (reveal waits, TweenSpeed) as attributes or constants. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] For falling particles, set Acceleration Y negative (world axes). [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] When a stretched particle looks wrong on a large surface, build a small particle scaled up and compare. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] On Humanoid.Died, fade character parts with TweenService after about a 0.25 s delay. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] For glowing surfaces, try an emissive mask with emissive strength (about 0 to 40) and emissive tint. [vfx-and-particles]
- [ ] For organic beam wiggle, compute offset = math.noise(os.clock() * multiplier) each frame and set CurveSize0 = offset, CurveSize1 = -offset. [vfx-and-particles]
